Yudhistira said I know what you are saying but I do not want to go. You take Bhima and Arjuna with you and go to Hastinapura.  
Krishna said - Do not go against my words again and again. This does not suit you. If you become king, people will know about me. I will get benefited.  
Yudhistira agreed. Bhima was happy that Yudhistira would become king. Sons Madri and Arjuna were also thrilled.  
Dhritarashtra then started crying out for his sons, saying the Pandavas had taken all, leaving him alone. Hearing this Yudhistira fainted. Krishna and the other brothers wondered what had happened. They helped him up.  
Coming to his senses, Yudhistira said I am able to bear the cries of Dhritarashtra. There is no point in getting the kingdom.I have been the cause of the death of Bhishma who held my hand when I was growing up.  
Vyasa then came there said - Discard your grief. Where there is a union, there will be a discord. No one is immortal. Why do you grieve for that? The body is bound to get destroyed. By illness or by death. One dies because of their actions and not because of someone else. Some die as infants, some in their middle age and some in their old age. Some die of old age, some of illness and some due to weapons. Some are bitten by snakes and die, and some get attacked by wild beasts and meet their end. The rich king and the poor beggar, both meet their end when their time comes. You are a learned person and I should not be explaining this to you. One who stays in the right path has a peaceful end. Like the change of seasons, there will be changes in life. Grief will come and so will Joy.  
No will be immortal. If so, where are the great kings like Pandu, Madhata ,Dilipa and Harishchandra. If two sticks are together, friction will be there. That does not mean that you will not keep the sticks. Your mother, your wives, your brothers - all will die. Get rid of the grief. You will go back where you came from. The body will get destroyed. This is a cycle of life and death and all are prisoner in it.

Yudhistira kept quiet.

Arjuna said to Krishna - Will all get destroyed because Yudhistira is sad? We stooped low and did things that we had never done, in the kingdom of Virata. You please talk to Yudhistira. Create an illusion for him, so that he understand. Only You can do that.

Krishna went and sat near Yudhistira. Holding his hands, he said - Discard your grief. You will not get anything out of this. One who grieves constantly looses prosperity. Vyasa has said so many things to you. Why do you not respond. Do not fear that you have sinned by killing your dear ones. Go to Bhishma. Talk to him. he will tell you what to do. Respect Vyasa and go what he says. Your subjects are looking forward to you.

Hearing all this, Yudhistira agreed to go to Hastinapura. A chariot was brought. Bhima became the charioteer. Krishna and Arjuna climbed up along with Yudhistira. The sons of Madri followed. Dhritarashtra followed. Yuyutsu and Sanjaya followed in a chariot.

Gandhari reluctantly followed.

Hearing that Yudhistira is coming, the people of Hastinapura rushed to greet him.

Seeing an auspicious moment, Yudhistira stepped into the city. Gandhari asked the sages to look for a date when Yudhistira could be crowned.

Finally Hastinapura was with Yudhistira.

## Yudhistira does not want the throne

Tuesday, 21 March 2017

Janmajeya said - O sage You have said about the grief of all. Now please tell me about Yudhistira's coming to Hastinapura.  
Vaishampayana said - Listen, while I tell you the story of Mahabharata.  
Yudhistira was grief stricken. Krishna went to him and said The throne at Hastinapura lies vacant. Please go there and occupy the throne. The people are waiting for you, like the people of Ayodhya waited for Rama to come back from the forests.  
Yudhistira said I do not want to go there. I want to stay here in this ground, till I die. I am not able to stand the cries of the women. I have killed my dear ones.

## Last rites performed

Monday, 20 March 2017

At the words of Krishna, Dhritarashtra called Yudhistira and said - Many people have fallen in the battle. You need to arrange for their last rites. Yuyutsu, Dhaumya, Sanjaya and Vidura will be with you.  
Arrangements were made and the pyres were lit. The Pandavas then went to towards the river to immerse the ashes. Kunti then came there. She said Karna, whom you have known as a charioteer's son is my son, born to me before I married Pandu. He was born off the sun and I put him in a basket and floated him away in the river.  
Hearing all from Kunti, Yudhistira broke down. He said You have told me know that Karna was my brother, I have plotted to kill my brother. If you had told me earlier, I would not have killed Karna or Duryodhana. Karna would have become the king of Hastinapura and Duryodhana would have happy also. I have killed my elder brother. I am angry and said. I curse you O mother. From today, women will not be able to keep secrets.  
Saying this, the last rites were concluded. Krishna consoled Yudhistira and the Pandavas.  
All were crying for their departed dead.  
For three days and nights no one ate anything. After three days, fruits were got for all.  
Krishna went about consoling all.

## Gandhari curses Krishna

Sunday, 19 March 2017

Gandhari continued - Fire comes in my mouth when I think of how unfair you have been. Listen Krishna. I will curse you and then my grief will be lessened. The way my daughters in law, the same way your daughters in law will cry. You created a divide among the Pandavas and the Kauravas and similarly your clan will get divided.

Yudhistira started shaking hearing this. Krishna knew this was to happen. He went to Gandhari and said I accept your curse. If this pleases you and makes you calm, I accept this.

## Gandhari addresses Krishna

Saturday, 18 March 2017

Janmajeya said - Please tell me what Gandhari said. How did she control her grief? What did she say to Krishna? Why did she curse Krishna?  
The sage said -  
Listening to Krishna, the queen sat up. Then Gandhari said to Krishna - See Krishna, my hundred sons lie dead, slain by Bhima. You can hear their wives crying. The women who had never stepped out of the house, are out in the open, in the field. All sons are the same to a mother, wether they are good or bad. I am not able to stand this. If I could show you the weight I have in my heart, I would have shown it to you. Off all the griefs one has, the loss of a child is the gravest. Only one who has carried a child in her womb and feel this. How will you console me Krishna? Duryodhana, who was worshipped by his subjects in now being eaten by jackals. When he asked me who will win, I said where there is justice, where ever there is Krishna, Victory will be there. I know Duryodhana did not listen to any advise and thus met his end. Shakuni, my brother, through his cunning mind brought about this. The Pandavas will rule here after and no one is left to rule after that.  
Krishna and the Pandavas were quite.  
Draupadi was crying for her sons. Uttara, holding Abhimanyu's body was crying.  
Gandhari was crying away, calling out to Duryodhana. She then turned to Krishna and said - O son of Devaki. You are the cause of all this destruction. You could have stopped this war. If you had not stepped in and become the charioteer, things would have been opposite. You knew Abhimanyu would step into the formation as he knew how to get in and did not know how to get out. You used Ghatotkacha to save Arjuna from the powerful weapon of Karna.

## Gandhari goes to the battlefield

Friday, 17 March 2017

It was a horrible sight at the battlefield. Vultures were flying everywhere. Jackals and Dogs were tearing at the flesh of the dead.  
People looked around for the bodies of their departed ones. Some recognized the body parts, some saw the bodies and some could find the bodies at all.  
There were dead animals and chariots and many jewels were scattered there.  
After some time, Gandhari was able to spot Duryodhana. She swooned seeing her dead son. People thought she had died. Krishna, Satyaki and the Pandavas raised her and revived her.  
Seeing Krishna, Gandhari said See, there is Duryodhana. Where is Karna? Why is not here with him? Where is Shakuni? Where are Bhishma and Drona? He who had never lain on a hard bed, why does he lie on the bare ground? Get up son. Bhima and Arjuna are calling for a fight.  
Krishna said Do no grieve. Your sons have what they deserved. Go with the Pandavas now.

## Kunti sees her sons

Thursday, 16 March 2017

Gandhari then asked the Pandavas to greet their mother, Kunti. Kunti was overjoyed to see her sons, Krishna and Satyaki. She hugged her sons, her eyes full of tears.  
Krishna said Why are your eyes wet? Yudhistira will now become the king.

Draupadi came and bowed down to all.

Gandhari then decided to go into the field to see the dead bodies of her sons. All the ladies, Krishna and the Pandavas went behind her.

## Dhritarashtra hugs Iron Bhima

Wednesday, 15 March 2017

Sanjaya held the blind king and made him sit. The five Pandavas, Krishna and Satyaki sat in front of the king. This was all that was left of the Pandava camp.  
Dhritarashtra said I want to see Bhima and hug him. He has been responsible for the death of all my sons. Come to me.  
Saying this, he stretched his hands out.  
Krishna knew this would happen and had already created an iron statue of Bhima. He brought that in front of Dhritarashtra. The king pressed the statue hard. The king was very strong and the statue crushed with his embrace. The king was happy. He had killed Bhima. He then had tears in his eyes.  
Krishna then said Bhima is alive. I knew you would get enraged and this is the reason I had arranged for an iron statue of Bhima to be made. You will not get back Duryodhana by killing Bhima. You yourself said earlier that sons of Pandu are your sons. You yourself knew that this would happen. Your sons tried to kill the Pandavas on many occasions. Why do you want to be a sinner by killing Bhima?  
Dhritarashtra said You have saved Bhima.  
Saying this he stretched his hands and hugged and blessed the Pandavas.

The Pandavas then with Krishna where Gandhari was. Krishna knew that Gandhari would curse them. He said You yourself had told Duryodhana that the victory will be where there is justice. Your words have come true and justice has prevailed. Throw away your anger and accept the sons of Pandu as your own.

Gandhari said As your say Krishna, I will accept the Pandavas as my own but where do I throw my hurt? My hundred sons are dead.

## Dhritarashtra goes to Kurukshetra

Tuesday, 14 March 2017

Vyasa said - Do not grieve for the departed. Let me tell you something. Once I was in the land of Brahma. There the earth came and said she wanted some relief. Vishnu said the demons that he had killed had been born as Kshatriyas. Brahma said Duryodhana will be born and will torment many.  
Hearing this, the earth said how will be relieved.Brahma said Gods will born as sons of Pandu. Duryodhana will be born as son of Dhritarashtra. Vishnu as Krishna will be there to help the sons of Pandu when turmoil will start with who will gain the kingdom. Do not worry O Earth. You will be relieved off Duryodhana. This Duryodhana is your son. He had Karna as his friend and Shakuni as his adviser. Thus I tell you, do not grieve.

Sanjaya said Kings from many lands have come to pay their condolences.

Dhritarashtra asked Vidura to fetch the women. Vidura said Dhritarashtra is going to the battlefield where all your sons lie dead.

Hearing this, wails broke out. Vidura said Please control yourselves. The king wants you to come to the battlefield.  
The women climbed on the chariots and left for the ground. They were crying away, cursing their fate and crying to their departed ones.

At that point, Ashwatthama, Kripacharya and Kritavarma came and met Dhritarashtra. They told him that all his sons were dead and Bhima had killed all of them. To take revenge they had killed the brothers and sons of Draupadi.

Hearing that Dhritarashtra was coming, the Pandavas decided to greet him. Yudhistira asked Krishna how should he face the King and Gandhari?  
Krishna said Do not fear. I am the One who controls Life and Death. Do not fear for your life. I know Gandhari will not curse you. Please go and greet her and the king.

The five brothers and Krishna then went to meet the King.

## Dhritarashtra grieves for his sons

Monday, 13 March 2017

It was early morning when Sanjaya told Dhritarashtra about the death of Dhritarashtra. It was as if sky fell on him. He could not believe his ears. He was wondering why he was alive with all his sons dead. Taking the names of his sons, the blind king fell down. He cried out taking the names of Bhishma, Karna and Drona. He called out to Shakuni blaming him for all this. He said he was feeling like a bird whose wings had been cut. He said I had been told by all to control my sons else this would happen. I did not listen to anyone and see what has happened now. Sanjaya, it must be because of sins that I have committed that this happened to me. How will I tell people that the Bhishma who could not be defeated by Parashurama, and felled by Arjuna. This Arjuna defeated Drona and killed Karna.  
Take me to the battlefield. I will go and kill the Pandavas. I will cut off Arjuna's head and then ask Yudhistira to rule.

Hearing this, Sanjaya said - You should be saying this. You are a learned person and these words do not do justice to you. You knew what would happen. Also remember, one who is born has to die. Your son was also advised many times but he too did not listen to anyone. He just listened to Dushshasana and Shakuni and Karna. He made fun of the elders. Pandavas asked for some villages and Krishna came and tried to mediate but that did not help. You yourself tried to talk to him. Did he listen? Get from the ground. Throw the fire of anger out before it burns you.

Vidura also came and consoled the king. He said Get up O king. Do not grieve. Death is the ultimate reality. Be aware that all have gone to heaven, having fallen in Kurukshetra.

Vyasa also came there and tried to talk to Dhritarashta and console him.

## Janmajeya asks a question

Sunday, 12 March 2017

Janmajeya asked - You have told me about the great war where in the end only three warriors were left off the Kauravas.  
Dhritarashtra hearing his sons were dead, how did he react? A son like Duryodhana was dead. How did his parents behave? How were the last rites of the departed performed?

Do tell, O sage.

The sage said Listen to me while I tell you all that happened.

## Draupadi given the gem

Saturday, 11 March 2017

The Pandavas along with Krishna, Vyasa and Narada returned back to the camp. All realized that Bhima was alive because of Krishna.  
Bhima gave the gem to Draupadi. She said I feel better. I want to say something. Please give the gem to Yudhistira. Let it shine on his crown.  
Yudhistira accepted. He then asked Krishna How did Ashwatthama manage to enter our camp and kill the Pandavas in the night? Who blessed him?  
Krishna said Shiva had protected the Pandavas for the eighteen days of the war. Ahswatthama came when Shiva was about to leave. he pleased Shiva and managed to do this. Shiva is innocent. As Rudra he destroyed the sacrifice of Daksha and then again as Vyomkesh, he blessed Daksha. All Gods have done what they could by worshiping Shiva.  
Krishna said Leave all grief aside. Get ready to take over the kingdom. Rule well.

Yudhistira took the blessings of Krishna and said he would follow his advice.

## Bhima goes after Ashwatthama

Friday, 10 March 2017

Yudhistira kept grieving - What is the use of the kingdom when I have lost my dear ones.

Bhima said this is destiny and there is no point crying. If you are there, you can regain everything back. This is a cycle of life and death. You do not know where you will be next. So do not grieve. The ones who have passed on have just left the body, their soul is there. You are a learned person. You should not grieve thus.

Draupadi said I will be consoled if you get me the head of Ashwatthama. On his head is a gem. I want that gem. Then I will be relieved. Else I will kill my self and die with my sons and brothers. You are the one who killed Kichaka and Hidimb and Jatasura. You can do this. You drank the blood of Dushshasana and broke the thighs of Duryodhana. As a brahmana, Ashwatthama has behaved like a rakshasa.

Bhima asked Yudhistira for permission. Yudhistira said this was fair. Bhima decided to leave with Nakula as his charioteer. Seeing this, Krishna said - Yudhistira, do not send Bhima to get Ashwatthama. He is a great archer and Bhima will die if he comes face to face with the son of Drona. Only a great archer can defeat him. Let me tell you something. When you were in the forest, once Ashwatthama came to me in Dwaraka. He said he had the knowledge of the Brahmashira weapon and wanted to exchange that with my discus. I gave him my discus and he could not lift it. He said that the brahmashira weapon that was capable of destroying the world, and left. Yudhistira, for someone who has the knowledge of such a powerful weapon, you need to think and then decide.

Yudhistira said What do you say then? What should I do? Bhima has already left.

Krishna said Then let us follow him.  
Saying this, Krishna along with Arjuna left to catch Aswatthama. Yudhistira followed them.

Ashwatthama seeing Bhima, took a blade of grass, and reciting a mantra hurled that towards Bhima. The grass became a ferocious weapon. Blazing, it sped towards Bhima. Seeing this, Krishna asked Arjuna to use a weapon taught by Drona to counter this weapon. Arjuna released the weapon. The earth started shaking and it seemed that all would get destroyed.

Suddenly Narada and Vyasa came there and stood in the middle of the weapons. They said the earth would end if they did not withdraw their weapons. Arjuna, with the help of mantras withdrew his weapon. Arjuna did so. Ashwatthama said he had released the grass in anger and did not know how to withdraw it. The weapon once released had to kill. It would kill the unborn child of Abhimanyu and return back.

Arjuna said he wanted to cut off the head of Ashwatthama. Vyasa asked Ashwatthama to give the gem, he had on his head, to Arjuna. Vyasa said When you take the gem out, a wound will form. That will never heal. For years, you will roam the earth in pain.

Ashwatthama gave the gem to Arjuna. Vyasa said I will try to relieve you a bit. On earth, if people take your name three times and drop some oil, that oil will soothe some of your pain. The ones who take your name three times before applying oil on their bodies, will be relieved of their sins. Aswatthama then left.

When the weapon entered the womb of Uttara, Krishna entered the womb and revived the unborn child.

## Yudhistira's and Draupadi's grief

Thursday, 9 March 2017

Janmajeya asked - What happened when the Pandavas realized what had happened?  
The sage said -  
The charioteer of Dhristadyumna had managed to escape somehow. With the sun rising, he decided to inform the Pandavas about this.  
He found Yudhistira and said The army of the Pandavas has been killed in the night. Dhristadyumna, the sons of Draupadi and many brave warriors have been killed by Ashwatthama who entered our camp in the middle of the night. All were sleeping and never realized what struck them. All are dead except me. I somehow was left for dead. Having managed to escape, I have come here to inform you.

Yudhistira was angry. He said - What Gods assisted him? How did he manage to do this? The brave warriors would have been sleeping tired from battle and they were killed thus. The sons of Draupadi were jewels of our eyes and they were mercilessly killed.

He then sat down on the ground and said - What I wanted and what have I finally got? All is lost. I was happy with the sages in the forest. I have not recovered from the shock of losing Abhimanyu and I am hearing this now.

Draupadi fainted when she heard this. Shrieking loudly, she started crying out for her sons and her brothers. She said I have been through hardships all my life. When Arjuna won me, I lived in the forests and worked like a maid. And what do I get in return? I was insulted in front of all and my clothes were snatched off me. I was insulted by Kichaka and was saved by Bhima.

## Duryodhana dies

Wednesday, 8 March 2017

With great effort, Duryodhana raised himself a bit, supporting himself on his elbows. He wanted to see the heads of the Pandavas. Aswatthama gave him the bundle. Duryodhana picked the head that resembled Bhima, crushed that with his fists. The head broke. He was surprised. He then hit the other heads which easily broke.  
Duryodhana said these are not the Pandavas. The head that could withstand the blows of maces could not so easily crush with his fists.  
He then said to Ashwatthama - You have killed the sons of Draupadi and thus not left anyone alive in our Kuru dynasty.

Grieving, Duryodhana died.

Ashwatthama said I had promised that I will kill the Pandavas. I still have the weapon of Brahma. I will use that on the Pandavas.

The sun was about to rise. Seeing that, the three went and hid somewhere.

## Ashwatthama enters the Pandava camp

Tuesday, 7 March 2017

Shiva said that would not possible.  
Ashwatthama said In case that he would kill himself there in front of Shiva.  
Shiva relented. Ashwatthama then asked for the sword of Shiva. Shiva gave that and vanished.  
Ashwatthama asked Kripa to guard the gate and kill anyone who tried to escape, while he went inside.

Ashwatthama saw Dhristadyumna sleeping. He caught him. Dhristadyumna, waking up asked Ashwatthama to fight with him and let him fight, and not just cut off his head.  
Ashwatthama said you killed my father and I will kill you like an animal. Dhristadyumna pleaded with Ashwatthama, asking for a chance to fight.  
Not listening to him, Ashwatthama started kicking and hitting Dhristadyumna. He kept hitting him till all his bones were broken.

A big noise broke out in the camp. The ones who tried to escape were killed by Kripa and Kritavarma.

Ashwatthama saw the five sons of Draupadi. In the dim light, thinking they were the Pandavas, Ashwatthama cut off their heads. Thinking the Pandavas had been killed, he collected their heads and tied that in a bundle.

He then attacked Shikhadi and killed him also.

he then came out and said he had done his job. The Pandavas were dead.

They went to Duryodhana and there Ashwatthama said I have killed your enemies. The Pandavas are dead.

## Ashwatthama meets Shiva

Monday, 6 March 2017

Ashwatthama got angry hearing the words of Kripa. He said he would go ahead with his plan, and if none would come with him, then he would go alone. He would kill the son of Drupada and the Pandavas in their camp. Any one who came to their rescue would also get killed. I have promised the king that I will do so, and I will make sure I succeed. Also, the day Dhristadyumna killed my father, I promised myself that I would kill him.  
Kripa was quiet.  
Ashwatthama went near the camp, with Kripa and Kritavarma following him.  
Near the camp they saw a person. He was covered in ashes and had matted hair. With a two headed drum in hand, he was wearing tiger skin. This was Shiva who was acting as the doorkeeper. He asked Ashwatthama not to go inside. Ashwatthama said he would go and asked the person to step aside.  
Shiva said I am the doorkeeper here and you will need to fight me to get inside.  
Angry Ashwatthama started shooting arrows which were all swallowed by Shiva.  
Ashwatthama realized that this was not any ordinary person.  
He said You do not seem to be in pain after swallowing my weapons. My quiver is empty but you are standing peacefully. Please tell me Who are you?  
Shiva revealed his identity.  
He then said He would stay there till he was instructed to leave.  
Not knowing what to do, Ashwatthama built a Shiva Linga with sand and started worshiping that.

Shiva pleased asked Ashwatthama what he wanted. Ashwatthama said he wanted Shiva to leave.

## Ashwatthama plans to kill the Pandavas

Sunday, 5 March 2017

Ashwatthama along with Kripacharya and Kritavarma then left in the night for the Pandava camp. They groped their way and then sat below a tree. There they saw an owl devouring the sleeping birds. Seeing this Ashwatthama said - This is the way to kill the Pandavas.  
Kripa said this was not the correct way to kill an enemy. A sleeping enemy was not to be attacked. Duryodhana brought about this condition by not listening to advice, and you are doing the same now. You want to defeat the Pandavas, ask for a war and fight. If you are not convinced, let us talk to Dhritarashtra and see what he says.

## Ashwatthama made commander

Saturday, 4 March 2017

Janmajeya asked - What happened after this?  
The sage said -

Ashwatthama, son of Drona, came to Duryodhana. He said - You made a bad choice of making Bhishma, Drona, Karna, Shalya as commanders. If you had made me the commander, I would have won the war for you by now. Make me the commander now and I will kill the Pandavas for you.

Duryodhana said I realize now that the others were close to the Pandavas. I make you the commander. Go and kill the Pandavas for me.

Duryodhana asked Kripa to fetch some water. With the water, Duryodhana declared the son of Drona as the commander of the army of Kauravas.

## Duryodhana rebukes Krishna

Friday, 3 March 2017

Hearing Krishna's words, Duryodhana got up a little, supporting himself on his elbows. He said - Krishna, I now realize you the main culprit. You had planned all this. To you the sons of Pandu should have been the same like the sons of Dhritarashtra.

Krishna said You touched a pure Draupadi with your hands. The kings like Karna dies because of your sins. You sealed your fate when you killed Abhimanyu.

Duryodhana said I behaved like a Kshatriya. I will go to heaven with the heroes. Yudhistira can rule with the widows.

Balarama said I will kill Bhima, who did this to Duryodhana.

Krishna stopped him and said Where was your anger when Draupadi was insulted by asking her to sit on his thigh? That was was the day when Bhima promised he would break the thighs of Duryodhana. You will not know as you were not there. Hear this. There was a sage called Maitreya who used to loose his temper easily. The sage cursed Duryodhana that Bhima would break his thighs.

Balarama calmed down and but still kept abusing Bhima. He then left the place.

Slowly everyone returned to their camps.

## Bhima kicks Duryodhana

Thursday, 2 March 2017

Bhima said to the fallen Duryodhana - You insulted Draupadi in front of all. This is a result of that.  
Duryodhana was quiet. Bhima kicked him on his head, with his left foot.  
Yudhistira stopped Bhima and said this was not proper. Kicking someone was not proper, and never on the head.  
To Duryodhana - Yudhistira said You have lost all. Today you lie on the ground and your crown is in the dust. All I had asked was for five villages. You could have given that and none of this would have happened. How will Dhritarashtra live, knowing that all his sons are dead? How will I face Gandhari?  
Krishna hearing this said - Do not pity him. He dragged Draupadi in the court when she was wearing a single garment. He tried to burn you alive in the house of Lac. He does not deserve kindness.

## Bhima breaks Duryodhana's thighs

Wednesday, 1 March 2017

The Gods came to see the fight between Bhima and Duryodhana. The two were shouting and trying to hit each other like two bulls fighting. Bhima hit Duryodhana many times and Duryodhana fell, but got up again swiftly. He then struck Bhima who fell on the ground and was unable to get up.  
Yudhistira asked Krishna about this. Krishna said Duryodhana is stronger than Bhima. Yudhistira said Does that mean we will not win?  
Krishna said Calm down.  
Bhima got up. Krishna, in a playful manner slapped his thigh. He reminded Bhima about his promise.  
Bhima remember this. He also remembered that Balarama had said while teaching him, that he was never to strike anyone below the navel. He was afraid of Balarama. Then he decided to try that ignoring the fact that he could become subject to the anger of Balarama.  
Bhima jumped in the air with his mace. Duryodhana thought Bhima would strike his head. Bhima swung the mace and hit Duryodhana on his thigh. He then struck on the other thigh. Duryodhana fell with both his thighs broken.

## February

## Greatness of Kurukshetra

Tuesday, 28 February 2017

Janmajeya said - Please tell me about the greatness of Kurukshetra.  
The sage said - Your ancestor Kuru was a great king who ruled over his subjects like his own children. He was a strong king and there was no one in equal to him, in might. All over the earth, his name was known.  
Taking a large yoke, one day, the king with two blue bullocks went on to plow the earth. He plowed early in the morning and then went on to take care of his subjects. This continued for a few days.  
One day, Indra in disguise, went to the king when he was plowing. He asked the king why he was plowing. The king said he was making the land fertile. After that he would have a grand sacrifice there. He would then please Indra.  
Pleased, the person said he was Indra. The king fell at his feet. He said I had not been able to recognize you with my mortal eyes.  
Indra said I will give you a boon.  
The king said Let the land that I have tilled be known as Kurukshetra. Let the one who touches the dust of this place be relieved from all sins.  
Indra said So be it.

After that Janmajeya asked the sage - What happened to Bhima and Duryodhana?

The sage said - Sanjaya told Dhritarashra about the fight and the fall of Duryodhana. The king was mad with grief. Sanjaya said Why do you grieve now? You knew this would happen.

Dhritarashtra said Please tell me details of the fight between Bhima and Duryodhana.

## Balarama comes to Kurukshetra

Monday, 27 February 2017

Janmajeya asked the sage - Tell me about the travels of Balarama.  
The sage said -  
After visiting all the pilgrim spots, Balrama came to the ashrama of the sage Shandilya. There Balarama saw the sage Narada.  
Narada told Balarama - You went for a pilgrimage and here the Pandavas and the Kauravas have started fighting. Krishna acted as the charioteer of Arjuna. Bhishma, Drona and Karna are dead. Your nephew Abhimanyu is dead. Duryodhana is the only Kaurava left along with Kripa and Ashwatthama. He is hiding in the Dwaipayana lake. There Bhima and Duryodhana will have a fight using their maces.

Hearing Narada, Balarama left for the lake. The Pandavas and Krishna greeted Balarama. With a sad face with tears in his eyes, Duryodhana also came and greeted Balarama.  
Balarama asked Krishna Why did he let the Kauravas get defeated?

Krishna said Listen. You know about the atrocities that Duryodhana brought about, on the Pandavas. Shakuni, with his enchanted dice, won over everything of the Pandavas. Draupadi was made a maid and brought in the court. Her clothes were snatched off. At that time Dhritarashtra stopped the game. Another game happened. Who ever lost would go to the forest for twelve years and then go in hiding for a year. After all this, when the Pandavas asked for their land back, it was refused. I was insulted by Duryodhana when I went to mediate. That is when the war happened. I am not at fault.

Balarama then asked Duryodhana to give their land to them, to which Duryodhana refused. He said he had lost a lot in the war and would now not give anything away. He said I know I was unfair. Abhimanyu was killed by unfair means. I also know that I will be killed by Bhima and the complete kingdom will go to Yudhistira.

Balarama said I will then leave for Dwaraka as my words are not being listened to.

Krishna asked Balarama to wait and watch the fight between Bhima and Duryodhana.

Yudhistira said This is the land of Kurukshetra. Whoever dies here attain heaven.

## Indra kills Vritta

Sunday, 26 February 2017

Vishnu asked the Gods to approach the sage Dadhichi.The sage had to give up his bones. From the bones would be formed a weapon that would kill Vritta.  
Indra asked Why would Dadhichi give up his body?  
Vishnu said Let me tell you about Dadhichi. He is always willing to serve others. Once the twin Gods Ashwini Kumar went to Dadhichi. Dadhichi wanted to know why they had come to him? If they wanted his life, he was willing to give that up. The Gods said they wanted to be his disciples.  
Dadhichi said I will do so. The day today is not an auspicious one. Please come another day.

When Indra heard this, he along with the other Gods went to Dadhichi. When the sage asked for the reason for the visit, Indra said When the Ashiwini Kumar come back to you, please do not accept them. If you do, I will kill you.

At this point, Janmajeya asked Why did Indra say this? The sage said -

Indra knew that the Ashwini Kumar had come to Dadhichi to get the knowledge that would make them take over the throne of heaven. That is the reason why Indra had stopped Dadhichi.

The next day the twin Gods came back to Dadhichi. They sat, eager to get knowledge from the sage. The sage told the two Gods what Indra had said. The Gods thought and decided to put a horse's head on Dadhichi's neck. If Indra got to know, he would cut off the horse's head, and then the two Gods would replace the horse's head with the the original head.

The sage agreed. The twin Gods carefully cut off the head of the sage and replaced that with the head of a horse.

When Indra got to know this, he went and cut off the head. Ashwini Kumar, seeing this, replaced the head with the sage's original head.

The Gods then went back to Dadhichi and said Vishnu had said.Indra told the sage about Vritta and how the heaven was in his control. On the advise of Vishnu, they had come to him to ask for his help.

The sage he would not give up his body.

The Gods were quiet.

The sage then said he was testing them. If his body and life came in the use of others, he would give that up readily.

Sitting in a yogic posture, the sage gave up his life.

The place where the sage gave up his life is the pilgrim spot of Dadhichi.

The Gods took the body of the sage to Vishwakarma. The celestial architect created a thunderbolt from the bones of the sage. The bolt had the power of all weapons. Brahma blessed the weapon and Indra.

Indra went and attacked Vritta but could not not face his might and had to flee. Indra went to Vishnu who said the power of Vishnu was missing.

Getting power from Vishnu, Indra attacked and killed Vritta and got his kingdom back.

The place where Dadhichi gave up his body was a pilgrim spot visited by Balarama.

## Indra kills Trishira

Saturday, 25 February 2017

The sage continuing, said to Janmajeya -  
Let me tell you about the pilgrim place of Dadhichi.  
Brahma had a son called Twastha. He was a learned person. He married an asura woman and through her he had a son called Trishira, who had three heads. From one head, the boy recited the Vedas and from one he constantly took the name of Rama. With the third head he consumed liquor.  
He performed sacrifices and gave a major share of the sacrifice to the daityas.  
The Gods hearing this went to their lord Indra and asked him to correct this. In anger, Indra cut off the head of Trishira.  
Twastha, hearing this, was furious. He performed a sacrifice, and from that was born an asura called Vritta. Vritta fought and defeated Indra. He then took over the throne of Indra.  
The Gods went to Brahma. Brahma asked them to go to Vishnu.  
They prayed to Vishnu and asked him to help them.

## The pilgrimage of Soma

Friday, 24 February 2017

After this, Balarama went to the pilgrim place of Soma. He had a bath there and gave gifts of cows to the Brahmanas.

Janmajeya asked the sage about this place. The sage said -

Once Shiva was making love to Durga. The sperm of Shiva was burning and Durga unable to control that, flung that in the waters of Ganga.

Ganga herself was not able to stand this and left the sperm in the clump of grass on the banks. From that was born a boy with six heads. Six wives of Chandra, seeing the boy, came to take care of him. They fed the boy and named his Kartikeya as the first one who had fed him him was Kritika.

The son of Shiva grew up. Indra asked Shiva to get the maiden Devasena get married to Kartikeya. He would then become the husband of Devasena (Devasenapati) and would become the commander of the Gods and thus eventually would kill Tarakasura.

Shiva agreed to this.

In the war with Tarakasura, Indra lost the battle and came to Kartikeya for help. All Gods gave weapons to Kartikeya and with those, Kartikeya killed Tarakasura.

The sage said to Janmajeya - Here Balarama also heard the story of the birth of Kartikeya and then proceeded further.

## The pilgrim of Vashistha

Thursday, 23 February 2017

The sage said to Janmajeya - Listen while I tell you about the sites visited by Balarama.

Balarama went to the site of Vashishtha on the banks of river Saraswati. He bathed there and fed the poor.

Janmajeya asked - What is special about the place?

The sage said - I have told you earlier about Vashistha and Vishwamitra and their fight. Vishwamitra killed the sons of Vashishtha. He made the king Saudasa a rakshasha and through him he killed the eldest son of Vashistha. Vashishtha's daughter in law was pregnant at that time, and gave birth to the great sage Parasara. His sage was the great Vyasa.  
Vashistha's ashrama was on the eastern banks of Saraswati and Vishwamitra was on the western bank. While Vashistha did not want to fight, Vishwamitra was looking for an excuse for the quarrel.

One day Vishwamitra called out to the river Saraswati. Scared of the sage, the river appeared before him. Vishwamitra said Vashistha is meditating. He is lost in that and has no sense left. Go and drown his ashrama.  
Fearing Vishwamitra's curse, Saraswati went and flooded the ashrama of Vashistha. Vashistha was lost in meditation and had no sense. He floated away into the ashrama of Vishwamitra. Vishwamitra was pleased. He decided to get a sword and kill his enemy.  
When he went to get the sword, the river started thinking. It is because of me that Vashishtha will die. I feared being cursed and thus brought Vashistha over. Let me become cursed but let me save the life of the sage.

Thinking thus, the river pushed Vashistha back into his ashrama. When he returned, Vishwamitra was furious to see what had happened. He cursed Saraswati to become bloody.  
The waters of the river turned red. The ghosts and spirits were happy and blessed the sage for having provided them a source of blood.

The sages refused to bathe in the waters of the river. They went to Brahma and asked him to help. Brahma asked them to please Shiva.

On the banks of the river, the sages worshipped Shiva. Making a Shiva linga from the earth they worshiped that with flowers, milk, honey and butter.

Please Shiva appeared before them, riding on his bullock with the trident on his hand. The sages asked him to restore the waters of the rivers and Shiva did that.

This is a great pilgrim spot. Whoever bathes in this atones from the sin of the killing of brahmanas.

The rakshasas who had been feeding on the waters came crying. The sages said From today you will be offered the last part of the sacrifices. Feed on that and be satisfied.

## Balarama kills Suta

Wednesday, 22 February 2017

Janmajeya asked Why did Balarama go on a pilgrimage?  
The sage -

In the forest Naimisha, Shaunaka and the other sages were reading and reciting the Mahabharata. The sage Suta was reciting the epic and the other sages were listening. Balarama arrived there. All sages except Suta greeted Balarama and offered him a seat. He thought that the sages were always praising and listening to him and he was above all. Thus he did not greet Balarama.  
Balarama was hurt and he said he would teach Suta a lesson.  
Suta said he was in the process of reciting the Mahabharata and it would be a sin to leave this incomplete.  
Balarama was angry. In his anger, he jumped up with his sword and cut off the head of the sage.  
All were aghast. The killing of a brahamana was a grave sin. Suta was a learned sage and killing of his was not proper.  
Balarama was ashamed at his deed.  
Knowing this, Vyasa arrived there. All sages greeted him and received him. They told Vyasa what had happened.  
Balarama said He had committed a mistake and wanted to atone for the sin.  
Vyasa said Then lead the life of a celibate and go and visit all the pilgrim places. Perform sacrifices and feed brahmanas. Give gifts to the poor.  
Saying this Vyasa left.  
Suta's son Sauti was there. Balarama went to him and asked him to perform the last rites of his father. Balarama arranged for the ceremony and the gifts. After the ceremony he made Sauti sit in the place of his father to recite the Mahabharata.  
After this, Balarama left for his pilgrimage. He had left when the Pandavas and Kauravas had been involved in the game of dice.

Janmajeya asked the sage - What pilgrim spots did Balarama visit?

## Yudhistira taunts Duryodhana

Tuesday, 21 February 2017

Yudhistira said Why do you hide in the water, o King. Having the caused the death of your near ones, you are now hiding. Do not think you can escape in the waters.

Duryodhana could not stand this. He said You are many and I am alone. I will fight one person among you. I will fight Bhima with the mace.

Yudhistira said Then rise fast and fight Bhima.

Duryodhana rose from the waters with his mace. Seeing this, Bhima came forward with his mace.

At this same time, Balarama, who had been on a pilgrimage, arrived there.

## Pandavas find Duryodhana

Monday, 20 February 2017

The sage said to Janmajeya -

Duryodhana hid in the lake and the Pandavas kept looking for him. Not finding him, they returned to their camp. Yudhistira was not satisfied. If Duryodhana had escaped, he would come back again and fight for the kingdom.

At the lake the three warriors called out to Duryodhana to come out and fight and not run away. Ahwatthama promised that he would make a massacre of the Pandavas.

Duryodhana said Wait for a day. We will start tomorrow.

A hunter in the forest went to the lake to drink water and heard the conversation. He reported back to Yudhistira about the whereabouts of Duryodhana. Along with Krishna and an army, the Pandavas went where Duryodhana was.

Hearing the noise of the approaching army, the Kuru warriors asked Duryodhana what to do.

Duryodhana said he would hide under the water and wait for the night. In the night they would defeat the Pandavas. Hearing this the three warriors and hid themselves while Duryodhana went and hid in the water.

At the shores of the lake, Yudhistira asked Krishna how was Duryodhana hiding under the water. Krishna said Duryodhana knew mantras to control his breath.

Krishna asked the Pandavas to insult Duryodhana. He would not be able to stand that and would come out.

## Sanjaya talks to Dhritarashtra

Sunday, 19 February 2017

Sanjaya said to Dhritarashtra - The great warriors like Bhishma, Drona and Karna are dead. Arjuna has killed many great ones. He has been guided by the lotus eyed Krishna. The house of Lac was built to kill the Pandavas. Many plans were hatched.  
Seeing Yudhistira become an emperor and seeing his wealth, your son became jealous. All was snatched and the Pandavas were banished. Not stopping, your son went to the Kamya forest to see them but was captured by the Gandharvas. The queens pleaded with Yudhistira for help. Yudhistira asked Arjuna to help and Arjuna freed your son.  
Jayadhratha was sent to kidnap Draupadi. Draupadi's cries were heard by Bhima who overcame Jayadratha.  
Whereever there is Krishna, there is victory.

Hearing Sanjaya, Dhritarashtra was quiet. All were grief stricken in the palace.

Yudhistira was wondering where Duryodhana was.

## Duryodhana enters the lake

Saturday, 18 February 2017

Sanjaya said to Dhritarashtra - All is gone among the Kauravas. The Pandavas have sucked out the strength of the Kauravas.  
Duryodhana roamed around with his mace in his hand, wondering who else was there to support him.  
Seeing Duryodhana in this state, Sanajaya went to him. Seeing Sanjaya, Satyaki rushed with a sword in hand, to kill him, when he was stopped by Vyasa who appeared there.  
Duryodhana roamed with tears in his eyes. Seeing Sanjaya he asked him who all were alive. Sanjaya said Ashwatthama, Kritavarma, Kripacharya are the ones alive.  
Duryodhana said Go tell my father that I have failed. I have not listened to wisdom and now its too late.  
Seeing a lake there, Duryodhana sat in the waters of the lake.  
Sanjaya was leaving when he met Ashwatthama, Kripacharya and Kritavarma. They asked where Duryodhana was. Sanjaya said he was in the lake. The three went towards the lake.

The Pandavas were wondering where Duryodhana was. They thought he had returned back and sent a messenger. The messenger met Vidura who said he had not seen Duryodhana.

In the lake, Duryodhana began crying. Sometimes he called out to his brothers, sometimes to Karna.

Seeing this condition of Duryodhana, Dhritarashtra and Gandhari could not control their grief.

## Sahadeva kills Shakuni

Friday, 17 February 2017

With his Gandiva in his hand, Arjuna began killing the Kaurava soldiers.  
Duryodhana was fighting Dhristadyumna. Dhristadyumna cut off Duryodhana's chariot and the king had to run away. He fell in the ground and ashamed he ran off where Shakuni was.  
In the meanwhile, Kritavarma was fighting Bhima. Bhima managed to control Kritavarma.  
Duryodhana returned back to fight. Arjuna continued killing the soldiers. He killed Susharma. Susharma's son came to fight and also met his end.  
Sahadeva and Shakuni were fighting, aiming weapons at each other. Sahadeva cut off Shakuni's bow. Shakuni took another bow. Shakuni's son, Ulluka, came to assist his father. He met his end in the hands of Bhima, who killed him with a crescent arrow.  
Angry at his son's death, Shakuni started aiming arrows at Sahadeva. Sahadeva cut off all the arrows and his bow. Shakuni then took a spear and used that on Sahadeva. Sahadeva cut that off. Whatever weapons Shakuni aimed at Sahadeva were cut off. Sahadeva then cut off Shakuni's chariot. He started walking away and Sahadeva began clapping saying the Kshatriya runs away.  
Hearing this, Shakuni came back and resumed fighting. Whatever weapons he used, Shakuni cut those off. Tired, Shakuni fell on the ground. Sahadeva grabbed Shakuni by his hair and dragged him. Sahadeva said You are the root of all problems. You cheated us in dice. I will make you pay for that.  
Shakuni said Kill me but do not insult me.  
Sahadeva cut off Shakuni's head.  
Duryodhana , Kritavarma, Kripacharya and Ashwatthama were the only strong warriors left in the Kaurava camp.

## Sahadeva plans to kill Shakuni

Thursday, 16 February 2017

Duryodhana agreed to lead the army. He soon came across Bhima. They started abusing each other and then began fighting. Defeated by Bhima, Duryodhana fled.  
Bhima attacked the army with his mace.  
The king Shalva came to fight. He came on a elephant. The elephant was killed by Dhristadyumna. The king jumped off the elephant and was attacked by Satyaki. Satyaki cut off Shalva's weapons. Seeing this, Kritavarma came to his rescue. Satyaki aimed arrows at Kritavarma, who had to flee. Shalva and Satyaki began fighting again and Shalva met his end in the hands of Satyaki.  
Seeing this, Kritavarma came again and attacked Satyaki. Satyaki cut off the chariot of Kritavarma. Kripacharya pulled Kritavarma on his chariot.  
Duryodhana came to fight with his bow and arrows.  
Yudhistira and Shakuni began fighting. Shakuni cut off Yudhistira's chariot. Seeing this Yudhistira jumped on the ground. Sahadeva seeing this, picked up Yudhistira on his chariot.  
The Pandavas began planning to kill Shakuni.  
Shakuni came to fight with Duryodhana supporting him.  
Sahadeva was excited. He remembered that he had promised to kill Shakuni. With the armies fighting each other, Sahadeva and Shakuni also began fighting, and the sky was covered with arrows.  
Shakuni was fighting fiercely and the Pandava army started stepping back. Seeing this, Krishna said -Why is this happening? The army that could stand Bhishma, Drona and Karna should be able to withstand the might of Shakuni. Kill Shakuni today.

Arjuna went ahead and started slaughtering the Kaurava army.

## Shalya dies

Wednesday, 15 February 2017

Yudhistira said Shalya is injured.  
Krishna said He is no relative of yours now but an enemy. Do not let go off this chance.

Hearing this, Yudhistira picked up a spear the likes of which Ravana had used on Lakshmana. He aimed that at Shalya. Shalya tried to combat the spear, but the spear hit him on his chest and Shalya died.

Seeing this, the younger brother of Shalya attacked Yudhistira but also killed in the process.

While the Pandavas began celebrating the loss of Shalya, the Kauravas were grief stricken.

## Shalya fights

Tuesday, 14 February 2017

Dhritarashtra asked Sanjaya - Please tell me how of the Kaurava warriors are left? What did Shalya and Duryodhana do? My son has brought an end with his thought process.  
Sanajay said There are eleven thousand soldiers riding on chariots. There many horses and elephants and crores of soldiers, The pandavas have a similar number in their army and none is less than the other.  
Yudhistira was fighting away. Seeing this Shalya got enraged. He went to attack.  
Nakula was fighting Chitrasena, son of Karna. Chitrasena cut off Nakula's bow. He cut off Nakula's chariot. Nakula jumped down with his sword and shield. With a blow he cut off Chitrasena's head. Two brothers, Satyasena and Sushena began fighting Nakula. Satyasena aimed a weapon at Nakula, who dodged the weapon. He then hit Satyasena with a weapon, who fell. Sushena seeing this cut off Nalula's chariot. Nakula jumped on to another chariot and then killed Sushena.

Shalya and Yudhistira began fighting. Kritavarma and Kripacharya came to help Shalya. Bhima joined the fight. He killed off the horses and the charioteer of Shalya's chariot.

Shalya jumped onto another chariot and then aimed a spear at Bhima. The spear pierced Bhima, who easily and plucked it off and threw it aside. He then hurled that at Shalya, who hit by the weapon, fell on his chariot.

Shalya then jumped down with his mace and both Bhima and Shalya began fighting. Later both discarded their maces and began fighting with their bows and arrows.

Later Yudhistira and Satyaki joined Bhima. Yudhistira cut off Shalya's chariot. Yudhistira was agitated. He had made up his mind to defeat Shalya. Shalya aimed weapons at Yudhistira managing to control him. Bhima came to Yudhistira rescue and has his armor cut by Shalya.

Yudhistira then aimed weapons at Shalya. Shalya collapsed on his chariot.

Seeing this, Krishna said to Yudhistira - Kill Shalya now.

## Shalya made commander

Monday, 13 February 2017

At the death of Karna, Duryodhana was shattered. He was wondering who would be the commander of the army now. He asked Shalya to be the commander to which Shalya agreed.  
Krishna said to Yudhistira - See the Kaurava army coming. Go and fight. Make an end of Shalya. With Bhishma, Drona and Karna gone, what can Shalya do?

Shalya created a formation for the Kaurava army to attack.

## Karna dies

Sunday, 12 February 2017

The snake said - Arjuna killed my mother when he burnt the Khandava forest. I want my revenge.  
Saying this, the snake rose to the sky. His mouth was emitting fire. Karna released an arrow. The snake climbed on that.  
The arrow shot forth.  
Krishna saw this and realized the danger. He pressed the chariot with his knee and the chariot went partly into the ground. The arrow touched Arjuna's crown and fell with that on the ground.

The snake went back to Karna and asked for a chance again to kill Arjuna. Krishna asked Arjuna to quickly kill the snake.With six arrows, Arjuna cut off the snake. Krishna then brought the chariot up from the ground.

Karna and Arjuna began fighting again. Each tried to kill the other off but was only wounding the parties. At one points, Karna was tired. Krishna said to Arjuna to kill Karna soon.

Karna began shooting arrows. Suddenly the wheel of Karna's chariot got stuck in the earth as if the earth was trying to swallow the wheel.

Karna asked Arjuna to wait while he took the wheel out. He said The one who has put his arms down is not to be attacked. It is the act of a coward to attack such a person.You are on a chariot and do not realize what I am going through now. Also, Krishna is with you. My destiny is against me and the earth has swallowed my wheel. Let me get that out.

Hearing this Krishna said - At times like this, the evil become preachers. You were an aide of Duryodhana and Shakuni in all evil deeds, Where was your preaching when the kingdom of the Pandavas was taken away? Where was this when the plans to burn the Pandavas alive happened in the house of Lac? Seven of you surrounded Abhimanyu and killed him. Where was justice then?

Hearing all this, Arjuna became angry. Knowing that Arjuna would attack, Karna got out a arrows and aimed them at Arjuna. While Arjuna was thinking what to do, Karna got busy trying to extract the wheel.

Taking this opportunity, Arjuna took an arrow given to him by Shiva and aimed that. The arrow struck Karna. The sun then began to set and the battlefield was golden in hue. With the sun setting, the soul of Karna went along with the sun, up in the sky.

Duryodhana was shocked. The army began running around. Bhima seeing this, began killing off soldiers.

Duryodhana retired to his camp.The war was called off. The Pandavas returned to their camp. Yudhistira was given the news. Yudhistira wanted to see Karna's body. Krishna took Yudhistira where Karna's body was lying near the body of his dead son.

## Some changes in Mahabharata

Saturday, 11 February 2017

I discovered that some initial posts were missing or were in a different order.  
I am reviewing all the posts to ensure that they are correct and in the proper order.

## Arjuna kills Vrishasena

Saturday, 11 February 2017

Duryodhana said to Karna - Arjuna comes with his Gandiva. Karna left.  
Bhima, with blood all over him, came and started showering arrows. Duryodhana had no option but to run away.  
Karna's son Vrishasena started fighting with Nakula. Vrishasena cut off Nakula's chariot and Nakula climbed up on Bhima's chariot. Duryodhana, Kripa and Ashawatthama came to support Vrishnasena, while Dhristadyumna and the five sons of Draupadi, along with Sahadeva came to support Nakula.  
Arjuna came there and Vrishnasena shot arrows at Arjuna, wounding him.  
He then managed to subdue Bhima and Sahadeva.  
Arjuna then took arrows and those he pierced Vrishasena's armor. With another arrow, Arjuna cut off the head of Vrishasena, which fell near Karna.  
Karna had tears in his eyes.  
He then came to face Arjuna.  
Krishna said to Arjuna - Be careful. Karna, mad with grief, in coming towards you.  
The two warriors faced each other like two blazing suns.  
Karna asked Shalya - Tell me one thing. If I die in the battle with Arjuna, what will you do?  
Shalya said - I will kill the Pandavas along with Krishna.  
Arjuna asked Krishna the same question.  
Krishna said Listen Arjuna. Even if the world goes upside down, Karna will not be able to win over you.  
Arjuna said Then I will kill Karna today.

Karna and Arjuna began shooting arrows at each other. Karna used the Brahmaastra on Arjuna. Krishna took the blow and managed to divert the weapon. Bhima then asked Arjuna to kill Karna quickly.

Karna was in a frenzy. He managed to thwart all weapons coming towards him. He even managed to hit Krishna.

Arjuna managed to hit Shalya with his arrows.

A snake that was hiding in the ground came out. He went to Karna and said Arjuna killed my brothers. I want to kill Arjuna. Please tell me how I can help you.

## Bhima kills Dushshasana

Friday, 10 February 2017

Krishna and Arjuna returned to the battlefield. Bhima took stock of how many weapons he still had with him. Arjuna covered the sky with his arrows. Shakuni said to Duryodhana - See, Arjuna is destroying our army.  
Bhima's attack was ferocious. Karna came to attack. He started killing the Pandava army with his arrows.  
Arjuna said to Krishna - See Karna there. He is killing off our soldiers and defeating our warriors.  
Krishna smiled and turned the chariot towards Karna. Seeing this, Duryodhana said to Karna - See there come the greatest heroes of all time. Get your army together and kill Arjuna today.

Along with Ashwatthama and Dushsasana, Karna faced Arjuna.  
Bhima with his mace tried to kill off the army but Dushshasana shot arrows wounding him. Like a made elephant, Bhima took his mace and rushed towards him. With the blows, he destroyed Dushshasana's chariot, charioteer and horses. Remembering his promise, he went where the Kaurava prince was and caught him. Taking a sword he brought it down on him, cutting his chest. With his hands, he plucked his heart out. He then drank Dushsshasana's blood.

Duryodhana and Karna were seeing this. Karna tried to control Bhima but could not. Bhima ran around, his face smeared with blood. Thinking this was a rakshasa, anyone who came close to him, ran away.

Duryodhana was sad at the death of his brother.

## Arjuna promises to kill Karna

Thursday, 9 February 2017

Yudhistira was lying down when Arjuna entered. Yudhistira got up and smiled thinking that Karna had probably been killed. He said I have been through a lot of pain today because of him. Has the disciple of Parashurama, been killed?  
Arjuan said I was fighting in a different part of the field and did not get to face Karna. I fought Ashwatthama whom I managed to turn away. I was going to catch Karna when I heard from Bhima how he insulted you, so I came to you.  
Yudhistira said You should not have left Bhima alone and come to see me. If you plan to kill Karna, why have you fled from the field? You have celestial bows and weapons. You have Hanumana on your chariot.You have a celestial chariot. You have Krishna for your charioteer. If Krishna was there instead of you, all the Kurus would have been dead by now. If you cannot manage, give Krishna the Gandiva. Let him handle the war.  
Arjuna got angry.  
Krishna controlled him. Arjuna said - I have made a promise that I will Karna. I did not say I have done it. Anyone who insults my Gandiva is my enemy. I will kill my enemy even if that is my Guru. I will suffer the pains of hell later. What is Yudhistira saying? He is scared to face the battle, and now he comes and blames me for all this. He runs away from Karna and wants me to kill him. Bhima is the field. He does not insult me. Yudhistira goes and plays a game of dice and looses all. For him all of us had to suffer and now he says this.  
Krishna said - Do not praise yourself. That is the job of a coward.  
Arjuna bowed down to Yudhistira and begged for forgiveness. He then said before the end of the day Karna would be dead.  
To Krishna he said - With your blessing, I will win over the battle and be able to kill Karna. Yudhistira will then be able to sleep peacefully.

## Karna defeats the Pandavas other than Arjuna

Wednesday, 8 February 2017

In the battlefield, Shalya said to Karna - See the Pandava army. There are Arjuna and Bhima. Do you think you can defeat them?  
Angry Karna started shooting arrows at the army. A son of Karna entered the field and was killed by Bhima, who then attacked Kripa and cut off his bow. He then attacked Dushsasana and wounded him. He then attacked Shakuni and Uluka wounding them.  
Seeing his son dead and also remembering defeat in the hands of Arjuna, Karna decided to kill Yudhistira. Shivering with rage, he started shooting arrows killing the Pandava army.  
Karna cut off the bow of Yudhistira who took another one. Yudhistira shot an arrow which hit Karna on the right side and he fainted on his chariot.  
Regaining consciousness, Karna took his bow and a divine arrow and aimed that towards Yudhistira. Pandava warriors came in support of Yudhistira. Karna wounded Yudhistira, who in rage hurled a spear which hit Karna. Karna retaliated and shot arrows which pierced Yudhistira's armor. Karna then went to him and said You are a Kshatriya. My advise to you is, do not fight anymore. Go back, if you want to live.  
Saying this, Karna left.  
Bhima then attacked Karna. Karna cut off Bhima's bow. Bhima then hit Karna with his mace. Karna fell on his chariot.  
Ashwatthama pledged to kill Dhristadyumna. He came and attacked the army, looking for Dhristadyumna. He was confronted by Bhima.  
Karna, recovering began killing the Pandava army. Yudhistira decided to go and fight again and was wounded again. Nakula and Sahadeva came to help Yudhistira. Karna cut off Nakula's chariot and horses. Nakula climbed up on Sahadeva's chariot. Shalya saw his nephews fighting Karna. He said to Karna - You said you will fight Arjuna. Forgetting that you fought with Yudhistira.  
Hearing this Karna decided to go in search of Arjuna.

Yudhistira was taken away to the camp by Nakula and Sahadeva.

Karna began using all the weapons, the skills of which he had learnt from Parashurama. All the while he was calling out to Arjuna, asking for a fight.

Krishna said to Arjuna - Karna is destroying the army. I see Bhima fighting. I do not see Yudhistira anywhere.

Arjuna said - Take the chariot towards Karna. Let me see if I can spot Yudhistira.

Krishna started moving the chariot in the direction of Karna when Ashwatthama came and blocked their way. Arjuna won over the son of Drona and then seeing Bhima, asked him about Yudhistira. Bhima said Yudhistira had gone back to the camp as he was hurt.

Arjuna said You go and take care of Yudhistira and I will take care of the battle. Bhima said he would stay and fight.  
Hearing this, Arjuna went towards the camp to see Yudhistira.

## Duryodhana and Karna talk

Tuesday, 7 February 2017

In the camp Karna was ashamed of having to flee from Arjuna.  
Duryodhana said If I knew you would not be able to win the battle for me, I would have taken refuge with the Pandavas.  
Karna said It is because of Krishna, who is Arjuna's charioteer, that I am unable to control him. If I get the king Shalya as my charioteer, I will be able to win over Arjuna.

Hearing this, Duryodhana went where the king of Madra, Shalya, was sitting.

Shalya agreed and climbed up on Karna's chariot. Karna said He would now be able to face all and also make an end of Arjuna.

Shalya said Leave your pride aside. If the entire army attacks and Arjuna is there alone, he will be able to win over all easily.

This made Karna angry.

In the Pandava camp, Yudhistira asked Arjuna to create a formation that Karna would not be able to cross.

## Karna leads the army

Monday, 6 February 2017

With Bhishma and Drona not there, Duryodhana was wondering who would lead the army. Shakuni said Karna was there who would be able to lead them.  
Duryodhana arranged for a ceremony to make Karna the commander.  
The army got ready and marched into the battlefield.  
Seeing Karna in the field, Yudhistira said to Arjuna - There goes Karna, whose name brings fear in the hearts of many. Defeat him and bring glory to your name.

Arjuna formed a crescent formation for the Pandava army.

The two sides began fighting. Bhima took his bow and arrows and climbed up on an elephant. A king, called Kshemmurti, in the kaurava side was angry seeing Bhima. He began shooting arrows at Bhima. He cut off Bhima's bow. While Bhima got another bow, the king killed Bhima's elephant. Bhima jumped down with his mace. He picked up the dead elephant and hurled that at the king who avoided being hit. Bhima then hit him with his mace, killing him.

Karna then started attacking the Pandava army with his arrows.  
Bhima began fighting Ashwattthama. Yudhistira fought with Duryodhana and Arjuna with the Narayani army. The princes of Avanti, Bind and Anubind were fighting Satyaki. They cut off Satyaki's bow. Satyaki took another bow and with a sharp arrow, killed Anubind. Seeing his brother fall, Bind started shooting arrows at Satyaki, wounding him till he started bleeding. They two cut off each others charioteers and horses. They kept fighting each other till they were exhausted.

Bhima and Ashwatthama were fighting each other. Both were skilled and could not win over each other.

Arjuna was raining arrows like it was monsoon. The Narayani army was falling. Ashwatthama saw this and attacked. Arjuna aimed arrows at Drona's son to stop him.

A king of Magadha, called Dandadhara, attacked riding on elephants. Arjuna attacked the elephants. Arjuna then killed the king.

Ashwatthama was killing soldiers. Seeing this, Arjuna renewed his attack. Karna came to assist Ashwatthama and all had to flee, as they could not stand Arjuna's attack.

With the sun setting, the war ended.

## Ashwatthama vows to kill Dhristadyumna

Sunday, 5 February 2017

The sage said to Janmajeya -  
All were in shock on hearing off Drona's death. Karna said to Duryodhana - The Guru had become old and was weak. Leave the Pandavas to me. I will get them for you.  
Ashwatthama then came there with Kritavarma and Kripacharya. He pledged to kill Dhristadyumna.

## Drona killed

Saturday, 4 February 2017

Seeing Bhagdatta fall, Duryodhana came to attack, riding an elephant called Ashwatthama. He went to face Bhima.  
Bhima was on a killing spree, killing who ever crossed his path. Duryodhana then faced Bhima. With a blow of his mace, Bhima killed the elephant by crushing its skull.  
Duryodhana jumped down and began fighting with his mace.  
Bhima started hitting Duryodhana with his mace. Not able to face Bhima, Duryodhana fled from there. Seeing Duryodhana run, Bhima resumed killing off the army.  
Drona saw this and began shooting arrows at Bhima, wounding him. Bhima fled from there. Arjuna then came to face Drona. With ten arrows, Drona managed to subdue Arjuna and took off elsewhere.  
Whoever faced Drona was being killed. Yudhistira was worried.  
Krishna then said to Drona, Ashwatthama has fallen today, killed by Bhima.  
Drona could not believe his ears. Aswatthama was supposed to be immortal. How could he fall?  
He said to Krishna -I do not understand your illusions. My son was supposed to be immortal.  
Krishna said Ashwatthama has fallen, killed by Bhima. If you do not believe me, ask Yudhistira.  
Drona said I will believe this only if Yudhistira says this to me.  
Krishna then called Yudhistira and asked him to tell Drona that Ashwatthama had fallen. Yudhistira said he would not tell a lie.  
Krishna said This is not a lie. The elephant Ashwatthama has fallen.  
Yudhistira said This is still a lie. Drona's son is not dead.If I lie I will go to hell.  
Bhima got angry and said Being the eldest of the Pandavas who managed to throw away everything and now to save all do not want to lie. Where was justice when Abhimanyu was surrounded by many warriors and killed. If Drona asks me I will say Ashwatthama is dead.  
Saying this Bhima began shouting Ahwatthama is dead.  
Drona said I will trust only Yudhistira.  
Yudhistira said quietly Ashwatthama the elephant is dead.  
Drona said I did not hear you. Speak loudly.  
Yudhistira said Ashwatthama is dead. Quietly he said Elephant. Many times Drona asked him, every time Yudhistira said the same.  
Drona dropped his weapons and began crying.  
Krishna then asked Arjuna to attack. Arjuna, with his weapons, cut off Drona's weapons.  
Dhristadyumna seeing this, climbed Drona's chariot with a sword and cut off his head.

Duryodhana was aghast hearing about the death of Drona.

## Bhagdatta killed

Friday, 3 February 2017

Seeing Bhagdatta, Arjuna said to Krishna - He is killing off the Pandava army. I promise that I will kill him today. Please take me close to him.

Hearing this, Krishna smiled and turned the chariot. Arrows started pouring like rain. Bhagdatta shouted that he would kill Arjuna, Arjuna said Leave your pride aside and fight.

Arjuna and Bhagdatta began fighting. Bhagdatta tried to trample the chariot with his elephant but Krishna quickly managed to maneuver the chariot quickly. Bhagdatta shot arrows which were cut by Arjuna. Arjuna then hit an arrow at the belly of the elephant. The elephant collapsed. Bhagdatta then got a chariot driven by elephants and began fighting, riding that.

Bhagdatta shot arrows which Arjuna cut off. Bhagdatta then used weapons which Arjuna was not able to combat and fell on the chariot. This angered Krishna who said You are not concentrating. You said you will kill Bhagdatta. How do you plan to do this? Use divine weapons and kill him fast.

Arjuna used a divine weapon which Bhagdatta managed to avoid. He then got a weapon called Vaishnava and said he would use that. Bhagdatta released the arrow. Seeing this, Krishna pushed Arjuna aside and faced the weapon. The weapon merged with Krishna.

Arjuna asked Krishna why he did this.  
Krishna said There was no way Arjuna could have faced the weapon. Arjuna said What is this weapon? How did Bhagdatta get this?  
Krishna said Narakasura got this weapon from me. From Naraka, this weapon went to his son Bhagdatta. This weapon will always kill its target and there is no way it will be futile. You will not be able to combat it, which is why I faced it. Now that Bhagdatta has used that weapon, quickly cut off the head of Bhagdatta.

'Arjuna then shot five arrows and cut off Bhagdatta's bow. Bhagdatta picked another bow and that was cut. This continued with Arjuna cutting off all bows of Bhagdatta.  
Bhagdatta then began hurling spears at Arjuna who cut them. With a weapon, Arjuna then killed Bhagdatta. The dead body of Bhagdatta fell on the chariot.

## Death of Drupada

Thursday, 2 February 2017

In the morning, the battle began. Arjuna with Krishna left to fight.  
Bhima was fighting Duryodhana. Satyaki was fighting Karna, Drona with Dhristadyumna, Virata with Somdatta, Sahadeva with Shakuni, Nakula with Dushsasana and Drupada with Bhagdatta. While Shikahndi was fighting with Ashwatthama and Yudhistira was fighting Shalya.

Dushsasana was wounded by Nakula and fainted in the battlefield and had to be carried away. He came back again but had to face a defeat.

Bhagdatta was fighting Drupada. This son of Naraka was sitting on an elephant. Drupada used weapons which were cut off by Bhagdatta. Seeing this, Drupada shot five sharp arrows at Bhagdatta, which penetrated his armor. Bhagdatta began bleeding. Bhagdatta then, with an arrow, cut off Drupada's bow. He then killed the charioteers and the horses. Then choosing an arrow he shot that at Drupada, killing him.

Yudhistira was shocked at the death of Drupada.

The Pandavas were in grief, while the Kauravas were rejoicing.

Shikhandi was fighting Ashwatthama. Shikhandi cut off the arrows of Ashwatthama. Ashwatthama then shot an arrow which wounded Shikhandi. Shikhandi fainted. Shikhandi's chariuoteer fled with him.

Sahadeva, son of Jarasandha, was fighting Kripa. He wounded Kripa, who had to flee, wounded.

Kritavarma and Chekitan were fighting each other. Chekitan had to flee from the attack.

The king of Kashi was fighting with Yuyutsu. Yuyutsu cut off the bow of the king of Kashi. With his bow cut off, the king jumped off from his chariot with his sword and shield. With his sword, he cut off the horses of Yuyutsu's chariot and then cut of the head of the charioteer. Seeing this, Yuyutsu fled. Seeing Yuyutsu flee, Yudhistira stepped in. Shalya saw Yudhistira and came to face him. Yudhistira cut off the bow of Shalya. Shalya jumped with his mace, and with a blow, broke Yudhistira's chariot. Yudhistira jumped down and fled.

Bhima then started killing soldiers with his mace. Seeing this, Bhagdatta attacked Bhima, riding his elephant. He tried to trample Bhima with the elephant. Bhima caught the elephant by its feet and tried to lift it, but was unable to. He then hit the elephant hard on its stomach. Hurt, the elephant ran away from there.

Bhagdatta, then returned and started killing the Pandava soldiers.

## Karna gives away his armor

Wednesday, 1 February 2017

Bhima was devastated to see his son die. Taking his mace, he began killing soldiers, horses and elephants. The soldiers were tired, having fought all night but fearing Duryodhana they could not refuse, so agreed to face Bhima and die.  
Arjuna asked for the war to be called for. All were tired and needed rest.  
He then sat with Bhima trying to console him. Arjuna said I understand your pain. I too lost Abhimanyu. Krishna, Please tell us what we should do now.  
Krishna said Know that death of Ghatotkacha means life for you, Arjuna.  
Karna was born with armor and earrings. With these, no weapon could pierce him.  
Also, Karna was a great believer in charity and never refused anyone who came to him.  
Knowing this, Indra went to Karna in the guise of a brahman.  
Karna saw the brahman and bowed down to him and asked who he was and where he was coming from.  
The brahman said he had come from far for alms.  
Karna said What do you want.  
The brahman said First vow that you will give me what I ask for.  
Karna wondered who this was in the guise of a brahman. Leaving the thought aside, Karna promised that whatever was asked for, would be given.  
The brahman said Then give me your armor and earrings.  
Karna remembering Surya telling him about this. He then said I know you are Indra. You have come for Arjuna. If you had asked for my life, I would have given you that. Why do you fear for Arjuna when Krishna is with him?  
Saying this, Karna took a sword and then cut off the armor and the earrings from his body.  
Indra said Ask for something.  
Karna said I know of a weapon that you have that will never miss its mark. Give me that. Indra gave that to Karna, and left with the armor.

Arjuna, Karna had been saving this to use on you. He had to use it on Ghatotkacha thus saving your life.

## January

## Ghatotkacha dies

Tuesday, 31 January 2017

Seeing the death of his father, Alambusha's son, Alambushi, came on an elephant to take revenge. Ghatotkacha killed the elephant. Alambushi escaped and then came riding a chariot. He started shooting arrows at Ghatotkacha. Ghatotkacha smashed the chariot. Alambushi and Ghatotkacha then began fighting with their mace. Ghtotkacha first broke Alambushi's hands and then smashed his chest. Alambushi died.  
The Kaurava army was now scared. Ghatotkacha, with his mace, began smashing the soldiers. A king called Pandya said he would fight Ghatotkacha. He began shooting arrows, which broke when they touched the mace of Ghatotkacha. With arrows, the king then cut the mace. Ghatotkacha then picked up the chariot and flung it far, killing the king.  
Seeing the king fall, some warriors attacked Ghtotkacha shooting arrows. With his arrows, Ghotkacha killed them.  
Duryodhana was worried. Ashwatthama then said Karna has a weapon which is sure to kill whomever it is aimed at. Let Karna use that to kill this demon.  
Karna said I have been saving that to kill Arjuna. I got that by giving away my armor, with which I was born. If I kill Ghatotkacha with that weapon, know for sure I will also die in the battlefield.  
Ashwatthama said Get rid of Ghatotkacha first. You will fight Arjuna only if Ghatotkacha dies. Else we all will be dead.  
Duryodhana then asked Karna to use that weapon on Ghatotkacha.  
Karna then went to face Ghatotkacha. He tried to subdue Ghatotkacha with various weapons. What that was not working, Karna got the weapon that he had stored. He aimed that at Ghatotkacha.  
Ghatotkacha realized that this will mean his death. He tried to break off the weapon with various other weapons of his, but none worked.  
The weapon pierced Ghatotkacha. He shouted out in pain to Bhima- I am dying. What do you want me to do before I die.  
Bhima said Crush as many Kaurava warriors as you can.  
Ghatotkacha increased his size and fell on the army, crushing many soldiers.  
Cries broke in both the sides.  
It was the second part of the night when Ghatotkacha fell.

## Ghatotkacha kills Alambusha

Monday, 30 January 2017

After the death of Jayadratha, Krishna said to Arjuna - Let us rush to Yudhistira. He would be anxious for us. They also went to Yudhistira who was relieved to see them. They told him about the death of Jayadratha.

In the Kaurava camp, Duryodhana gave orders that there would be no rest. Torches would be lit and the fight would happen in the night also. The Pandava army hearing also lit up torches.

Drona tried to capture Yudhistira but was stopped by Dhristadyumna. Drona wounded Dhristadyumna, who then fled. Satyaki then attacked Drona. Satyaki also was wounded by Drona. When Yudhistira was wondering what would happen, Hidimba's son, Ghatotkacha, came to his rescue. Taking his mace, Ghatotkacha blasted his way into the Kaurava army. He was followed by Sahadeva, son of Jarasandha, five sons of Draupadi and Yudhistira.

Ghatotkacha made his body like a mountain. He then started eating up the Kaurava soldiers. Doshan, son of Dushsasana attacked Ghatotkacha. Ghatotkacha smashed his chariot and Doshan barely escaped. Ghatotkacha, seeing this, brought his mace down on Doshan, killing him.

Seeing this angered Dushsasana who attacked but had to flee, not able to stand the might of Ghatotkacha.  
Using the magic of the rakshasas, Ghatotkacha played havoc on the Kauravas.

Seeing all perplexed, Karna decided to attack. Ghatotkacha smashed Karna's chariot and Karna barely managed to save himself. Ashwatthama then came but could stand the might of Ghatotkacha.

Alambusha then came into the attack. Ghatotkacha and Alambusha began fighting each other. Alambusha hid in the skies and Ghatotkacha went and caught up with him. Alambusha started showering arrows and Ghatotkacha broke his bow. Both then began fighting with their maces. Alambusha took the form of a mountain range and attacked Ghatotkacha. Ghatotkacha climbed up on the mountain and began smashing till he saw Alambusha. He caught Alambusha by his hair and dragged him to the ground. With his mace, he then smashed his head.

## Jayadratha dies

Sunday, 29 January 2017

Killing the sons of Bhima, Bhima began killing soldiers on the field. With the remaining brothers, Duryodhana attacked Bhima. Bhima wounded Duryodhana with his arrows. Seeing this Dushshasana and the other eight brothers attacked. Taking his mace, Bhima killed eight of the brothers. Off the sons of Dhritarashtra and Gandhari, only Duryodhana and Dushsasana were alive.

Karna came again to attack. He cut off Bhima's mace. Without a weapon, Bhima hurled a dead elephant at Karna. Karna cut that off. Bhima started throwing other dead animals and then dead bodies of soldiers which Karna cut off. Bhima was now empty handed and bleeding . Karna caught him. Remembering his promise to Kunti, he released Bhima.

Arjuna had traveled a long distance. Some more, and then Jayadratha would be in front of him. Krishna said - Only four hours are left for the sun to set. Please hurry up.  
Arjuna said I still do not see Jayadratha. How do I kill the one I do not see.

Krishna said Do not worry. Jayadratha will die today.
