Seeing Arjuna coming, Jayadrata hid himself. Krishna then asked Sudarshana to go and cover the sun. It suddenly became dark. The kuru army was happy. Now Arjuna will kill himself.

Jayadratha then came in front of Arjuna and reminded him of the promise.  
Arjuna said I will not go back on my word. I will kill myself.

Arjuna then began arranging for a pyre and lit that. Krishna said If you have to enter the fire, take your weapons with you.

Picking up the Gandiva, Arjuna went around the fire seven times. Krishna then removed Sudarshana. The sun would be there for two more hours. Krishna asked Arjuna to make use of the opportunity. Krishan said Make sure you do not drop his cut head on the ground. Using arrows, throw the head onto the lap of his father who lives in the forest as a mendicant.

Arjuna did that. The cut head fell on the lap of the old mendicant. The old king threw that on the ground and his head burst into a thousand pieces.

After this, Arjuna asked Krishna about the secret of Jayadratha.

Krishna said Jayadratha had prayed to Shiva and pleased him. Shiva had then granted him a boon that who ever dropped his head on the ground would die.

Hearing this, Arjuna bowed down at Krishna for helping him.

## Bhima kills ninety sons of Dhritarashtra

Saturday, 28 January 2017

Yudhistira was wondering how Arjuna was faring. With Satyaki also away, he called Bhima. He said Bhima, Arjuna is fighting somewhere. I sent Satyaki and he has not returned.  
Bhima called Dhristadyumna and asked him to protect Yudhistira till he returned.  
Saying this, Bhima climbed on his chariot and went into the middle of the battlefield.

Karna stopped Bhima. Bhima cut off his chariot and his weapons. Karna then fled.

Duryodhana seeing this, asked his brothers to attack Bhima.  
Ten sons of Dhritarashtra came and started showering arrows at Bhima. Bhima jumped with his mace and smashed the brothers killing them.  
Thirty more brothers of Duryodhana then attacked Bhima. With his mace, Bhima killed the horses and elephants on which they were riding. He then killed the thirty brothers.

Bhima then started his chariot and soon saw Satyaki. He asked him what was happening. Satyaki asked Bhima why he had left Yudhistira. Bhima said hearing no news, Yudhistira had sent him.

Karna came again, and Bhima began shooting arrows at Karna. Karna fled again.

Bhima then began killing off the soldiers. Fifty of Duryodhana's brothers, seeing this, came and attacked Bhima. Bhima killed all fifty of them.

Sanjaya told Dhritarashtra that ninety of his sons had died so far in battle. Dhritarashtra fell from his throne.  
Gandhari, hearing this, fainted. Wails broke out in the palace when the wives heard of the loss of their husbands.

Sanjaya said You knew things would come to this. Many times it was asked to avoid the battle. Vidura said All Yudhistira had asked for was five villages. If that had been given, things would not come to this.

## Bhurishrava dies

Friday, 27 January 2017

With his hands cut off, Bhurishrava fainted. Regaining consciousness, he said to Arjuna - Shame on you. You cut off my hands in an unfair manner. I was fighting Satyaki. Why did you interfere? You will go to hell for this.  
Arjuna was ashamed.  
Krishna said - Do not fear Arjuna. Bhurishrava talks of fair war. What did he do when Abhimanyu was killed by unfair means?  
Bhurishrava said Krishna, I know no one as evil as you.  
He then realized he was cursing the Lord and kept quiet.  
He then said I will not go anywhere cursing the Lord.  
Krishna said Leave all ill will aside and go off to Vaikuntha.

Bhurishrava sat quietly. Satyaki, seeing his enemy, sitting on the ground, jumped with a sword in his hand.

He cut off Bhurishrava's head. The kauravas seeing this started showering arrows at Satyaki. Satyaki them jumped on his chariot and began fighting.

## Birth of Bhurishrava

Thursday, 26 January 2017

The sage said to Janmajeya - Let me tell you about Satyaki a bit.

Once Krishna was performing a sacrifice for pleasing the departed forefathers. He invited many kings for the ceremony. Of the invited kings was a king called Somdatta. Seeing this angered Shini, who was a uncle of Krishna and the brother of Vasudeva. Shini said how could Somdatta be there? He was not fit to be invited? Somdatta also started abusing Shini. Shini got up and grabbed Somdatta by his hair. He then started slapping Somdatta till his teeth started shaking.

The insulted Somdatta left. He went to the forest to pray for revenge. He prayed to Shiva who appeared before him. He asked Shiva to bless him with a son who would be able to avenge him. Shiva said a son would be born who would win over the son of Shini.

This was Bhurishrava, who with the blessing of Shiva, was able to capture Satyaki.

## Bhurishrava catches Satyaki

Wednesday, 25 January 2017

While Arjuna had gone to look for Jayadratha, Yudhistira was worried. Finding no one, he asked Satyaki to lead the battle.

Satyaki went inside the formation and started killing warriors. The king Bhurishrava, seeing this came and attacked Satyaki. Satyaki cut off the arrows of Bhurishrava.  
Bhurisharava asked Satyaki to come down on the ground and fight.  
Satyaki jumoed down and both started fighting with their swords.

Krishna saw this and asked Arjuna to help Satyaki. He said Bhurishrava has caught Satyaki by his hair and is about to cut off his head.

Arjuna then aimed arrows at Bhurishrava, cutting off his hands.

Janmajeya asked Satyaki was able to defeat all, but how was he easily overcome by Bhurishrava?

## Arjuna goes in search of Jayadratha

Tuesday, 24 January 2017

In the camp Krishna was worried. He said to Arjuna - You took a vow not knowing the consequences.  
Arjuna said What do I have to fear when you are with me.  
Krishna asked Arjuna to come with him and drove the chariot to the abode of Shiva.  
Shiva was pleased to see Krishna. Krishna said Arjuna had taken a vow to kill Jayadratha in the day. Shiva blessed both and said they would be successful.  
Krishna and Arjuna went back to their camp.  
The next day Drona created a wonderful formation which was difficult to penetrate. Jayadratha was placed in the center of that.  
Arjuna said He would fight and kill the king of Sindhu.  
Bhima told Arjuna to concentrate on that while he would take care of the rest.  
Krishna then asked his charioteer, Daruka, to be ready with weapons. He said If Arjuna was unable to kill Jayadratha before sunset, he would blow his conch shell. Hearing that Daruka should come, and then Krishna would kill all the Kauravas alone.  
Krishna then sat on Arjuna's chariot and took that into the battlefield.  
Drona was in the front stopping Arjuna. Arjuna asked him to give way which he refused. Krishna said Do not pelad with one who planned the death of your son.  
Arjuna and Drona started fighting. Arjuna cut off all arrows of Drona. Krishna said quickly deal with Drona. We need to reach Jayadratha in time.  
Arjuna asked how to do that.  
Krishna said On the right of Drona is an army fighting. Kill them off. I will take the chariot there.  
Krishna took the chariot there and Arjuna killed many soldiers. Drona asked Why Arjuna was fleeing. Arjuna said he would deal with the Guru later. Drona smiled and left the path.  
Arjuna then was confronted by Ashwatthama. The son of Dona started shooting arrows at Arjuna. Arjuna cut off the weapons and then his bow and wounded him.  
Karna then faced Arjuna. Karna and Arjuna started fighting, shooting weapons at each other. Karna took got wounded and had to leave. Arjuna then killed multiple soldiers.

It was noon and the sun was blazing.  
Krishna then said to Arjuna We need to stop. The horses are tired and wounded. They need rest and some water. I am sweating away myself.  
Arjuna said This is impossible. I cannot stop now.  
Krishna said Let me get off and get some water.  
Arjuna said If you leave me here, this will be the end.  
Krishna said Your devotion has bound me and I cannot leave. Just step off the chariot and fight, standing in the ground, while I take care of the horses.  
Arjuna stepped off and started fighting. Krishna said the horses need water. Let me do something.  
Saying this, he created a lake there. The horses drank their fill in the lake. Krishna then tied the horses back to the chariot.  
Krishna then asked Arjuna to get on the chariot and started the vehicle again.

## Arjuna vows to kill Jayadratha

Monday, 23 January 2017

Arjuna said to Krishna - I do not see anyone as brave as Abhimanyu.  
Krishna said Do not grieve for one who had gone to heaven. Listen to me. On a tree, birds live in the night and when the day breaks, they forget about the night and go flying in teh world. They dont care which tree they spend the night in.  
Vyasa then came there. Arjuna said He was feeling lost without Abhimanyu. Vyasa said Death was the only truth. When the earth was born, there were people who were living for ever. From the breath of Vishnu was born a woman, She was Death. Vishnu told her take with her, those whose time was up. The woman started doing that.  
Vyasa left.  
Krishna then asked Yudhistira to tell about Abhimanyu's fight. Yudhistira told in detail what had happened starting from Abhimanyu saying he just knew how to enter and also Jayadrata stopping them from entering. He then told about Abhimanyu's fight and death.  
Arjuan hearing this said he would kill Jayadratha. He said the next day either he would kill Jayadratha or kill himself, if he failed.  
Jayadratha was scared when he heard this. He went and told Duryodhana this.  
Duryodhana took Jayadratha to Drona and said Arjuna has vowed to kill Jayadratha. He needs to be saved.  
Drona said Jayadratha need not fear. He would get protection from all. He said he would create a formation and Jayadratha would be in the center of that.  
When Duryodhana left, Kripa said With Krishna on the Pandavas side, know for sure, Jayadratha will meet his end in the fight tomorrow.

## Arjuna sees ill omens

Sunday, 22 January 2017

The sage said to Janmajeya -  
While Arjuna was fighting, he saw crows coming and sitting on the flag posts of the chariots. They were continuously cawing away. The bow string of Gandiva broke once and his left eye started throbbing. He suddenly started sweating.  
Arjuna said to Krishna - Nothing is amiss. I can hear crying noises. I wonder what is happening.  
Krishna said Till the time Abhimanyu is there, there is no fear. If Abhimanyu is killed, that will have to be by unfair means and that will mean Duryodhana's end.

Krishna knew that Abhimanyu had fallen but he could not tell Arjuna that. He started the chariot towards the camp. Arjuna saw more ill omens. He entered the camp.

It was all dark inside and all were sitting on the ground. Arjuna said Why is everyone avoiding me?

He saw Yudhistira crying. He moved around and saw Abhimanyu missing. He asked Bhima where Abhimanyu was. Bhima was quiet.

Arjuna had tears in his eyes. Slowly Bhima told Arjuna about the battle and the death of Abhimanyu.

Arjuna could not control his grief. He started crying, Krishna then consoled him.

## Moon cursed

Saturday, 21 January 2017

The sage said to Janmajeya -  
The Pandavas returned to their camp. Yudhistira sat on the ground, unable to control his grief.  
Vyasa came there. He asked Yudhistira why he was grieving.  
Yudhistira said I am very unfortunate. I wanted my kingdom back and because of that the brave Abhimanyu has fallen today. He had told me that he knew how to enter but did not know how to come out. I had told him I would be behind him, but I was unable to proceed because of Jayadratha. He fought bravely and was killed by unfair means.  
Vyasa said Stop your grief. You have no control over destiny. Let me tell you about his previous birth. He was the Moon born as Subhadra's son because of a curse. Listen, as I tell you more.  
The sage Garga once went to the Moon with his disciples. The Moon was sitting with Rohini, lost in her love, when the sage arrived. Chandra forgot to receive the sage.  
The sage got angry at being ignored. He cursed the moon to be born as a human.  
The Moon said he had not ignored the sage purposefully and begged forgiveness.  
The sage said You will born as the son of Subhadra and Arjuna. You will be a brave warrior and at the age of sixteen you will fight a great battle and will die there.

Vyasa said This is Abhimanyu's destiny. You could not have done anything about this.  
Yudhistira said I am not sure how I will face Arjuna. What will Krishna say? What will I say to Subhadra? How will I pacify Uttara?

Vyasa said One dies when the time is up, not before that. Krishna will understand and he will make Arjuna understand.

Saying this, Vyasa left.

## Abhimanyu falls

Friday, 20 January 2017

The sage said  
Listen Janmajeya - The son of Arjuna, fought valiantly. The formation, which was like a slow blooming lotus, had been penetrated by Abhimanyu. At every petal formation, Abhimanyu killed many people.Bodies were floating in pools of blood. A son of Shakuni came to fight and was killed by Abhimanyu.  
Duryodhana went to Drona and asked him how to stop Abhimanyu. He said You are pleased with the skill of Abhimanyu and that is why you remain quiet.  
Drona got angry. He said No one is able to face the son of Arjuna. He is a great warrior like his father. If Karna had to face defeat in his hands, who do you think and defeat him? If you want to defeat Abhimanyu, then you will have fight using unfair means. Do not forget that this is Krishna's nephew. He will be able to defeat all, alone.  
Duryodhana then gave orders for seven of the Kuru warriors to face Abhimanyu together.  
Kripa said this was not a proper means of fighting.  
Duryodhana said this was the time to win the war. Kill the boy by any means and win the war.  
It was decided. Drona, Kripa, Karna, Shakuni, Dushsasana, Ashwatthama and Duryodhana decided to surround Abhimanyu and attack him.  
The sky was covered with arrows.  
Abhimanyu was aghast seeing this unfair means of fight. Abhimanyu shot arrows cutting off arrows coming towards him. He was easily able to overcome the seven warriors. Duryodhana said to Drona - See this boy make a massacre of my army.  
Drona and the others attacked again. This time they cut his chariot and then his bow. With his bow gone, Abhimanyu jumped with a sword and sheild in his hand. Karna, with an arrow, cut off his shield. Abhimanyu then began fighting with the sword. He began cutting whoever came in front of him. Ashwatthama, with three arrows, cut off the sword. Abhimanyu now realized that there was no way and his end was near. He looked around and could not see any Pandava warrior close by. His father and uncle were far away, fighting.  
He then picked up a wheel of a chariot and using that, began fighting using that.  
Karna, with five arrows, cut off the wheel. He picked up another wheel and used that to avoid the arrows coming at him. Soon, with the arrows hitting him, it looked as if a porcupine was fighting. Abhimanyu was now tired. Seeing this, the warriors attacked again.  
A son of Dushasana then jumped forward with his mace and struck Abhimanyu.  
The blow hit him so hard and that he lost his life. Abhimanyu fell and went over to the land of the moon, Chandraloka, where he had originally come from.  
The Kauravas then started celebrating, playing musical instruments.  
Yudhistira heard this and fainted.  
The sun also set and the day of the war came to an end.

## Abhimanyu fights

Thursday, 19 January 2017

Abhimanyu was inside. Bhima and the other warriors were feeling frustrated. They had been pushed back by Jayadratha. They were worried that Abhimanyu was alone inside.  
Abhimanyu started his attack shooting arrows wherever he went. No one was able to face him.  
Uluka, son of Dushasana, came to face Abhimanyu. He was not able to face Abhimanyu and soon lost his life in his hands.  
Vrishasena, son of Karna, then attacked Abhimanyu but was not ableto stand in front of Abhimanyu for long and had to leave, gravely wounded. Seeing this, Karna got angry., He started shooting arrows at Abhimanyu which the young prince easily cut off. Karna could not also stand in front of Abhimanyu for long and had to flee.  
Lakshmana, son of Duryodhana, then came to fight. Abhimanyu cut off the chariot of Lakshmana. With another arrow he cut off the head of Lakshmana.  
Duryodhana then picked up his mace and jumped to avenge his son's death. Abhimanyu cut off Duryodhana's mace into pieces and then wounded Duryodhana, who had to flee.  
Abhimanyu was on a spree, He kept shooting arrows killing whoever came in front of him. He killed many sons of the Kauravas.

Sanjaya who was telling Dhritarashtra about the battle said The young son of Arjuna, who is just sixteen years of age, is fighting like a lion. The great Kuru warriors are afraid to face him.  
Dhritarashtra said Arjuna's son is truly great.

## Narayani Army enters the battle and Abhimanyu steps in the formation

Wednesday, 18 January 2017

In the camp Duryodhana went to Drona and asked what was the way to win the war. Drona said You got the Narayani army from Krishna. Let them take Arjuna someplace and fight with him. With Arjuna away, Yudhistira can be captured.  
Duryodhana sent for Susharma who was leading the Narayani army and explained the plan to him. He was to take Arjuna northwards.

The battle started. Arjuna was challenged to a fight by Susharma. Arjuna agreed. Susharma thought this was his chance to kill Arjuna. He was happy. Karna and Drona saw Arjuna being led away and they were happy.

Drona said he would form a formation never seen before. Saying this he decided to form the chakravyuha formation. This was a very complex formation and it was very critical for one to knowhow to enter the formation and also how to come out of it. Jayadratha was at the opening of the formation and the other warriors were inside.

Drona saw Yudhistira and began shooting arrows at him. Yudhistira retaliated by shooting arrows back cutting off the horses and killing the charioteer. Drona jumped off and ran to grab Yudhistira. Seeing this, Dhristadyumna came and faced Drona. Yudhistira took shelter with Nakula. So ferocious was the fight that Drona was injured and fainted.

Coming to his senses, Drona then attacked Dhristadyumna, wounding him till he started bleeding.  
Draupadi's brother retaliated and attacked Drona again.  
He then jumped off his chariot and climbed up on Drona's chariot. There he cut off the horses and killed the charioteer.Drona then shot an arrow which pierced Dhristadyumna. The prince fell on the ground. Seeing him fall, the other Pandava warriors started shooting arrows at Drona.

Yudhistira was wondering how to penetrate the formation that Drona had formed. Arjuna was somewhere else, fighting. He then remembered Abhimanyu. He asked Abhimanyu.

Abhimanyu said I know how to get in but I do not know how to come out. Let me tell you how this happened. When I was in my mother's womb, my mother had heard from my father the way to enter the formation but Arjuna had not mentioned the way out. This is the reason I am not aware of the way out.

Yudhistira said Do not worry. When you enter the formation, we will all be behind you. We will all enter the formation with you.

The brave Abhimanyu said he would fight a battle like never seen before and agreed to enter the formation.

Abhimanyu entered the formation shooting arrows everywhere. Jayadratha who was guarding the formation managed to prevent the other Pandavas from entering the formation.

## Arjuna and Bhima create havoc

Tuesday, 17 January 2017

The next day the fight began. Arjuna started showering arrows killing anyone who came in the path. Seeing this Duryodhana climbed on a chariot and came to face Arjuna. He aimed arrows at Arjuna which were cut off. Arjuna then cut off the flag staff, horses and the charioteer. Duryodhana then picked up a mace and flung that at Arjuna. The chariot was hit by the mace and started shaking. Arjuna then shot arrows at Duryodhana, who had to flee.  
Ashwatthama seeing this came to fight Arjuna. Both started shooting arrows at each other. Soon, hit by an arrow of Arjuna, Ashwatthama fainted. The charioteer then fled with the fainted warrior.

Dushsasana and Bhima were fighting. Dushsasana was on an elephant. Bhima attacked the elephant. The elephant fell and Dushsasana fled. Bhima then began killing who came in front. Seeing this, Ashwatthama attacked Bhima. Seeing the son of Drona, Bhima climbed up on his chariot and picked up a bow. Ashwatthama shots arrows at Bhima which caused him to loose his senses. regaining his sense, Bhima flung his mace at the chariot, breaking it. Ashwatthama then jumped onto another chariot and began shooting arrows which were all cut off by Bhima. Bhima then flung his mace, breaking the chariot. Ashwatthama fled. Bhima tried to chase him. Seeing this, Karna stopped Bhima by showering arrows at him. Bhima using his mace crushed Karna's chariot. Karna got another chariot and fled.  
Bhima then attacked the army.  
Arjuna was killing the Kaurava army. Seeing this, Duryodhana went to Drona and said You said you would be capturing Yudhistira. I do not see that happening. On the contrary, I see Bhima and Arjuna making a mess of my army. if Karna had been made the commander, I would have had Yudhistira as my prisoner.  
Drona was angry. He said I had said earlier that I am a brahmin and I should be in the war.  
Saying this, Drona decided to walk away.  
Shakuni then said to Drona - Duryodhana did not mean anything bad.  
Drona was pacified. He said I can capture Yudhistira only if Arjuna is away.

The day ended and all went back to the camp.

## Bhima's fight

Monday, 16 January 2017

Janmajeya said - O sage what you said is very brief. Please tell some more details.  
The sage said -  
Seeing Drona, Arjuna bowed to him and said I have heard you wish to capture Yudhistira. I will not let you do that.  
Drona said I will see if you can stop me.  
Drona then started shooting arrows at Arjuna. Arjuna cut those off. Drona shot more arrows and those were cut off too.  
Arjuna the cut off Drona's bow. Drona picked up another bow and shot the arrow that made everything burn. Arjuna then invoked the water weapon and the fire went out.  
Everywhere there was water now. Drona invoked the wind to dry up the water.  
Angry, Arjuna cut off the horses of the Guru's chariot. He then cut off the flag of the chariot and killed the charioteer. Arjuna then shot an arrow that pierced Drona. Seeing this, some of the Kuru warriors rushed and took Drona away from there.  
Seeing Drona leave, Arjuna started shooting arrows, killing many soldiers.  
Bhima and Duryodhana were fighting each other. They were using their maces and trying to hot each other. The maces were moving at the speed of the wind. Not able to stand the fight, Duryodhana fled from there. Bhima chased him and seeing this, other Kuru warriors started shooting arrows at Bhima. Bhima was on a rampage killing whatever came on his path - whether it was a chariot, horse, elephant or a soldier.  
Duryodhana ordered elephants to be let loose on Bhima. Bhima killed all those elephants. Picking up the elephants by their trunks, Bhima began hurling them in the air. Karna seeing this rushed there. Seeing Karna, Bhima went and killed the horses of the chariot with his fists. He then kicked the chariot which broke. Karna then fled from there.  
Bhima picked up the broken chariot and hurled that killing soldiers with that.  
The sun then set and the day came to an end.

## Battle begins

Sunday, 15 January 2017

The battle began. Drona and Arjuna fought each other. Karna and Satyaki fought each other and Bhima and Duryodhana fought each other., While Nakula fought with Dushsasana, Sahadeva fought with Shakuni. The fight was fierce and rivers of blood were flowing everywhere.

## Duryodhana goes to Bhishma

Saturday, 14 January 2017

Before the war was about to begin, Duryodhana went where Bhishma was lying. He said I have come to take leave of you as I go to fight the Pandavas. Drona has been made the commander.  
Bhishma said Listen to what I say and agree to respect my word. Make friends with Yudhistira. Give Yudhistira something. I will convince Yudhistira to be happy with he has got. Krishna is with the Pandavas and there is no way you can win the war. Ask Drona and see if he agrees to what I say.

Duryodhana was stubborn and refused to accept Bhishma's advice. He said Every one points out at me. What did I do? All I did was find fault with the Pandavas. By strength or by wit, I need to defeat my enemies. If I die in the battlefield, people will consider me a hero. I have made a promise that I will fight and I will not go back on my word.

Bhishma kept quiet knowing that the Kauravas would be destroyed.

Duryodhana left for the battlefield.

## Bhima made commander

Friday, 13 January 2017

The Pandavas were in their camp. They were happy that Bhishma had fallen. They got the news that Drona had been made commander. The messenger also said that Drona had promised that he would capture Yudhistira and make him prisoner.  
This scared Yudhistira who asked Krishna what was the way out.  
Krishna said Do not worry. Even if a hundred Drona come here, they will not be able to capture you.  
Bhima said There was no need to fear.  
Krishna said It was best to make Bhima the commander,

Yudhistira declared Bhima as the commander of the Pandava army.

## Drona made commander

Thursday, 12 January 2017

The sage said to Janmajeya -  
After the fall of Bhishma, Duryodhana was wondering who would lead the army. He asked Karna to become the commander. Karna was ready but Kripacharya said it was not proper to make Karna the commander while Drona was around.  
Duryodhana agreed and declared Drona the commander.  
All the Kuru warriors went to Drona and Duryodhana said he was making the Guru the commander of the Kaurava army.  
Drona said he was ready to accept the role on one condition. Karna would not fight in the battle.  
Duryodhana said Karna will not fight close to you.  
Duryodhana agreed and Drona was made the commander.  
Drona said he would form the spiral formation and trap the Pandavas.

This pleased Duryodhana who announced the commander's name to all.

## Day Ten - Bhishma falls

Wednesday, 11 January 2017

The next day, battle began. Bhishma asked his charioteer to take the chariot near Arjuna. The charioteer said this was not a good day and he was seeing ill omens. It was raining without clouds in the sky. Vultures were flying around, wavering over chariots.  
Bhishma said What ill omen can be there when the Lord himself is the charioteer for Arjuna?  
The charioteer took the chariot near Arjuna. Bhishma aimed arrows at Krishna and the flag staff having Hanumana on it. Arjuna cut off the arrows.  
Bhima killed many kaurava warriors. Dushsasana came and wounded Bhima with arrows. Enraged, Bhima threw his bow and picked up his mace. With a blow, he smashed the chariot killing the charioteer and the horses. Dushsasana fainted and began bleeding. Drona then attacked Bhima, who killed the guru's charioteer and horses. Seeing Bhima wounded, Abhimanyu came to help. He cut off Drona's armor. Drona then recovered and started showering arrows at Abhimanyu.  
At another corner, Drupada, Virata, Dhristadyumna and Ghatotkacha were fighting. They made havoc of the Kaurava army. Seeing this, Bhurishrava, Kritavarma, Shalya and Jayadhrata rushed there.  
Ghatotkacha was making a mess of the army. Seeing this Duryodhana fled. Bhishma stopped the fleeing Duryodhana and asked him to stand and face the battle.  
Bhishma then started showering arrows at Arjuna and Krishna, wounding them and killing the horses. Seeing this Arjuna started shooting arrows randomly killing countless soldiers.  
Krishna decided this was the time to get Shikhandi.  
Shikhandi came and keeping him in front, the battle began. Arjuna said this was not fair. Krishna said It was time to fight.  
Seeing Shikhandi Bhishma dropped his bow. Shikhandi said I will kill you.  
Bhishma said Nature made you a woman and dressed as a man you have joined the Pandavas.  
Shikhandi then started shooting arrows at Bhishma as directed by Arjuna. Arjuna seeing this was not working, picked up his bow and started shooting arrows at Bhishma. Bhishma knew these were not Shikhandi's arrows.  
Soon Bhishma was pierced every where with arrows, bleeding profusely. Arjuna picked up a weapon and shot that. That pierced Bhishma's heart. The old man fell from his chariot.  
His body did not touch the ground. The arrows piercing the ground formed a bed on which Bhishma lay.  
All left the war and rushed to Bhishma's side.  
Duryodhana could not control himself and burst into tears.  
Yudhistira and the Pandavas along with Krishna went to Bhishma. Yudhistira said You were the father to the Pandavas. Today we have killed that father figure.  
Slowly Bhishma said I will live till the sun comes its journey from south (Dakshinayana) to the north (Uttarayana .  
He said to Duryodhana - My head is hanging, please get me a pillow fit for me.  
Duryodhana got the best of the pillows. Bhishma rejected those and asked Arjuna to get a pillow. Arjuna shot three arrows which pierced Bhishma's head and then went into the ground. Bhishma then said he was thirsty. Duryodhana got water but that was rejected. Again Bhishma asked Arjuna.  
Arjuna shot an arrow into the earth. A stream of water burst forth falling in Bhishma's mouth.

Bhishma asked Duryodhana to stop the war but Duryodhana refused. Spending sometime with Bhishma, everyone left for their camps.

## Yudhistira approaches Bhishma

Tuesday, 10 January 2017

In the camp, Yudhistira was wondering how Bhishma could be finished. Krishna said Your brothers are fighting bravely, so why are you so worried?Arjuna will be able to kill Bhishma. Do not worry on that.  
Yudhistira said I can understand that but I want to know how Bhishma will be killed.  
Krishna said Bhishma is a noble person. Let us go to him and ask him. He has a boon of dying only when he wishes to. He will be able to answer your question.  
With the Pandavas, Krishna went to Bhishma. Bhishma greeted them and the five brothers bowed down to Bhishma. Bhishma wanted to know why they had come at that hour.  
Yudhistira said When I asked for five village that was denied. The war started and now no one is able to face you. I now ask you, please let me know how can you be killed?  
Bhishma said Listen. I am known for my skill and strength. No one defeat me as long as I hold my weapons in my hands. As long as I am in the field, I will serve the Kauravas. if you kill me, know for sure, it will be your victory and the defeat of the Kauravas. I will not lift a weapon at some fleeing or someone weak or sick. I will not lift a weapon at a woman. Anyone who has a name of a woman will not be hurt by me. Listen to me. Drupada's son Shikhandi was born a woman and now as a man is skilled in warfare. If Shikhandi comes in front, I will drop my weapons. Arjuna can use Shikhandi as a sheild and kill me.  
Yudhistira, Krishna and the Pandavas took leave of Bhishma and went back to their camp.

## Day Nine

Monday, 9 January 2017

Yudhistira was sad. Bhishma was fighting in full form and Krishna's vow had been broken.  
Drupada said The Lord will bend down to please his devotee.  
This talking the night ended and the day started and battle began.  
Arjuna killed many soldiers and so did Bhima. Rivers of blood were flowing in the ground and in that dead animals and soldiers were floating.  
Bhishma killed many of the Pandava soldiers. Foot soldiers, horses and elephants fell. Bhima shot arrows at Duryodhana, wounding him. Duryodhana picked up his mace and jumped off his chariot. He killed Bhima's charioteer with a blow of his mace. Bhima cut off the mace with his arrows.  
Drona attacked Bhima cutting off his bow. Bhima picked another bow. Before he could string the bow, Drona cut that too. Angry, Bhima picked his mace. Drona tried to cut the mace, but before that Bhima smashed Drona's chariot. Drona jumped on another chariot and started showering arrows at Bhima. Bhima killed the charioteer. Drona, with sharp arrows, cut off the mace. Angry Bhima picked up the chariot. Drona fell on the ground. Drona then rushed towards Bhima and both started wrestling.  
With no one winning, Drona climbed on a chariot and started aiming arrows at Bhima. Bhima then got on his chariot and cut off Drona's bow again.  
Abhimanyu was killing many soldiers. Kripacharya attacked Abhimanyu. Abhimanyu cut off Kripa's arrows. Kripa, taking divine weapons, cut off Abhimanyu's bow. Taking another bow, Abhimanyu killed the horses and charioteer of Kripa. With two arrows, Abhimanyu pierced Kripa's armor. Kripa fainted.  
Ashwatthama saw this and came to Kripa's rescue. Abhimanyu cut off his bow. Abhimanyu cut off all weapons of the son of Drona.  
Arjuna and Bhishma were fighting. Arjuna cut off all the divine weapons that Bhishma tried to use. Arjuna then cut off Bhishma's bow. He then pierced Bhishma's armor. Then he began busy, killing soldiers.  
Bhishma recovering shot arrows wounding both Arjuna and Krishna.  
With evening, the day came to an end.

## Krishna breaks his vow

Sunday, 8 January 2017

Seeing the havoc being created by Bhima, Kirpacharya came to face Bhima. Kripa cut off Bhima's bow. Bhima picked up another bow and showered arrows on Kripa.  
Satyaki was fighting Bhurisharava and Abhimayu was fighting with Susharma. Ghatotkacha was fighting Alambush and Drupada and Ashwatthama were fighting each other. Yudhistira and Shalya were fighting while Nakula and Dushshasana were fighting. None was able to win over the other. Sahadeva was fighting Shakuni. Shakuni had to flee, seeing Sahadeva's fighting skills.  
Arjuna and Bhishma's war was something to see. The Gods had gathered in the skies to see the battle. Bhishma shot arrows at Arjuna, who cut those. Arjuna then cut off Bhishma's bow, who picked up another one. Bhishma then started aiming arrows at Krishna. Arjuna seeing this, cut off Bhishma's bow. Bhishma picked up another bow and Arjuna cut that too.  
Krishna was wounded by Bhishma's arrows. He knew what Bhishma wanted. Bhishma and Arjuna were fighting each killing many soldiers in the other's army.  
Arjuna was getting tired trying to fight off Bhishma's arrows.  
Krishna becaem angry. He said he would now kill off all the Kauravas. He said I had made a promise that I will not lift a weapon. If I try to fulfill my promise, the Pandavas will be finished.  
Krishna jumped off the chariot and picked up a wheel of a chariot and went to kill Bhishma with that.  
Bhishma dropped his weapons and said Come O Lord and quickly make an end of me.  
Seeing Krishna angry, Arjuna jumped off the chariot and caught Kirshna's hands. He said I had promised to kill Bhishma. With your grace I will fulfill that. Please return.

Krishna returned and resumed his role as the charioteer. Arjuna using divine weapons killed off many soldiers. Bhishma tried to attack but his arrows got cut off. Angry, he began killing soldiers.

The day then ended.

## Day Eight

Saturday, 7 January 2017

The day began. Duryodhana was sad about being tricked. Arjuna faced Bhishma who said Let me see your skill today. Taking the crown of Duryodhana, you tricked me. Now listen to me, I will make Krishna break his vow. He will pick up a weapon.  
Gods wondered what would happen. Krishna had vowed not to touch a weapon and here Bhishma had made a promise. Let us see who will break his vow.  
Bhishma fought furiously. He cut off all Arjuna's arrows.  
Drona and Dhristadyumna were fighting. The son of Drupada was cutting off all of Drona's arrows. Drona cut off Dhristadyumna's bow. Dhristadyumna then picked up a mace and flung it at Drona who cut that off. Drona then cut the chariot of the prince. Dhristadyumna then picked up a sword and rushed at Drona. He cut off the horses of the chariot. Using five arrows, Drona cut the sword. Dhristadyumna then climbed up on the chariot of Abhimanyu.  
Bhima and Duryodhana were fighting each other with their maces. Wounded, Duryodhana climbed on his chariot and began shooting arrows at Bhima. Bhima seeing this, also climbed up on his chariot. Bhima cut off Duryodhana's bow. Duryodhana picked up another bow. Bhima cut that off too. Seeing this many warriors came to rescue Bhima and all of them met their end in the hands of Bhima.

## Krishna tricks Bhishma

Friday, 6 January 2017

In the evening, in the camp, Duryodhana went to Bhishma. He said You are one whom even Parashurama could not defeat. You alone can destroy the three worlds. How then do the Pandavas go back to their camps alive?  
Bhishma said He would kill the Pandavas the next day unless Krishna did something.  
Duryodhana was pleased and went back happy.  
Sahadeva knew about this. When Krishna was discussing the plan for the next day, Sahadeva said Bhishma took five arrows from his quiver and said he would kill the five Pandava brothers with those.  
This scared Yudhistira. He said Bhishma is known for his promises and his word. How will we survive the day?  
Krishna said Do not worry. Give me Arjuna. We will go to the Kaurava camp and get the five arrows. That will end the story there.  
Yudhistira said How will that be possible?  
Krishna said Think back. When you were in the forest, Duryodhana had come to the forest to show off his wealth and make you jealous. He had left Bhishma and Drona in Hastinapura. There he along with Karna had been made prisoner by Chitraratha. There Arjuna had gone and rescued them. Duryodhana had then said Ask for something. Arjuna had said I will ask for it when the time comes.

Krishna and Arjuna went to the Kaurava camp. Krishna stood outside and asked Arjuna to go inside, remind Duryodhana about the promise and ask for his crown. Then with that, go to Bhishma and get the five arrows.

Duryodhana was told Arjuna had come. He welcomed Arjuna and made him sit. Arjuna reminded Duryodhana about the promise and asked for the crown, Duryodhana took that off and gave it to him.

Placing the crown on his head, Arjuna went to Bhishma's camp. Bhishma was able to make out the crown and asked why Duryodhana had come to him at that hour. Arjuna said Give me the five arrows. I will use those myself. Bhishma handed those over. The moment he did that, Krishna came inside. Bhishma knew immediately that he had been tricked.

## Day Seven

Thursday, 5 January 2017

The battle resumed at dawn. Bhishma and Arjuna kept fighting. Bhima created a havoc with his mace. Ashwatthama attacked Bhima. Bhima climbed on his chariot. Bhima cut off the arrows coming towards him. Bhima then wounded Ashwatthama who fainted. Seeing this, Kalinga, a brother of Duryodhana, came to fight Bhima. He could not withstand Bhima's attack and fainted. Other brothers came to fight, seeing this but met their end at the hands of Bhima. Kalinga seeing his brothers dead, challenged Bhima.  
Bhima flung his mace. Krishna added power to the mace, and soon elephants and soldierswere flying in the air, as the mace went through the army. Many died there. Soon Bhima killed Kalinga also.  
Drona, seeing this, fired arrows at Bhima, wounding him. Bhima then lept onto his chariot and picked up his bow. Soon Bhima and Drona began fighting, showering arrows at each other.  
Nakula and Jayadhrata were fighting. Sahadeva and Shakuni were fighting. Sahadeva wounded Shakuni who fainted and was picked up brought to safety by his army.  
Ashwatthama and Abhimanyu were fighting.  
Krishna took the chariot near Bhishma. Arjuna and Bhishma were fighting each other when the day came to an end.

## Hanumana and Arjuna

Wednesday, 4 January 2017

In the camp, Yudhistira was worried with Bhishma killing off people.  
Arjuna asked him to be optimistic. He said - When I was in exile, I went to the beautiful city of Dwaraka. There Satyambhama was a given a lotus by Krishna which angered Rukmini. Knowing this, Krishna asked me to get another lotus. I asked where I would get one. Krishna told me the flower was in a banana grove. I went there with my weapons and saw the place guarded by vanaras. They tried to stop me when I tried to pluck the flowers. They reported this to Hanumana. Hanumana was offended. Why had Arjuna come and disturbed him, when he was praying to Rama? He decided to come and see for himself. I said it was because of the vanaras that the ocean was crossed by Rama. If Rama was brave, he could have bridged the ocean with his arrows. This angered Hanumana. He said Rama had broken the bow of Shiva which no one else could do. If Rama had to do this, what would we have done. Saying this, he said If you are that skilled, bridge the ocean with your arrows. If the bridge can stand my weight, then I will accept you as a brave skilled person.  
We went to the ocean shore. I bridged the ocean. Hanumana increased his size. So huge was he, that the sun was hidden. I called to Krishna for help. Taking the form of a tortoise, Krishna, shid below the bridge. The moment stepped on the bridge, the tortoise started bleeding from its mouth and the water of the ocean became red. Hanumana was wondering what had happened. He closed his eyes and saw Rama as the tortoise. He begged forgiveness for having stepped on his Lord.  
Krishna came out and then assumed the form of Rama. He said the two of you are my greatest devotees. Saying this, Krishna left.  
Hanumana said he would help whenever needed. Saying this,he too left. I returned with the flowers.  
Do not worry. With Krishna on our side, we will win.

## Day Six

Tuesday, 3 January 2017

The day started with the armies fighting. Karna was creating havoc with his mace. Sahadeva started his fight, killing many soldiers. Shakuni aimed a sword at Sahadeva, who cut that off. All arrows of Shakuni were cut off by Sahadeva. Shakuni fled.  
Nakula and Jayadhrata were fighting. Dhristadyumna and Bhurisrava were fighting.  
Arjuna and Drona began fighting. Seeing this, Abhimanyu joined in. He cut off Drona's bow. When Drona picked another bow, Abhimanyu cut that too. Whatever weapons Drona used were cut off by Abhimanyu.  
Krishna took the chariot towards Bhishma where Arjuna attacked the grand sire. It was a severe combat with each cutting off the other's arrows. Arjuna cut off the flag staff of the chariot and killed the charioteer and the horses of Bhishma's chariot.  
Bhishma then picked up a weapon called Narayana and invoked the power by reciting the mantras. When the weapon was released, the earth started shaking. Krishna told Arjuna - drop your weapons on the chariot and sit down. Arjuna said this was not his duty. His duty was to fight.  
Krishna said, this was not the time for words. Arjuna dropped his weapons seeing which the other Pandava warriors and their allies did the same. Bhima still had his mace in hand. Krishna said Immediately drop the mace. Bhima refused and said he would rather die.  
The weapon saw only Bhima with a weapon and came towards him. Krishna seeing this went towards Bhima and stood in front of him. The weapon came and struck Krishna. The weapon, on touching Krishna, lots all its power.  
Bhishma seeing this started praising Krishna.  
The war resumed.with Bhishma wounding Arjuna.  
Soon it was evening and the war ended.

## Duryodhana, Karna and Bhishma talk

Monday, 2 January 2017

In their camp Duryodhana sent for Karna. He and Shakuni spoke to Karna. Duryodhana said I made Bhishma the commander thinking the Pandavas would be finished but now it does not seem to be working.  
Karna said Bhishma and Drona have a soft corner for the Pandavas. Whatever you do, think carefully before that. Let us go and talk to Bhishma.  
Duryodhana went to Bhishma and said You said you will make an end of the Pandavas. I do not see that happening. It would have been better if Karna had been the commander.  
Bhishma became angry. He said When I tried to tell you something, you called me old. What did Karna do when the Gandharvas bound you? When you stole the cows of Matsya, where was Karna? Arjuna and Krishna together are invincible. Listen to me. In the house of Nanda, when Krishna was growing up, he used to play with boys of his age. The sages who used to pray to Indra, stopped praying to him and began praising Krishna. Angry, Indra created a great storm. Not knowing what to do, the sages and people of the city went to Krishna for help. Krishna lifted the mountain Govardhan and the people took shelter under the mountain. For seven days, Krishna stood with the mountain on his hand. Who can destroy the ones who have Krishna to help them?  
I will try my best tomorrow.

## Day Five

Sunday, 1 January 2017

Battle began at dawn. While the kuru army used a formation like an ocean, the Pandavas used a horned formation.  
Many horses, elephants and soldiers were killed.  
Bhishma was on a rampage. Seeing this, Bhima attacked Bhishma. Bhima avoiding the arrows with his mace, killed the charioteer and horses of Bhishma's chariot. Bhishma climbed onto another chariot and started a fresh attack. Avoiding Bhima, Bhishma went where Arjuna was. Krishna also took the chariot near Bhishma. While Arjuna and Bhishma fought, Bhima, Nakula and Sahadeva killed many Kaurava soldiers. Ilavanta, son of Arjuna came to fight there.  
When Arjuna was in exile, he had been captured by Uloopi, daughter of a Naga. This was their son. Ilavanta had grown up in heaven, learning under Indra. When Arjuna had gone to the heaven, he had met his son there.  
The sons of Shakuni attacked Ilavanta. Ilavanta killed them. Seeing this, Duryodhana came to fight. Seeing no respite, he asked the demon Alambusha to attack.  
Ilavanta fought valiantly, combating Alambusha. Alambusha fainted and Ilavanta kept killing the soldiers of the Kauravas. Seeing his army getting killed, Duryodhana attacked. Duryodhana had to flee, seeing the might of the young man. Ilavanta then cut off the weapons of all the kings who came to help Duryodhana.  
Alambusha coming to his senses attacked Ilavanta again. The two fought with each other, sometimes with arrows, sometimes with swords.  
Alambusha hid behind the clouds and Ilavanta caught him. Soon Alambusha caught up with Ilavanta and struck him with his sword.  
Ilavanta fell dead. Seeing this, Ghatotkacha attacked Alambusha.  
Hearing off the death of his son, Arjuna attacked whoever came in front of him. Bhishma cut off his arrows. Arjuna used the fire arrow to burn Bhishma's chariot. Bhishma invoked water and the fire went out. Arjuna then used the wind to evaporate the water.  
The fight between Arjuna and Bhishma continued till it was the end of the day.

### 2016

## December

## Drupada talks to Yudhistira

Saturday, 31 December 2016

In the camp, Yudhistira said to Krishna - Bhishma is making a massacre of our soldiers.  
Hearing this Drupada said - You are forgetting the past. When you were in Varnavrata in the house of Lac, you had been visited by a hunter woman and her five sons. Her husband had tried to kill a pregnant deer, but the deer had called out to Krishna for help, and the hunter had perished. Not knowing where to go, his wife and five sons had come to your house. When the house was set on fire by you, the hunter woman and her sons died. When you had escaped, Bhima had realized he had left his mace behind. He came to collect it and the flames of fire tried to make a meal of Bhima. Bhima had said he would give 100 princes as sacrifice and the fire had left Bhima. Krishna had taken the heat of the fire on him so that you could be saved. When Rukmini asked Krishna, the cause of the burn, Krishna had said this was for the welfare of mankind.  
With Krishna with you, you cannot loose this battle.

## Day Four

Friday, 30 December 2016

Every one accumulated in their camps and began discussing. The night thus ended and the new day began.  
Krishna took the chariot in front of Bhishma.  
Bhima was fighting Susharma. Bhima killed many Kaurava soldiers. Susharma showered arrows at Bhima which Bhima dodged using his mace. Susharma started fleeing. Seeing this Duryodhana attacked Bhima. Duryodhana showered arrows which Bhima cut off. Bhima then shot arrows at Duryodhana. Duryodhana cut off Bhima's bow. Bhima took another bow and cut off Duryodhana's bow. Bhima cut that off. This happened many times with bows getting cut off. Bhishma seeing this asked some of the kings to go and help Duryodhana.  
Bhima was attacked but he managed to cut off the weapons coming towards him. Bhagdatta attacked Bhima and managed to control him for sometime, till Bhima cut off his bow. Ghatotkacha saw this and came to Bhima's side. Bhagdatta's elephant was killed. Alambish came to fight. Both Ghatokacha and Ambalush used magic on each other.  
Arjuna and Bhishma kept fighting.  
The day ended.

## Day Three

Thursday, 29 December 2016

Having bathed and changed, Yudhistira sat in his camp. He was trying to console Virata who had lost 2 sons in a day.  
The next day the Kaurava army created a Garuda formation with Bhishma at the tip. Drona and Kritavarma were the eyes.  
The Pandavas formed a crescent formation.  
When the two armies clashed, the noise was so great that it was very difficult to hear any other sound. Like rain, the sky was overcast with arrows flying around.  
Arjuna along with Ghatotkacha and Abhimanyu killed many Kaurava warriors.  
Bhima and Duryodhana fought with bow and arrows and at one time Duryodhana fainted.  
Coming to his sense he asked Drona and Bhishma to concentrate on the battle. This angered them and they said - When we tried to talk to sense to you, you refused to listen. Now, let us handle things ourselves.As a king you need to be responsible for your people.  
Bhishma started fighting again. It was difficult to stand his might.  
Arjuna got showered by arrows from Bhishma. Using divine weapons, Arjuna cut off Bhishma's weapons. Arjuna cut off the flag staff on Bhishma's chariot and cut off his bow. Arjuna then killed Bhishma's charioteer. Bhishma took another bow. The sun was hidden with the arrows flying around.  
The day's fight ended.

## Day Two

Wednesday, 28 December 2016

In the camp, all changed from their armor into their regular clothes. All were talking about Bhishma having killed ten thousand soldiers. Yudhistira asked how Bhishma could be controlled. Krishna said make the son of Virata the commander. Arjuna will kill the Kaurava soldiers.  
Krishna called Virata and said Make your son Sankha the commander of the Pandava army.  
Virata was happy to hear this. He called Sankha and gave him the news. Sankha said he would gladly perform the duties but he did not have an able charioteer. Krishna asked Satyaki to become the charioteer of Sankha.

When dawn broke, armies marched forward. Seeing Sankha, Bhishma blew his conch shell. Sankha showered arrows on Bhishma who cut off the arrows and wounded Sankha. Sankha fainted and Satyaki carried the chariot away.  
Arjuna killed many Kaurava soldiers. Seeing this, Duryodhana decided to face Arjuna but could not stand for long and had to flee. Seeing Arjuna kill the soldiers, Bhishma came to attack Arjuna. The two, equal in might, began showering arrows at each other.  
Bhima also began attacking and killing the Kaurava soldiers. Seeing this, Karna decided to attack Bhima.  
Seeing Salya, Uttar attacked him. Salya was furious seeing Uttar's skills, and with a weapon killed Uttar. Seeing his son fall, Virata attacked Salya.  
Ghatotkacha and Ambulish were fighting.  
Sankha attacked the Kaurava soldiers. Drona seeing him came to face Sankha. Drona showered ten arrows at Sankha, who cut those off. Sankha then cut off Drona's bow. When Drona tried to take another bow, Sankha cut that off too. Drona furious picked up a weapon of Brahma.  
Satyaki asked Sankha to take refuge under Arjuna. Sankha decided to face Drona and not flee.  
Satyaki decided to turn the chariot and seeing that Sankha decided to jump off and face the weapon. He tried cutting that off but was unable to. The weapon came and killed Sankha.

The second day of the battle came to an end.

## Day One

Tuesday, 27 December 2016

A great noise was created with the conch shells blowing. Krishna told Arjuna -Bhishma should be our target.  
Arjuna approached Bhishma and took his blessings.  
Arjuna said to Bhishma - Why are you decked up as a warrior at this age?  
Bhishma said That was his duty.  
Bhishma said Glory to Kunti and Pandu whose son has managed to get the Lord as his charioteer.

Bhishma then began showering arrows at Arjuna who cut those off.  
Bhima was fighting Duryodhana and Satyaki was fighting Kritavarma. Somdutta fought Uttar and Dhristadyumna was fighting Drona. Abhimanyu was fighting Karna. Yudhistira was fighting Salya. Salya cut off his bow. Yudhistira took another bow and cut off Salya's bow.  
Abhimanyu and Ghatotkacha together were killing off the Kaurava army. This angered Bhishma.

Abhimanyu stood like a wall. He cut off the flag post on Salya's chariot and then cut off the arrows of Kripa. Attacking Kritavarma, he cut off his chariot, killing the horses and the charioteer. He then cut off the flag post on Bhishma's chariot. Bhishma, angry started showering arrows, wounding Abhimanyu. Seeing this, some of the Pandava warriors rushed to his rescue. They started showering arrows on Bhishma who cut them off. Arjuna seeing this attacked Bhishma. Bhishma picked a weapon given by Parashurama and hurled that. Seeing that Krishna said, Use a powerful weapon. Arjuna used a weapon given by Indra, which cut off Bhishma's weapon.

Bhima on the meanwhile killed many Kaurava soldiers.

The first day's battle got over.

## Gita

Monday, 26 December 2016

Bhishma said I will fight for ten days. In those ten days I will not leave anyone, even if Krishna and Arjuna come.  
Duryodhana was happy.

At the start, Krishna, holding his conch shell, Panchajanya, in both hands, blew it loudly. Pandavas blew their own conch shells.  
Arjuna asked Krishna, Whom should we attack first?  
Krishna took the chariot in the middle of the ground. Arjuna saw all his relatives there. He was not sure how he would be able to kill them. Gandiva fell from his hands. He said to Krishna I see all my dear ones here. How will I fight them? Saying this, Arjuna sat down.  
Krishna said This is your duty to fight. Who can kill whom? All is my doing? I am the One who is doing this. You talk of death. What is death apart from the soul leaving an old vessel and taking a new one. Soul is invincible, body gets destroyed.  
I am the principal among all. All are in Me and I am in all.

Krishna and Arjuna spoke a lot which became known as Bhagwad Gita. Krishna explained the different yogas and about the philosophy of life.

Arjuna said I want to see your true form.  
Arjuna saw the Great form of Krishna with the entire Universe in Him.

Arjuna picked up his bow.

## Yudhistira seeks blessings

Sunday, 25 December 2016

Janmajeya asked the sage - Hearing Uluka's message, what did Duryodhana do? What did the Pandavas do?  
Vaishampayana said -  
Seeing a good date, the armies began marching towards the battle field, with Krishna leading. They were joined by Sahadeva, son of Jarasandha and other great warriors.  
Duryodhana then asked Dushshana to also march forth. A strong wind started blowing and it started raining. Owls started hooting in the daylight.  
Vidura told Dhritarashtra about this. The blind King said the days for the end of the clan had come.  
Vyasa came there. Dhritarashtra said to the sage - My son did not listen to anyone and now the war is going to start.  
Vyasa said Know for sure that Kurus will be destroyed. Your hundred sons will die.  
Dhritarashtra said How will I know what is happening in the battle field.  
Vyasa said Sanajaya will get the vision. He will narrate to you what is happening in the battlefield. You will hear what is happening in the field. Comets are appearing in the day. Mountains are crashing down. The clan is doomed.  
Saying this, Vyasa left.  
Bhishma became the commander of the Kaurava army. He said he would not use unfair means. He would also not kill anyone who took his refuge.  
When Yudhistira saw Bhishma as the commander, he was scared. He said to Krishna - Even Parashurama had to face defeat in front of Bhishma. Also warriors like Drona cannot be defeated. Who will defeat them?  
Arjuna said Why do you fear? The lord of the world is my charioteer.  
Yudhistira hearing this, calming down, went towards the Kaurava army.  
Bhima and Arjuna wondered what was happening.  
Krishna said Yudhistira is noble. He considers all his own. Do not worry.

Yudhistira went and bowed down before Bhishma, Drona and Kripa. They blessed him. They said Yudhistira had nothing to fear. With Krishna on his side, he would achieve victory.  
Yudhistira then said If there is anyone in the Kaurava army who wants to join the Pandavas, he is most welcome.

Yuyutsu decided to join the Pandavas.

Duryodhana wanted to know, what was happening. Why was Yuyutsu joining the Pandavas?  
Bhishma said Yudhistira asked for my blessings and asked if anyone wanted to join the Pandavas, hearing which Yuyutsu went there.  
Duryodhana asked Please tell me about the great archers and their skills.  
Bhishma said I can alone finish off the army in one day. Drona can do it three days, Karna can do it in five days and Arjuna can do this task in a moment.

Duryodhana then said How will we defeat Arjuna then?

## Kunti meets Karna

Saturday, 24 December 2016

Janmajeya asked the sage - Please tell me what was Kunti's reaction when she learnt that Karna was with Kaurava army?  
The sage said -  
Kunti heard that Karna was with Duryodhana. She decided to go and meet Karna. Kunti knew that Karna went for the banks of Yamuna. He went there alone and never took anyone with him. Kunti went there early morning.  
Karna, when he came out of the river after praying to the Sun saw Kunti.  
Kunti said Know that you are my son, born off the Sun when I was unmarried. I had a boon from Durvasa and when you were born, I had put you in the waters of Yamuna. You are not the son of Radha. Pandavas are your brothers. Join them.  
Karna said I know what you told me, Duryodhana gave an identity and I cannot leave him for that. My fight will be with Arjuna and either Arjuna will die in my hands or I will die in Arjuna's hands. I can promise you that you will have five sons after the war. I cannot give you anything else, Mother.

Kunti left after this. She went and told Vidura this.

## Uluka goes to the Pandavas

Friday, 23 December 2016

Uluka went to the Pandava camp and gave his message. This angered the Pandavas. They said - Tell Duryodhana that he is like the wicked bird. There was a bird who was wicked. The other birds realizing that killed him. Duryodhana will meet a similar fate.  
Bhima said I will break Duryodhana's thigh.  
Arjuna twang his bow. The noise scared Uluka.  
Krishna said Think what Arjuna can do with his bow.

Uluka went and told Duryodhana about the might of the Pandavas. Duryodhana told his army not to worry.

## The False Ascetic

Thursday, 22 December 2016

Duryodhana got the news of the Pandavas being in Kurukshetra. He too started for the battle field. Dhritarashtra was angry hearing this. He knew what would happen. Not knowing what to do, he blessed his son and let him go. Duryodhana took leave of Gandhari who told him to reconsider his decision.

When he started, the sky became red. Comets were seen. Ignoring these signs of ill omen he proceeded.

He then called Uluka and asked him to go to the Pandava camp and convey the details of theKaurava army to them. He said Tell them not to act like the holy cat.

Uluka asked about the holy cat.

Duryodhana said  
There was a brahamana who had no children. When he became old, he and his wife retired to the Himalayas. One day when the brahamanahad gone to look for food, he saw a kitten on the road. The kitten was weak and unable to move. Taking pity, the brahamanatook the kitten with him. Having someone to look after, the brahamanaand his wife returned back to the city.  
The kitten, having grown up, began robbing houses for food. The people in the city were angry and started cursing the brahamana.  
Angry the brahmana tied up the cat. The cat thought if he could free himself, he will become an ascetic. He cut off the bonds with his teeth. He ran off to the forest and began praying there. Soon he became famous as the ascetic cat.  
The mice, seeing the cat, started fleeing but the cat said he had given up violence. The mice believed him and started going near him. Soon they began leaving their children with the cat when they went for food.  
The cat unable to control his greed, soon began eating up the small mice.  
The mice realized their children were vanishing. One old mouse, who was wise, said the cat was a false mendicant. They dug a deep pit and pushed the cat there, where it died.

Finishing his tale, Duryodhana said Yudhistira is like the cat. He will soon loose his life in my hands.

## Nahusha cursed

Wednesday, 21 December 2016

Brihaspati said Let Sachi ask for sometime from Nahusha within which something could be done. Sachi, as per plan, went to Nahusha and said she would come to him later. Nahusha was smitten by lust seeing Sachi. He wanted her to accept him then, but Sachi asked for time again. She said she wanted to know where Indra was and wanted to find him. Once she was convinced that she would never find Indra, she would come to him.

Nahusha agreed to this. Sachi returned to Brihaspati. The Gods went to Vishnu. They said Indra was hiding because of the sin. Vishnu said - Let Indra offer me a sacrifice. I will liberate him from the sin. Indra will have to perform the horse sacrifice and this will also mark of end of Nahusha's rule. Be patient till then.

The Gods went and gave the message to Indra. Indra performed the sacrifice and was liberated. He then went to face Nahusha. Indra trembling the moment he saw Nahusha. He fled again and hid somewhere.

Sachi appealed to the Goddess of the night, Ratri, to help her. When Ratri appeared before her, Sachi said she wanted to know where Indra was hiding. Ratri agreed to help her. Ratri asked Sachi to follow her. They crossed many fields, many mountains and when they came to the sea, they stopped at an island in the middle of the sea. It was a beautiful island, where birds were nesting. Colorful lotus flowers blommed there. In the middle was a large white lotus with a thick stem. Ratri pierced the stem of the flower, and Sachi saw Indra there.

Seeing Indra in a minute form, Sachi too reduced her size and went to Indra. She told Indra all about Nahusha. Sachi asked Indra to come and fight Nahusha. Indra said at this moment, the battle had to fought with the mind and not weapons. He said he had a plan and told Sachi what to do.

Sachi returned. She then asked to meet Nahusha. When Nahusha came, Sachi said - I had asked for time. Now my time is over. I want to you to come to me in an unique vehicle. Indra had horses and elephants. I want you to come in a palanquin borne by the great sages.

Nahusha was pleased. He agreed to this. He said would come to her in a palanquin borne by the seven great sages.

Brihaspati, in the meantime , started a fire and started chanting mantras to find out where Indra was. Agni appeared before Brihaspati and the priest said he wanted to know where Indra was.

Agni assumed the form of a maiden and went around and soon came back to Brihaspati. He said he had searched everywhere except in the waters and Indra was not found. Brihaspati gave Agni the power to travel in water. Agni went again and this time Indra was found in the lotus stem. Agni came and informed Brihaspati.

The Gods and Brihaspati then went to Indra and asked him to return.

While Indra was thinking what to do, Agastya appeared there. He said he had helped in getting rid of Nahusha. All asked him what he had done. Agastya said -

Nahusha had become proud and haughty. He argued with the sages bearing the palanquin and had in the process, kicked Agastya. Angry, Agastya had cursed Nahusha to be turned into a snake.

Indra then returned back to his kingdom and regained his throne.

End his tale, Shalya said - Even Indra had to suffer. Your enemies will soon be gone.

Yudhistira repeated his request of asking Shalya to become the charioteer of Karna. Shalya agreed to that.

Saying this, Shalya returned back.

## Nahusha

Wednesday, 21 December 2016  
Indra was happy having killed Vritra but was still sad as he had killed a brahman, the three headed son of Twasta. Full of guilt, he went into hiding.

As soon as Indra vanished, the earth went into turmoil. Trees began falling, rivers became dry and animals and people were in distress. The Gods and sages were afraid. The universe had no ruler. The great sages suggested that Nahusha be invited from earth and he be made king.

Nahusha, in all humility said he was not the right person for this. The sages and the Gods said they felt he was the best choice. They said they would guide Nahusha. They said fear would be there when doing something different, but that would have to be overcome.

Nahusha became the king, but the heavenly pleasures took over him. He began enjoying the company of the apsaras. He enjoyed the beautiful breeze that blew and the songs that the bards sang.

One day Nahusha saw, Sachi, queen of Indra and wanted her for himself. He asked her to come and visit him. Sachi went the priest, Brihaspati, for advise. The priest asked her to stay with him, promising her that she would would soon be united with Indra.

When Nahusha heard that Sachi was with Brihaspati, he was enraged. How could Sachi refuse the king of heaven? Seeing him angry, the Gods and sages asked Nahusha to control his anger and be happy with what he had. Taking another's wife was a sin.  
Nahusha said - If this is a sin, then why was Indra not prevented when he tried to rape Ahalya?

The Gods said they would bring Sachi to him.

They went to Brihaspati and asked him to help. Brihaspati said he would help the one who had sought his protection. The Gods then sat with Brihaspati and decided on the course of action.

## Indra kills Vritra

Wednesday, 21 December 2016

Once Twasta, a great celestial, was engaged in austerities. He always hated Indra and wanted a son who was greater than Indra. He created a person with three heads. The heads were like the sun, moon and fire. From one head he recited the Vedas, from the second he drank nectar and the third he used to gain knowledge. He began performing austerities.

Indra was scared seeing this. He sent apsaras to entice the being. The person was unmoved. The apsaras went back to Indra. Indra then hurled his thunderbolt and killed the person.

On the other end, Indra saw that the dead person's head was like it had life. The eyes seems to follow Indra wherever he went. Indra saw a carpenter passing by. He asked the carpenter to cut off the heads from the body. The carpenter said his axe would break. Indra gave power to the axe. The carpentyer felt suspicios. Who are you, he asked, and why do you want me to do this?  
Indra said - I am Indra. I have killed my enemy and I want you to cut off his head. Help me, and I will help you in return.  
The carpenter said - You have killed a brahmana. You should be ashamed.  
Indra said - I will perform some rites to wash off my sins.

The carpenter cut off the heads of the three headed person. From the cut heads, birds emerged and began flying.

Twasta was furious. How could his son, who had not committed any crime, be killed? He decided to create Vritra. Let Indra face Vritra. Saying this, he created a terrible asura called Vritra. Vritra was commanded to put an end to Indra.

Vritra went, picked up Indra and swallowed him whole. The Gods saw this. They made Vritra yawn. The moment Vritra opened his mouth, Indra escaped.

Fight began between Indra and Vritra. When Vritra got an upper hand in the fight, Indra left the battle and fled. The Gods went to Vishnu for help.

Indra joined the Gods. The Gods said to Vishnu - You have helped us previously in the time of need. We need your help now. Vritra is troubling us and qwe need your help to defeat him.  
Vishnu said he would enter Indra's thunderbolt and with Vritra would be killed. Before that, a truce needed to be formed between Indra and Vritra. Then seeing a right time, he could be killed.  
started talking.  
The help of the sages was sought. They mediated and Indra and Vritra. Indra asked Vritra how he could be killed. Vritra said he could be killed by something that was not dry or wet; that was not a weapon for fighting; nor in the day nor night.  
Indra kepting thinking of a loophole- how could he kill Vritra. Once evening, Indra saw Vritra standing on the sea coast. He took the name of Vishnu and hurled that at Vritra. The foam was neither dry nor wet, nor was it a conventional weapon and neither was it day or night. Vishnu entered the froth. Vritra was killed.

## Armies march to Kurukshetra

Wednesday, 21 December 2016

The Pandavas were sitting and talking when Krishna arrived. The Pandavas asked him what had happened?  
Krishna said You have a stubborn enemy. He refuses to part off any land. He refused even five villages. He said he would give even that land that lies at the tip of a needle.

This angered Yudhistira. Calming himself, he said The war will happen.  
Saying this, he asked for the allies to be sent messages for the war. He asked Sahadeva when was the right time to start for Kurukshetra. Sahadeva looked at the almanac and said Today is the fifth day of the moon and is the best day for one to start.

People started arriving. Camp was set up in Kurukshetra.

Shalya, uncle of Pandavas and brother of Madri came with an intention of fighting for the Pandavas. Duryodhana knew that Shalya was fond of entertainment and setup entertainment houses on the way. Shalya was happy but later realized this was a ruse of Duryodhana. He agreed reluctantly but said he wanted to meet Yudhistira once, before he moved to the Kaurava camp.

Duryodhana agreed. Shalya went and informed the Pandavas about what had happened.

Yudhistira said to Shalya - I am fine that you are sticking to your word. I need your help. When Arjuna and Karna fight, I need you to become the charioteer for Karna. Demotivate Karna as much as you can.

Shalya said he would what he could for the Pandavas. He said - For the good of you and Draupadi, I will take revenge for the words spoken by Karna during the game of dice. Suffering is there and even the great Indra had to suffer.

Yudhistira wanted to know about this. Shalya said - Let me tell you about the miseries that Indra had to face.

## SanatSujat comes to Dhritarashtra

Tuesday, 20 December 2016

After Krishna left, everyone left the court. Only Vidura and Dhrtrarashtra were left in the court. Soon the sage SanatSujat came there. Vidura told Dhritarashtra, who bowed down to the sage.  
Dhritarashtra said My son has become evil. He is trying to harm the Pandavas who had never harmed him. All tried to talk sense to him, but he does not listen.  
The sage said Your son will not listen. Listen to me.  
Long ago when the asuras ruled the world, all they did was kill and loot. The earth, scared went to Brahma and said I cannot bear the asuras anymore.  
Brahma decided to approach Vishnu.  
Vishnu said He would take incarnations to kill the asuras. Brahma told his to Earth.  
First Vishnu was born as the Fish. He killed Hayagriva and rescued the Vedas.  
As the Boar he rescued the Earth and killed Hiranyaksha.  
As the half Man half Lion, he killed Hiranyakashipu.  
As the tortoise, he held the mountain on his back and helped the churning of the ocean. Taking the form of Mohini, he helped the Gods get nectar.  
He then took care of Bali.  
These form were in the Satya Yuga.  
In the next cycle, Treta Yuga, Vishnu as Parashurama, killed the vain Kshatriyas. As Rama, he killed Ravana.  
As Krishna, he has killed Kamsa, Putana and Bakasura.  
He will take care of the rest. Soon the Yadava clan will also be destroyed.  
In the next Yuga of Kli, he will be born again.

Saying this, the sage left and Vidura also went back to his house.

## Krishna

Monday, 19 December 2016

Krishna and Satyaki along with Vidura entered the court. Wanting to see Krishna, the celestial sages also came there.  
Krishna said Listen all. I have come here as a messenger for the king Yudhistira. He wants to remind all that as per the previous condition, they have lived in the forests and spent a year in hiding. The pandavas are willing to forget all the insults.

Dhritarashtra said Duryodhana, did you hear what Krishna said? What they have asked for is justified. Give back what is theirs.

Duryodhana said I will return anything and if needed, there will be a war. If they have the strength, let them take it away.

All tried to talk to Duryodhana but he would not listen.

Krishna said Leave five villages. Forget the war. Nothing good will come from there. I am a well wisher of all.

Duryodhana said Even if the sun rises from west, I will not give anything to the Pandavas. Not even the land that is at the tip of a needle will be given to them. On the contrary, I am making you prisoner.

Krishna said I came as a messenger, Dhritarashtra, and your son wants to bind me. I can finish you all instantly. I will not do that.

Saying this Krishna started laughing. In his body was visible the three worlds. On his back one could all the Gods and the eleven Rudras. On his navel was Brahma. On his arm was Narada.

Afraid of this, all said Please forgive us. Please show us the form we are used to.

Krishna resumed his form. He tried to talk but Duryodhana walked out.

Krishna then came out. He went to Kunti. he told her what had happened. He then saw Karna. He went to him. He said I know who you are. You were born off Kunti, before she was married. You are well versed and known for your deeds of giving away whatever is asked from you. The Pandavas are your brothers. They will respect you and love you. You will become king instead of Yudhistira.

Karna said I respect you and your words. I was discarded at birth by Kunti. It was Radha who took care of me, fed her breast milk and brought me up. Do not tell the Pandavas what you said. If I win, I will give the kingdom to Duryodhana. He helped me get a position. While I know that the Pandavas will win, I will still fight. My fight will be with Arjuna only. I will spare the others. I know that Arjuna will kill me and the sons of Drupada will kill Bhishma and Drona. Victory will be theirs who have your blessings. I see comets in the sky which is not a good sign. Kauravas will not win. I dreamt of the victory of the Pandavas.

Karna left and with that Krishna also left Hastinapura.

## Krishna stays at Vidura's house

Sunday, 18 December 2016

While Krishna was with Kunti, Vidura arrived. He said he was sorry he was not there to greet Krishna.  
Krishna said You are a wise one, known all over the world. I am pleased with what little a devotee can offer.  
Vidura said What can I do you for you Krishna.  
Krishna said I am very hungry. Please feed me.  
Vidura saw a few lotus stems and rice at home. He asked his wife to cook those. Krishna and Satyaki ate that with relish. Vidura was feeling ashamed. He said He was unable to feed the guest at his house.  
Vidura said I know you have come as a messenger to talk sense to the sons of Gandhari. Be careful though. They are planning to make you prisoner.  
Krishna said I hate the pride that the sons of Dhritarashtra have. I can finish them in an instant.  
Vidura said Who can bind you? If a devotee comes, you yourself go and get bound.

Spending the night at Vidura's hut. Krishna then started for the court the next day.

## Kunti talks to Krishna

Saturday, 17 December 2016

Krishna went to Vidura's house and called out to him. Vidura was not there. Kunti heard him and came running. Krishna, seeing his aunt, bowed down and touched her feet.  
Kunti said I am not sure I know anyone more unfortunate than me. My husband left leaving small children. I brought them up and am unable to bear their hardships. Duryodhana is a sinner who has troubled my sons many times. With the help of Vidura, we were able to escape from the house of lac. My sons begged for food till we went to Panchala. For some days we had peace when Draupadi was won and Drupada accepted us. We got Indraprastha and that too went. Now I am not sure what will happen when the war starts.

Krishna said Do not grieve. In time, your sons will rule again soon. I have come as a messenger to talk to the Kururs. I will see what I can do. If they do not listen, it will be victory for your sons. Do not worry.

This consoled Kunti.

## Krishna arrives

Friday, 16 December 2016

When Krishna entered Hastinapura, he was amazed to see the city decorated. He said to Satyaki who was with him - It seems to a city of Indra. It seems Dhritarashtra has come to his senses.  
Krishna realized that all this was for show. He just smiled.  
Dushshasana came and welcomed Krishna and Satyaki. He asked them to come with him, but Krishna said he wanted to stay the night with Vidura.  
Duryodhana thought - Krishna has come for the Pandavas. He needs to be captured and bound.  
He shared his thoughts with the people with him. Dushshana and Shakuni agreed to this.

Duryodhana then called his soldiers and said When Krishna comes tomorrow, make sure he is unable to leave the palace. Bind him and make him a prisoner.

## Dhritarashtra talks to Duryodhana

Thursday, 15 December 2016

Vidura went to Dhritarashtra and said Krishna is coming to Hastinapura. All will be well if you listen to him.  
Dhritrarashtra said I am blessed. Krishna is coming to me to save my clan. Fetch Bhishma, Drona, Karna, Kripa and Duryodhana and ask them to meet me.  
Vidura left and went to call the elders.  
Dhritarashtra said This is my good fortune. Krishna is coming for the good of all. Decorate the city. Welcome him.  
Duryodhana said Krishna is like any other and why should he be given special treatment. He killed Jarasandha only because Jarasandha had addressed Krishna as a cowherd. The husband of Rukmini is very shrewd. I will not listen to him.  
Bhishma said Duryodhana has lost his senses. Krishna is the supreme being. Saying this he left.  
Dhritarashtra said to Duryodhana - Krishna is coming for our welfare. Be sensible.

Duryodhana agreed to welcome Krishna. He ordered for the city to be decorated.

## Krishna talks to Yudhistira

Wednesday, 14 December 2016

Janmajeya asked I have heard that Krishna will go as a messenger of the Pandavas. Please tell me what Krishna and Yudhistira discussed.How did the war start?  
The sage said - Listen to me.  
Krishna sat with Yudhistira. Yudhistira said It seems the war is inevitable. Duryodhana's decision will be the end of the clan. I am not in favor of this. We are free of the condition that we had and are now homeless. We served as servants for a year. Now I will have to fight my dear ones and get my land back. What use is that? If you, I am willing to forgive all and go back in the forest for ever. If I fight and loose I will not be able to show my face anywhere. Kauravas have great warriors like Bhishma, Drona, Kripa and Ashwatthama. How will this work?
