In the heaven, Yama asked his followers to go and get the soul of Satyavana. The Yamdoots came there. There was a glow emanating from Savitri's body. The helpers, not able to go close, went back to Yama.  
Yama then came himself. He said the time of Satyavana on his world was over and he needed to go. Savitri said she was not stopping him.  
Yama swooped Satyavana's soul and started on his journey.  
Savitri started following Yama. Yama asked her to go back but she said she would go where her husband was going. Savitri knew that death was ultimate truth but as a human it would take her some time to come to terms with that, and she would thus have to go where her husband was going.  
Pleased Yama said Ask for a boon. Ask for anything other than your husband.  
Savitri said let my father have sons.  
Yama said So be it.  
Savitri still did not leave Yama.  
Yama asked her to go back and she said she was reluctant to do so.  
Yama said ask for a boon other than your husband.  
Savitri said Let my father in law regain his sight.  
Yama said So be it.  
Savitri still continued.  
She said I want to know the ultimate truth and thus I am coming with you.  
Yama said Ask for something other than your husband.  
Savitri said Let my father in law regain his kingdom.  
Yama said So Be It.  
Savitri still did not not leave.  
Savitri said I know the world is an illusion and that all have to go, leaving their near and dear ones. I want to know how to combat that.  
Yama said I will give you anything that you need other than your husband.  
Savitri said Let me have a son through Satyavana.  
Yama said You wil have many sons. No please go home.  
Savitri still did not leave.  
Yama said I gave you four boons, what more do you need.  
Savitri said You blessed me to have sons, but how will that be possible if my husband is not with me?

Yama said You are clever and pious. Anyone who listens to your tale will be blessed. Go where you were, your husband will come to life. Your tale will be told among women and you will be known as the one who got her husband back from death.

## Savitri gets married

Tuesday, 18 October 2016

Knowing his daughter's mind, the king went to the forest and got Satyavana to his kingdom and with all rites, got his daughter married to him. Satyavana's parents were sad to see that their daughter in law, who had raised in all luxuries, would have to live the life of a hermit. Everyone who Savitri were pleased with her beauty and behavior.  
After her marriage, Savitri took care of her aged parents in law. Somewhere in her heart she was aware that her husband would not live long, and she began worshiping the Gods and feeding the brahmans.  
Soon time passed and slowly, Savitri also forgot that.  
When two days were left for the year to end, Savitri realized it was time for her to be separated from her husband. She decided not to do anything and let destiny take it's course.  
She immersed herself in the worship of Vishnu and Lakshmi.  
When the day arrived, Savitri got up early and fed the brahmans who came to her door.  
Satyavana decided to go to the forest to get fruits and wood for fire. Savitri said she would accompany her husband. She did not tell anyone why she wanted to go.  
Reluctantly Satyavana allowed her to come with him.  
She was feeling restless but did not let anyone realize what was going on in her mind.  
Seeing a tall tree, Satyavana decided to climb that to get the dry branches for firewood.  
Suddenly Satyavana said he was having a massive headache and was not able to see properly.  
Savitri asked him to come down and lie with his head on her lap. She would gently press his head and the headache would vanish.

## Savitri

Monday, 17 October 2016

Yudhistira said I am pleased to hear the amazing story of Rama. Who is this Savitri that you mention? I want to know more.  
Markandeya said - In the king of Avani was a king called Ashwapati. He prayed to Shiva for a child. He soon was blessed with a daughter who was named Savitri. She was beautiful to look at and even the Gods were jealous of her beauty. She was also a very learned person.

One day, when she was grown up, Savitri went to the forest with her friends. She came across a ashrama of a sage and decided to visit that. This was the ashrama of Dyumatsena. He was the king of Avantika, who had been defeated by his enemies and had settled down in the forest. His son, Satyavana, was with him. Satyavana was a very handsome person.  
Savitri saw him and wanted to know who the handsome person was. Hearing and knowing about him, Savitri wanted to have him as her husband.  
She went and told her mother. The queen and her husband were sad that Savitri had chosen a homeless person to be her husband.  
One day Narada came to visit the king. Seeing Savitri, the sage asked who she was. The king said this was his only daughter. The sage asked if she had been married, and the king said No, but his daughter had chosen to marry the son of sage.  
Narada asked Savitri who the person was, whom she had chosen. She said he was the son of King Dyumatsena. Narada said he knew who the sage was and asked her to choose another as her husband.  
Savitri said she would not do that. She had given her heart away and would live with that.  
The king asked the sage Why he was asking his daughter to choose another person?  
The sage said - Dyumtsena was a noble king in the solar dynasty. One day, the king lost his sight and became blind. As a blind king was not able to rule, he left his kingdom along with his wife and son and retired into the forest. Remember that one that is born, will die one day.  
The king hearing this said That was truth that would not be averted. He said he was happy knowing that his daughter had made a good choice.  
The sage said I will object to this alliance. There is no person who can beat Satyavan in qualities or looks. but a year from now, he will be no more.  
The king he would now like to rethink and probably arrange for a swayamvar for his daughter. After all, life was tough for a young widow.  
Savitri hearing her father said I will marry no other. If being a widow is there in my destiny, then that will come true, irrespective of whom I get married to.  
Hearing this, Narada blessed the girl and left.  
The king tried to convince Savitri to marry another, but she was adamant.

## Death of Ravana

Sunday, 16 October 2016

Ravana, shocked at the death of his son, came to fight. He saw Vibhishana and shot an arrow to kill him. Lakshmana cut that off. Angry, Ravana took a might spear and hurled that towards Lakshmana. Lakshmana fell. Hanumana then fetched the medicine and brought Lakshmana back to life.  
Ravana then came back to fight. Indra sent a chariot for Rama.  
Rama cut off the heads of Ravana and new heads emerged.  
Vibhishana said Ravana could be killed by only one specific arrow that had been hidden by Mandodari.  
In disguise, Hanumana tricked Mandodari to tell him where the weapon was and brought that to Rama.  
Using that, Rama killed Ravana.  
When Sita came to Rama, Rama said to her - You stayed ten months in the palace of a demon and I will not accept you.  
Sita asked Lakshmana to light a pyre and jumped into that.  
Agni came out and handed Sita over to Rama.  
Rama then crowned Vibhishana the king of Lanka and then returned to Ayodhya.  
Ravana and Kumbhakarna were none other than Jay and Vijay who were killed by Rama.  
Later Jay and Vijay were born as Shishupala and Dantavakra. Shishupala tried to stop your sacrifice and was killed by Krishna. Later Krishna killed Dantravakra also.  
Thus in three births, Jay and Vijaya were killed by incarnations of Vishnu.

What is your grief in front of Rama's. All will be well soon. Draupadi is pure like Savitri and should not be doubted.

## Indrajit dies

Saturday, 15 October 2016

In Lanka, Vibhishana told Rama about the rakshasas. The fight started with Rama cutting off the crowns from the heads of Ravana. Rama then sent Angada to Ravana. Yudhistira, it is a big story but in short, the mighty rakshasas fell one by one. Indrajit came and bound Rama and Lakshmana with snakes. Pavana asked Rama to think of Garuda, who came and drove away the snakes. Many rakshasas came after that and fell, Indrajit came back and gravely injured Rama and Lakshmana. Listening to Vibhishana, Hanumana went to fetch the mountain, Gandhamadana. With the medicines, Rama and Lakshmana and the army was revived.  
Ravana then woke up Kumbhakarna and sent him to battle. Kumbhakarna tried swallowing up the army of vanaras but was killed in battle by Rama.Lakshmana went and stopped the sacrifice of Indrajit and killed him.

## Rama meets Sugriva

Friday, 14 October 2016

Seeing no one around, Ravana kidnapped Sita. Fearing Rama, he swiftly drove his chariot. Sita crying out to Rama, started dropping off her jewels in various places. Jatayu, a bird and a friend of Dashratha, fought Ravana and finally fell when Ravana cut off his wings. Ravana reached Lanka with Sita and pleaded with Sita to accept him. Sita said Rama was her Lord. Ravana then made Sita a prisoner in the Ashoka garden. Few rakshasis reamined there to guard Sita.  
Rama returned to see an empty home and Lakshmana rushing towards him. Lakshmana said Sita had sent him to see if all was well with Rama. The brothers cried, looking for Sita. Rama saw some jewels of Sita and went that way. Soon he came across a dying Jatayu. Jatayu said Sita had been captured by Ravana and taken to Lanka. Jatayu then died and Rama and Lakshmana performed the last rites of the bird. They then went towards the mountain Rishyamukha where they met the vanaras Sushena, Nala, Nila, Hanumana and Sugriva. Sugriva said Vali, his brother, had taken over his kingdom by force.  
Rama agreed to help Sugriva by killing Vali. In return Sugriva promised help in rescuing Sita.  
As per his word, Rama killed Vali and made Sugriva the king of Kishkindhya. After four months, search for Sita started. Hanumana was sent to look out for Sita.  
Hanumana killed many rakshasas and burnt the city of Lanka. He then came back to Rama with the news.  
Vibhishana asked Ravana to return Sita and Ravana insulted him, by kicking him. Vibhishana came to Rama and took his shelter. Rama promised to make Vibhishana the king of Lanka, after killing Ravana.  
A bridge was built over the mighty ocean and Rama crossed over along with the vanaras.

## Rama in Panchavati

Thursday, 13 October 2016

Hearing Rama had left, Dashratha died. This was due to a curse from a blind sage, that the king would die when his son left him. Ayodhya was grief stricken. Bharata heard this when he returned. He rebuked his mother. He performed the last rites of the king. When the minsters told him to become king, Bharata said he was just there to serve, and the throne belonged to Rama. he asked all to accompany him and try to get back Rama.

Bharata found Rama on the Chitrakoota mountain. He asked Rama to return. Rama was grieved to hear of the death of Dashratha. He asked Bharata to rule for fourteen years till he came and claimed his throne. Bharata said he would rule alongside Rama and asked for his slippers. Rama gave away the slippers. Bharata took that back, placed it on the throne of Ayodhya.

After Bharata left, Rama, Lakshmana and Sita left Chitrakoota. Then across the ashrama of the sage Agastya. They asked the sage where they should reside. The sage asked them to live in the forest of Panchavati.

Surpanakha, sister of Ravana, one day came there. She saw Rama and wanted him for herself. She changed her self to look like a beautiful woman and approached Rama. Rama said he was married. Surpanakha then went to Lakshmana who said he would not marry or take any woman. Surpanakha thought all this was happening because of Sita. She tried to kill Sita. Lakshmana seeing this, cut off her ears and nose.

Surpanakha went to two rakshasas, Khara and Dushana and told them what had happened. The rakshasas came to fight Rama and were killed.

Surpanakha then went to Ravana. She said she was in the forest when the son of Dashratha had cut off her ears and nose. There was a very beautiful woman there, who deserved to be in Lanka. When tried to get that woman for Ravana, her nose and ears were cut.

Hearing of the beauty of Sita, Ravana wanted to get her. He asked Maricha to help him. Maricha said he had been once attacked by Rama and he did not have the courage to face him again. When Ravana threatened to kill him, Maricha agreed to go in the guise of a golden deer. Seeing the deer, Sita wanted it. She asked Rama to get it for her. Keeping Lakshmana guard, Rama went running behind the deer.

When Rama struck the deer, it shouted out for help, in Rama's voice. Sita was shocked. She requested Lakshmana, and sent him to look for Rama.

## Birth of Rama and marriage with Sita

Wednesday, 12 October 2016

In the solar dynasty, the king Dashratha was performing a sacrifice to get sons. Because of the great deeds he had done, Vishnu was born in his house. From the sacrificial fire, rose a pot. A voice asked the content to be divided among the queens. Seeing two queens there, the king gave the pot to them. The youngest queen, Sumitra was not there then. When she came to know, she was sad. The other two queens, Kaushlaya and Kaikeyi, gave Sumitra portions from their share. Soon the queens were pregnant.  
One by one, the queens delivered their children. The eldest son was Rama, a dusky boy, born off Kaushalya. Bharata came next, born off Kaikeyi. Sumitra had twins Lakshmana and Shatrughna.  
The princes began growing and soon it was time for them to go and learn the scriptures and the art of using weapons.  
In Mithila, the king Janaka, was farming for many days tilling his land. Lakshmi was born there. Seeing a child in the furrow, Janaka brought her up as his daughter and named her Sita.  
Seeing the future, the Gods kept the bow of Shiva, in Janaka's house. They said to Janaka that he should give away his daughter to that person only, who would be able to, easily lift and bend and thus break the bow.  
None could break the bow and soon Rama came there and breaking the bow, married Sita.  
Listen Yudhistira, what had had happened before this.  
Ravana had sent his followers to destroy sacrifices. Seeing this, the sage Vishwamitra went to Dashratha asking for Rama, who was destined to help him. Fearing the sage, the king reluctantly agreed to give Rama and Lakshmana.  
On their way, they came across the ferocious rakshashi Taraka. Rama, not fearing her, killed her.  
Vishwamitra then took Rama to the place where he would be performing his sacrifice. He said the rakshasas would come when he started his sacrifice. Rama said He would stay guard.  
Vishwamitra started his sacrifice. In the skies, the evil Maricha, started his attack. His shadow fell on the pyre. Rama released a flaming arrow, fearing which Maricha fled from there.  
The sacrifice finished peacefully.  
Vishwamitra then told the princes about the bow of Shiva. Rama said he wanted to see it. Taking the two brothers with him, the sage reached Mithila.

Janaka greeted the sage. Vishwamitra said he wished Rama should marry Sita. Janaka told the sage that he was most willing to have Rama as a his son in law, except that he had promised that Sita would be given to only that person who would break the bow.

Rama said he would do that and went where the bow was, Rama lifted the bow with his left hand. The Gods were wondering if the world would sink and asking Vasuki, who was holding the earth, to be careful. Lakshmana asked Rama to hurry up. Rama took the names of the Gods and pulled the bow. The bow broke and fell down. In Lanka, Ravana heard the noise, and realized that the one who would kill him was there.

Yudhistira asked if Rama was Vishnu, then why did he take the name of Vishnu with the other Gods, before breaking the bow?

The sage said - When Hiranyakashipu was killed Narasimha, the creature kept growling and this disturbed the world. A brahmana then cursed him saying You are so proud of yourself. When you are born again, you will forget about divinity.

With the bow broken, Janaka gave away Sita to Rama. Rama said First send messengers to my father and ask him to come with my two brothers. Only when they are here, I will marry.

Janaka swiftly sent messengers. Dashratha hearing the news, came with his two sons. Janaka had another daughter who was given away to Lakshmana. Janaka's brother, Kushadhwaja had two daughters who were given away to Bharat and Shatrughna.

Dashratha decided to leave for Ayodhya with sons and daughters in law. He got on his chariot when Parashurama appeared there.

To Rama, Parashurama said, All know that I am an enemy of the kshatriyas. You have the audacity to have the same name as mine. You think you are brave because you broke the bow of Shiva. That is an old bow. If you are brave, lift my bow.

Rama agreed. When Parashurama gave his bow to Rama, along with that all his powers also got transferred to Rama. Rama put an arrow on the bow and asked what should he do with that. Parashurama asked Rama to stop his entry to heaven.

Parashurama left and Dashratha also started for Ayodhya, with his sons and daughters in law.

Soon after, one day, Bharata decided to visit his grandfather, father of his mother. he took Shatrughna along with him. Dashratha also decided to make Rama the crown prince.

When Kaikeyi, Bharata's mother heard this from her maid, she became jealous.

When Dashratha came to her, she said she wanted two promises. The king agreed. Kaikeyi said she wanted Bharata to be made king and Rama to be exiled for fourteen years.

The king fainted and when he regained consciousness, he rebuked the queen.

Rama, when he heard this, agreed to go to the forest. Rama went to his mother and told her this and consoled her. Later, he left for the forest with his wife, Sita and Lakshmana.

## Birth of Ravana

Tuesday, 11 October 2016

Markandeya said In Lanka, the rakshasas were creating havoc. Vishnu, using his discus killed them. The remaining ones hid in the nether world.  
On earth, there was a sage called Vishwashrava. The sage had a son Vishravan. The son was made the Lord of Lanka. Of the rakshasas there was one named Sumali. He called his daughter Nikasha and said to her - Lanka belonged to us and the son of Vishwashrava is now there. Kubera as Vishravan is known, is ruling Lanka. Do something to have a rakshasa there. Go to Vishwashrava and please him. If you have sons from him, that will help have someone from our clan in Lanka.  
Nikasha went to Vishwashrava and asked him to accept her as his wife. She served the sage and the sage pleased asked what she wanted. She said she wanted sons. The sage the time is bad and you will have two ferocious sons. Seeing her sad, the sage said there will be a son who will be good natured. Soon three sons were born to her. They were called Ravana, Kumbhakarna and Vibhishana. The sons prayed for boons. When Brahma wanted to give them boons, Ravana said he wanted to be immortal. Knowing Kumbhakarna, Brahma asked Saraswato to intervene. Saraswati sat on Kumbhakarna's tongue and when Brahma asked what boon he wanted, Saraswati, as Kumbhakarna, said he wanted to sleep. Ravana begged Brahma to do something. Brahma said Kumbhakarna would sleep for six months and wake for a day. If he fought on that day, no one would be able to defeat him. In Kumbhakarna was woken untimely, he would meet his end. Vibhishana wanted to stay as a devotee of Vishnu and was granted the boon of immrotality.  
With his boons, Ravana took over Lanka and Kubera fled from there. Ravana soon had a son Indrajit and with the help of his son, he was able to conquer the three worlds.  
Indra asked Brahma to help the Gods from Ravana. Brahma took them all to Vishnu.  
Vishnu asked the Gods not to worry. He said he would incarnate on earth and put an end to Ravana.

## Narasimha

Monday, 10 October 2016

In sweet tones, the king addressed hi son. I am amazed to see you being saved every time someone tries to kill you. I have never seen anything like like and would like to know more.  
Prahlada said It was Vishnu who was saving him every time.  
The asura said Where is this Vishnu? What form does he hold?  
Prahlada said He is present in all things. All living and non living things are a manifestation of Vishnu.  
Pointing to a pillar, the asura asked Is Vishnu in this too? Prahlada said - Yes.  
Angry, the king took his sword and struck the pillar. In the pillar was Vishnu in the form of half human, half lion. The asura saw a human body with the face of a lion. As the daylight receded, this creature started expanding its body. Soon the entire universe was covered by Narasimha - half lion, half man. Narasimha let out a might roar. Picking up the asura, Narasimha placed him on his thigh, and using his nails, tore his body apart.  
The creature was angry and terrible to look at. The Gods were scared of approaching him. Only Prahlada approached him and bowed down to him, and asked him to calm down.  
Pleased Narasimha said there is no devotee of mine, equal to you. Do not grieve for your father. Your descendants will also be devotees.

Thus Jay Vijaya were relieved of their curse.

## Prahlada

Sunday, 9 October 2016

The child Prahlada grew to be a great devotee of Vishnu. There was no one equal to him. Vishnu was his life. Disturbed, his father sent him for his education.  
When his guru looked at him, Prahlada recited the name of Vishnu silently. When the guru was away, Prahlada taught the other students the way to worship Vishnu. The guru reported this to the king.  
The king sent for his son and asked him why he was taking the name of the enemy of the clan.  
Prahlada started praising Vishnu in front of the king.  
Angry the king gave orders for his son to be killed. When the soldiers tried to strike the boy with their weapons, the weapons broke. When the king asked Prahlada how this happened, the boy said Vishnu was protecting him. Angry the king asked his soldiers to have the boy poked by the tusks of the wild elephants. When the tusks touched the boy, they became powder. When the king asked his son, how this happened, Prahalada said this was all because of Vishnu.  
The king then had a huge rock brought. Prahlada was tied to that and flung in the sea.  
In the water, Prahlada prayed to Vishnu. He said I am not afraid of death but I do not want to die before seeing you. With Vishnu's grace. the rock floated to the surface. Vishnu asked Prahlada what he wanted. The boy said having seen him, all his wishes were fulfilled. He asked for the deliverance of his father. Vishnu said he would soon do that.  
The king sent for his son when he heard Prahlada was alive.

## Hiranyaksha killed

Saturday, 8 October 2016

The sons were named Hiranyaksha and Hiranyakashipu. From childhood they performed evil deeds like destroying sacrifices. Soon they envied the throne of Indra and fought for that.  
The Gods went to Brahma and asked him to do something.  
Brahma said I am aware of this. Soon they will meet their ends in the hands of the God who carries the mace (Gadadhara - Vishnu).  
Listen Yudhistira, what happened next. The elder, Hiranyaksha, was looking for battle. He wanted to fight someone who matched his strength. Asking Hiranyakashipu to look after the throne, he went out looking for battle. With his mace in his hand, he was searching for an opponent when he came across the sage Narada. Narada said Hari was the opponent to fight. The demon asked where he could get him.  
Narada said Fearing that you were coming, he has fled. Taking the shape of a boar, he is hiding in the waters.  
The demon went near the waters and shouted for battle. Hearing this, with a mighty roar, the boar came out. The demon said Roar as much as you can, I will destroy you today.  
In the battle that followed, the demon was killed.  
After the battle, the boar did not come out of the water. The world was worried. What was Vishnu doing?  
Hiranyakashipu was wondering what happened to his brother, when he was told by Narada that in the fight with Vishnu, his brother had been killed.  
Hiranyakashipu declared Vishnu his enemy. He started destroying sacrifices and killing brahmans.  
Soon a son was born to him. His name was Prahlada.

## Jay and Vijay cursed

Friday, 7 October 2016

Markandeya said Listen Yudhistira while I tell you the story. I will tell you what happened before this. In Vaikuntha, Vishnu resided. He had two brothers who guarded his doors. The elder one was Jay and the younger one was Vijay. One day they stopped a brahmana from entering. Angry, the brahmana cursed them to be born on the earth.  
The brothers told this to Vishnu. Vishnu said : The brahmana should not have been stopped. There is nothing I can do about the curse. You will be born on the earth as per the curse.  
The brothers then said How many years would they have to live like that, away from Vaikuntha?  
Vishnu said If you are on earth and you become my disciple then you will have to go through seven births and deaths before you are back here. If, you become my enemies, then you will have to go through three births and deaths and you will return then. Do not worry, I will also be born to be with you.  
The brothers agreed for the later.  
At that time, Diti, wife of Sage Kashyapa, was praying for a son. She went to her husband asking for a son to be born to her. The sage said The time when you have approached me is an extremely inauspicious time. The son born to you will be evil.  
Diti said she was ready for the consequences.  
Kashyapa said you will have twins. They will grow up and become evil. Vishnu will be born and will kill your sons with own hands. You will also suffer grief for the loss of these sons.  
Jay and Vijay were then born as the twin sons of Diti.  
The earth trembled when they were born.

## Markandeya visits Yudhistira

Thursday, 6 October 2016

Janmajeya then asked the sage What happened after this? What did the Pandavas do?  
The sage said Listen Son of Parikshita.  
The Pandavas returned to their hut and had their meal. They were sad. They were sitting heavy with a heavy heart, when the sage Markandeya came there. They were pleased to see the sage and greeted him.  
After seating the sage and washing his feet, Yudhistira asked the sage the reason for his visit.  
The sage sensed that Yudhistira was not happy and something was troubling him.  
He said I know of the sufferings you have been through. Let me tell you that soon all will be over. The learned and wise do not ponder over their misfortune.  
Yudhistira said I am not sure if there any one who is as unfortunate as I am, in all worlds. I have lived hard lives in the forests, having been tormented by my cousins. Born in the royal household, we have spent our lives in forests. Draupadi, born in the royal family, has to toil in the forest. Today, seeing a empty house, Jayadhrata came and kidnapped her.  
The sage said What grief do you have when you have able brothers with you and you are all together. Draupadi cooks and with that you are able to feed countless people. If you say you are a hermit, then who is a householder. The daughter of Janaka, who was more beautiful than Draupadi, had to go through worse. She was captured by the king of Lanka and had to spend ten months as his prisoner. Killing the evil Ravana, Rama rescued Sita. Fourteen years were spent in the forest, wearing bark and animal skin. Rama was no other than Narayana.

Yudhistira said I am interested to know more about Rama. Why was Narayana born as Rama.

## Jayadhrata returns of Hastinapur

Wednesday, 5 October 2016

The Kauravas were worried Was all fine with Jayadhrata? While all were wondering, Jayadhrata came there.  
Duryodhana greeted his brother in law and asked him the reason for his delay. Jayadhrata told him all that had happened.  
Hearing all, Duryodhana decided that it was futile to try anything. Let destiny take its course.

## Jayadhrata prays to Shiva

Tuesday, 4 October 2016

Jayadhrata felt insulted. He did not know how to face Duryodhana. He wanted to kill the Pandavas. He decided to pray and the strength from the Gods. He decided to pleased Shiva and get boons from him.  
He went to the Himalayas and concentrating, he began meditating on Shiva. His penance was austere. Seeing his devotion, Shiva appeared before him in the guise of a brahmin.

Shiva said Leave your meditation and ask for a boon. Jayadhrata opened his eyes and saw a brahmin in front of him. Jayadhrata asked him who he was, and the brahmin said he was Shiva. Jayadhrata then said If you are truly Shiva, show me your real self.

The brahman then showed him his self. Glowing body with snakes curled around, clad in tiger skin. A trident on his right hand and a two headed small drum in his left. The moon glowing on his head.

Jayadhrata fell on his feet, prostrate. When Shiva asked him to ask for a boon, he said he wanted to kill the Pandavas. Shiva said he was unable to do so. Saying so, Shiva went away.

Jayadhrata started praying again and Shiva came again. Jayadhrata asked for the same boon again. Shiva said he would not be able to help. Finally after many pleading, Shiva said Jayadhrata would be able to defeat Abhimanyu, the valiant son of Arjuna and the nephew of Krishna. This would break the Pandavas.

Saying this, Shiva went away and Jayadhrata set forth for Hastinapura.

## Jayadhrata kidnaps Draupadi

Monday, 3 October 2016

Listen Janmajeya what happened next.  
Jayadhrata hid in the forest. In the morning, Bhima and Arjuna went for a hunt. Soon Yudhitira and the sons of Madri went for a bath. Draupadi was alone in the ashrama. She was preparing food. Jayadhrata saw his opportunity. He parked his chariot in the entrance of the hut and got down.  
Seeing a relative coming, Draupadi came out. As no one else was home, Draupadi gave Jayadhrata a seat. She asked if all was well and how others were doing?  
Jayadhrata asked I see you alone here. Where have the others gone? I had come to see Yudhistira but I do not see him.  
Draupadi said Nakula, Sahadeva and Yudhistira with the brahmans had gone for a bath. Bhima and Arjuna had gone hunting and would be back soon.

Hearing this Jayadhrata decided to act fast. He caught Draupadi and dragged herr onto his chariot, He then started driving his chariot as fast as possible.

Draupadi started shouting saying by touching her, he had brought about his end. Seeing no other respite, she started shouting out to Krishna for help.

In the meanwhile Yudhistira returned back with the sons of Madri. They were surprised to see the hut empty. In a distance they heard the cries of Draupadi. They picked up their weapons and ran towards the direction of the sound.

Jayadhrata increased his speed. As destiny would have it, he came face to face with Arjuna and Bhima who were returning back. The brothers having heard the cries of Draupadi had also run towards the direction of the sound.

Arjuna then thought of a chariot, and one appeared there. Arjuna and Bhima climbing on, started chasing Jayadhrata. Seeing the distance between the chariots narrowing, Bhima jumped off the chariot and caught Jayadhrata by his hair.

Draupadi kicked Jayadhrata three times of his face. Bhima then began slapping him. He then began dragging Jayadhrata's face on the ground, till it was bloody. Jayadhrata fainted. Yudhistira then arrived there and asked Bhima to leave Jayadhrata.

When Jayadhrata came to his sense, Yudhistira asked him why he had come there to perform this evil deed. When he was quiet, Yudhistira asked him to go and tell the ones who had sent him, to show what had happened.

Along with Draupadi, the Pandavas then went back to their ashrama.

## Jayadhrata asked to kidnap Draupadi

Sunday, 2 October 2016

Duryodhana said Whatever advise I have got so far has backfired. If the Pandavas live a year in disguise and return unnoticed, all hell will break loose. It will be difficult to defeat the Pandavas who are blessed by the Gods.  
Karna said The daughter of Drupada is the life of the Pandavas. If, somehow, Draupadi is kidnapped, we will be safe. Let Jayadhrata go in disguise and lure Draupadi away. He will hide her somewhere where the Pandavas will not be able to find her. In her sorrow, the Pandavas will never return.

It was decided then,Jayadhratawas to go and lure Draupadi away.Jayadhratawas happy to be of service and Duryodhana asked him to proceed.

Jayadhrata said he would go but what if the Pandavas attacked. Arjuna alone could defeat all.  
Duryodhana askedJayadhratato look for an opportunity when the Pandavas were away. The Pandavas were not to feared. Karna was no less and would be able to defeat the Pandavas. When the Pandavas went for the bath was the time to approach and capture Panchali. She would have to hidden in a place where no one would find her. Only you can do this.

Jayadhratha said he was ready to perform the act. He got onto a decorated chariot and took leave of all. He entered the forest. There were trees on all sides and a small path in the middle. All the time Jayadhrata was thinking of the Pandavas and how he would save himself if attacked.

He soon spotted the ashrama of the Pandavas and hid himself near that.

## Durvasa pleased with Yudhistira

Saturday, 1 October 2016

In the morning, after finishing his daily tasks, Yudhistira asked Bhima and Arjuna to hunt and get some food. He then went about gathering flowers and fruits.  
Draupadi came there after her bath. They were all awaiting Durvasa to come and leave satisfied. Draupadi said she would cook a meal which would easily expand to feed millions.

On the riverside, Durvasa got up and after offering his morning prayers, along with disciples decided to visit the pandavas.

The Pandavas, hearing of the visit, greeted the sage and the disciples, and offered them seats on mats made of deer skin. Cool water was offered for drinking.

Yudhistira said I am blessed that a great sage like you has come to visit me.It is because of the good deeds of my forefathers that you have come to see me.

Pleased Durvasa said I am pleased with you, O son of Dharma. You are a righteous person with none equal to you in wisdom. You are untouched by vices. I have to confess that I am shamed today. I asked you to cook and then went to the river bank in the evening and was unable to return to you. Now we are hungry and and would request you to feed us.  
The sages ate to their fill and were very pleased.  
Durvasa said If this is the service I get in a forest then what is the need for heaven. Do not worry. Your sorrows will be gone soon.

Saying this, the sage left along with his disciples.

Hearing that Durvasa had left the ashrama of the Pandavas after blessing them, Duryodhana was sad, His hunger vanished. Karna then said to Duryodhana to listen to what he had to say.

## September

## Delay in posts

Monday, 19 September 2016

Going through a rough patch and will not be in a position to post anything for a month

## Durvasa's hunger satisfied

Saturday, 17 September 2016

Garuda bowed down to Krishna and wanted to why he had been summoned. Krishna said he wanted to go immediately where the Pandavas were residing.  
Krishna climbed up on Garuda and in a minute he was in the Kamya forest.  
Yudhistira seeing Krishna was relieved. Krishna wanted to what had happened.  
Yudhistira said Seeing Krishna was like a beggar getting a lot of wealth. Krishna asked Yudhistira how things were. Yudhistira said - I do not know how to tell about my plight. At this time of the night Durvasa has come with his disciples. He has gone to the river to offer his prayers and will then come back and ask for food.  
Krishna said Do not worry. I will make sure that the sage and the disciples are satisfied.  
Krishna then went where Draupadi was. Draupadi gave Krishna a place to sit. She said Durvasa would be coming soon for food. Krishna said Let us think of that later. I am hungry, give me something to eat.  
Draupadi said This is not fair of you. If I was able to feed anyone at this time of the night, then I would have not have summoned you.  
Krishna said I cannot talk now, I am dying of hunger.  
Draupadi said I have had my meal and have kept the dish aside. The vessel is empty.  
Krishna said There must be something in that. Look closely. Whatever it be, I will be happy. Do not be lethargic. Get up and get that vessel.  
Draupadi went and fetched the vessel for Krishna. He saw a piece of green and a grain of rice on the vessel. He put that in his mouth and said I am satisfied now. My hunger is gone.  
Krishna then burped and the whole world also burped.  
Durvasa and his disciples were puzzled. They were not hungry anymore. They decided to rest where they were and decided not to go back to Yudhistira.  
Krishna knew this. He went to Yudhistira and said Durvasa would come in the daylight to have his meal. Yudhistira thanked Krishna calling him the only savior. Krishna said he knew all that was happening and asked him to never leave the path of truth.  
Saying this Krishna left for Dwaraka, riding on Garuda.

## Durvasa comes to the Pandavas

Friday, 16 September 2016

Durvasa set forth with his disciples. He said to them Let us go to the Kamya forest and visit the sons of Pandu. We will also have a bath in the holy pilgrimage of Prabhasha. Also we will be able to fulfill the wish of the king Duryodhana. We will be able to fulfill all three things.  
Yudhistira hearing about the arrival of Durvasa went with his brothers to greet him. They wondered why the sage had come. The sage was famous for cursing people on the slightest fault. Yudhistira asked the sage the reason for his visit. The sage said He had been to Hastinapura and had been served by the Kauravas. Wanting to see the Pandavas, he had come there. He also said he was hungry having traveled a great distance and wanted food. After having food they would go for a bath.

Yudhistira was in a dilemma. Draupadi had finished her meal. He asked the sage to finish his worship and then come.

He went to Draupadi. She had heard the conversation between the sage and Yudhistira was equally worried. She said she would be able to get food for all at dawn but in the evening, it would be impossible.  
Not knowing what to do, they both began taking the name of Krishna. The other brothers also joined in taking the name of Krishna and asking him to help them.

In Dwaraka, Krishna had just lain down in his room and was being taken care of, by Rukmini. He sat up. Someone was calling him. Rukmini asked him the reason and Krishna said someone in distress was calling out to him for help.  
Krishna sensed that Yudhistira was calling out to him. He said he would immediately start for the Kamya forest.  
Rukmini asked him to stay the night and go in the morning. Krishna said he needed to leave immediately else the clan of Pandavas would get destroyed.

Krishna thought of Garuda, who immediately appeared before him.

## Durvasa comes to Duryodhana

Thursday, 15 September 2016

Janmajeya said - O Sage, Duryodhana was a wicked person, jealous of all good. He thought evil of those who helped him from death. What did Duryodhana do after this? I am interested to hear more.  
Vaishmpayana said - The pandavas stayed in the Kamaya forest. They fed the brahmanas in the forest.

Duryodhana felt insulted seeing the Pandavas happy. He said to Karna and Shakuni - How do I defeat the Pandavas? I was insulted by Chitrasen but being rescued the Pandavas is worse. We will have to think of something to finish them off in the twelve years when they live in the forest.

One day Durvasa came to Duryodhana. He had with him ten thousand of his disciples. Duryodhana went and greeted the sage and the disciples and then asked for the reason for visit.  
The sage he wanted to see how Duryodhana was prospering.  
The sage was pleased to see how Duryodhana was ruling his land and blessed him.

One day Duryodhana called Karna, Dushasana and Shakuni. He said The Pandavas live in the Kamya forest. Draupadi by the grace of the Sun God cooks little which multiplies and thus she is able to feed countless people with that. All get food till Draupadi eats. Once she has had her meal, the vessel is empty. She has a meal in the evening. Let us send Durvasa there in the evening, once she has eaten. Having nothing to eat, the sage with curse the Pandavas. That will be their end and we will be saved.

The next day Duryodhana went to Durvasa and in sweet tones he said he wanted the sage to go and visit the Pandavas in the forest.

## Arjuna fights Chitrasen

Wednesday, 14 September 2016

Yudhistira said to Arjuna, Stop the Gandharva before he reaches his abode. Rescue Duryodhana. Do not fight or quarrel with Duryodhana.  
Bhima and Arjuna said We are happy. Of all the evil that he has done towards us, now is the time for him to suffer. Now that Duryodhana is out of the way, we can go and take over our kingdom.  
Yudhistira said What you said is not wrong. I also want to fight but this is not the time. Chitrasen will take Duryodhana's wife and give her as a maid in the heaven. This will not be good for the family. We will become a laughing stock for all. Your strength will be doubted.

Arjuna's pride was hurt. Without any delay he took Yudhistira's blessings and taking the name of Govinda, he set forth.

Chitrasen saw Arjuna coming and he increased the speed of his chariot. Arjuna with a weapon stopped the motion of the chariot. Chitrasen got down from his chariot and seeing Arjuna asked him the reason for stopping him. He said - You have come to rescue one who tried ways to kill you. Leave my way and let me go.

Arjuna said The fight is between us and Duryodhana, so why are you intervening. Duryodhana is elder to me and it is my duty to respect that.

A war started between Arjuna and Chitrasen. Using the same weapon that Chitrasen had used to tie Duryodhana, Arjuna bound the gandharva and then taking Duryodhana, the women and Chitrasen went where Yudhistira was.

Yudhistira untied Duryodhana and the women and asked Arjuna to release Chitrasen.

Duryodhana then set forth for Hastinapur and Chitrasen returned back to his owm palace.

## Duryodhana bound by Chitrasen

Tuesday, 13 September 2016

The Gandharva then attacked Duryodhana. Duryodhana had to take care of the women travelling with him and also fight along side. Using a weapon, Chitrasen bound Duryodhana. Putting the bound Duryodhana along with the women in his chariot, Chitrasen started driving away. The women started shouting out for help. Someone of them started cursing Duryodhana, saying all this was happening because of the fight he had picked up with the Pandavas.  
Not seeing any way out, Duryodhana's wife asked a soldier to somehow run from there and trace out the Pandavas and seek their help. She said she was aware that her husband had done many bad deeds and there was a possibility that Pandavas could refuse to help. If that was so, then she was prepared to consume poison and die.  
The soldier rushed and soon was able to find the Pandavas sitting with sages. The soldier said he had come for help. In a war with Chitrasen, Karna, Dushasana and Salva had fled the battlefield. Duryodhana had been bound and captured and was being taken away, along with the women of the royal household. Seeing no other way, Duryodhana's wife had asked him to seek help from the Pandavas.

Hearing this, Yudhistira was hurt. He asked Arjuna to go and help Duryodhana and rescue him.

## Chitrasen attacks Duryodhana

Monday, 12 September 2016

The army and troupe of Duryodhana went through the forest. On the way they trampled many trees. As destiny would have it, they ended up crossing a garden which belonged to the Gandharva Chitrasen. The guards reported to their master that the garden had been destroyed by Duryodhana. They said they tried to stop him, but Duryodhana had insulted them and said they were alive because he was not in a mood to fight. Karna and the others had then pushed the guards out.  
Chitrasen climbed up on his chariot and decided to confront Duryodhana. He went where Duryodhana was and said - You dare destroy my garden and behave rudely. Have you lost the desire for life?  
Saying this the Gandharva shot an arrow which hid the sun and it became dark. Karna then shot an arrow and the sun was visible again. Seeing this Chitrasen shot a few arrows which were cut off by Karna. The Gandharva then picked an weapon called Simhamukha and aimed that. Karna cut that off. The snake weapon was released which was cut off by Karna who release eagles.  
Chitrasen said he was pleased to see the skill of Karna. He then released a few arrows which hit Karna and he fell senseless. While Karna was trying to get up, the army attacked Chitrasen but the gandharva cut off all the weapons of the army. Karna then began showering weapons.  
Chitrasen then understood that to combat this, magic would be needed. He then made himself invisible and then started showering arrows. Unable to fight an invisible enemy, the army was soon vanquished. Unable to fight, Karna soon gave up.

## Duryodhana decides to visit Prabhas

Sunday, 11 September 2016

Janmajeya said - O Sage, your tale is wonderful. Please tell what happened after all left.  
The sage said - Pandavas with Draupadi lived in the Kamya forest, at the pilgrim spot of Prabhas. Arjuna would hunt and Draupadi would cook that for all.  
In Hastinapura, Duryodhana was ruling the combined kingdoms of Hastinapura and Indraprastha.  
One day Shakuni said to Duryodhana - The land is blessed to have you as the king. You are surrounded by wealth and luxuries, All is good except there is no one to praise you. You need to show this off to your enemies. The Pandavas need to see you are happy.  
Karna said What Shakuni had said was true and the Pandavas needed to be shown the wealth of the king. The Pandavas are residing at Prabhas in the guise of sages. We should visit them and show them our wealth. Let us go to visit Prabhas and bathe in the waters there.  
Getting the families together with horses and elephant, Duryodhana started off for Prabhas. They did not disclose this to Bhishma and Drona as they were sure they would be stopped from venturing. A huge army went along with them. The women were happy to come along. This would be chance for them to meet Draupadi again.  
Vidura came to know of this. He approached Duryodhana and gently said - I am happy that you are going on a pilgrimage and I will not stop you from that. It is a very beautiful place. Do go there. Do not pick up a fight with anyone there.  
Duryodhana thanked Vidura and said he was taking his army along so he was not worried if a fight did take place.  
Taking leave of Vidura, Duryodhana resumed his journey and reached the Kamya forest. Sanjaya wento to meet the Pandavas. He bowed down at the feet of all. Yudhistira asked him how things were and what the reason for the visit.  
Sanjaya said Duryodhana wanted to visit Prabhas. Yudhistira asked Sanjaya to tell Duryodhana to conduct himself in a nice manner and go in peace.

## Krishna comes to meet the Pandavas

Saturday, 10 September 2016

United with Arjuna, Yudhistira asked Lomesh what was it that they should do now? What place shpuld we go to now?  
The sage let us first leave the mountains and return to the Kamya forest where we were originally.  
Yudhistira thought of Ghatotkacha who instantly appeared there. Yudhistira asked him to carry them all back to the Kamya forest.  
Happy to be back, the Pandavas constructed huts and settled there. Bhima and Arjuna would go hunting and whatever food was cooked, was kept in the vessel given by Surya. The food would remain there, multiplying, till Draupadi had her meal.  
One day the sage Lomesh came to the Pandavas and said it was time for him to return back to the land of Indra. Sad, the Pandavas bowed down at the feet of the sage and the sage left.

The forest became like Hastinapura with Yudhistira holding court and resolving problems of others.

Four years passed since the Pandavas were all united, Arjuna having returned. The Pandavas lived in the forest for one year. They used to hunt for food. One day, Bhima went out hunting. He saw a cave and wondered if he could find anything there. He came across a huge hungry snake. The snake caught Bhima and Yudhistira had to intervene to rescue Bhima.

Janmajeya asked the sage - Please tell me about this. I am interested to know how Yudhistira had to intervene to save the strong Bhima.

The sage said - Bhima was roaming around with bow in hand and a sword found a beautiful spot full of birds and animals. There many shady trees there. He saw streams in which swans were swimming. Bhima roamed in the forest, killing wild boars and wild buffaloes. Excited, he began jumping and pulling trees out by the roots. The earth started trembling. Then Bhima saw a huge serpent, which was so big that it had coiled itself around the mountain. Seeing Bhima close by, the snake grabbed him. This was a celestial snake and Bhima could not free himself.

Bhima thought this was no ordinary snake. He asked the snake who was he. He said - I am Bhima, next brother of Yudhistira. I have great strength and have killed many rakshasas but why am I not able to defeat you?

The snake said - It is my luck that I have found someone like you to feed on. Let me tell you how I became a snake. You may have have heard about Nahusha. I am Nahusha, an ancestor of yours. I became a snake due to the curse of Agastya. I was ruling Indra's throne when I was cursed. When I slowly began transforming into a serpant, I asked Agastya how would I free myself. Feeling pity for me, Agastya said, I would be free after a long time. I then became a snake on earth but I remember my previous life. The sage said The person who knows about the soul and its relation with the Supreme Being can free you by answering your questions. Other than that, anyone caught by you will lose their strength. Today you have come as my food.

Bhima tried to free himself but was unable to.

Yudhistira saw ill omens. He heard jackals howling. Sensing something wrong, he went in search of Bhima, leaving the others to protect the sages.

Going by, he saw dead animals and knew Bhima had passed that way. He saw the pulled trees and came across the snake, holding Bhima, in its coils.

Yudhistira asked Bhima how this had happened. Bhima, said this was Nahusha, in the guise of a snake.  
The snake said I live on wil goats. Your brother came towards me. If you stay here longer, I will eat you too.

Yudhistira said he was willing to offer food in lieu of Bhima's life. The snake said I was your ancestor, known as the king Nahusha. I was known amongs Gods and Humans. It was due to my acts that Agastya cursed me. I had made the sages my palanquin bearers and insulted them. My memory is still sharp. If you answer my questions, I will think about your offer.

Yudhistira asked the sage to ask the questions, and he would answer if he could.

The snake said - Who is a Brahmana?  
Yudhistira said - One who is wise and known for his wisom. He practices truth, charity, good conduct, forgiveness and follows all rites that he needs to.

The snake said - A person born of a lower caste could also have these qualities. Would he be a brahman also?

Yudhistira said - A lower caste is not by birth alone. Also, one born a brahman does not become one. A low caste person is one who does not have the qualities. A brahman could be called a low caste because of his qualities.

The snake said - If that is so, what is the use of the caste system? That becomes senseless then.

Yudhistira said - Men from all castes have children from women from various castes. The wise ones have given emphasis on qualities. The person is a low caste by birth till he learns the Vedas. As per Manu, the mixed castes are better than many, if they perform good deeds. Whoever conducts himself in a pure and virtuous manner is a brahman as per me.

The snake said - Having heard you, how can I now eat your brother.

Yudhistira said - O learned snake, how does one attain salvation?

The snake said - It is by one's actions.

Yudhistira said - What is more important - truth or giving away of gifts?

The snake said - The actions need to be measured based on the need.

Yudhistira said - What is the characteristic of the mind and the inetllect?

The snake said - Through illusions, the soul becomes a slave of the intellect. Perception rules the inetllect. Sensation is caused by the mind. What is your opinion?

Yudhistira said Having lived in heaven and having all knowledge, why do you ask me this? How does illusion overpower one?

The snake said - Prosperity makes the wise fall. Luxury blinds one. I have been a subject of this. My feet came in touch with Agastya and the sage cursed me to be a sage. By talking to you, my curse is over. I was then told by the sage that talking to Yudhistira would relive me. I release Bhima. Let good luck be with you. I need to return to heaven.

Saying this, Nahusha quite the form of a snake and took a celestial form and proceeded towards heaven.

One Krishna and Balarama decided to visit the Pandavas. The Pandavas were very have to have Krishna and Balarama among them.  
Krishna was very pleased to see Yudhistira having made the forest his kingdom and ruling from there. He said the sorrows that were there would soon be gone. The sages there, hearing Krishna, said that was true. Soon Yudhitira would be back in Hastinapura.  
Soon Krishna and Balarama decided to take leave. With a heavy heart the Pandavas let them go.

## Arjuna united with Yudhistira

Friday, 9 September 2016

With the daityas dead, Matali turned the chariot back towards the house of Indra. Indra came out to greet Arjuna. Indra and the other Gods blessed Arjuna for his deeds. Indra gave Arjuna many weapons. He gave him a gem that would shine on Arjuna's head called Keereta. Arjuna also got a pair of earrings. Arjuna got the name Kiriti because of the gem. As Arjuna had white horses drawing his chariot, Arjuna also got the name Shwetavahana. Arjuna was also named Vibhatsu as he was able to combat his enemies alone. As he was victorious in battle, he was also called Vijaya. Having the power to use weapons with both hands simultaneously, Arjuna also got the name of Sabyasachi. Having defeated Kubera, the god of wealth, Arjuna was also named Dhananjaya.

In the morning Indra asked Matali to deck the chariot and bring it. Calling Arjuna, Indra said it was time for Arjuna to leave and be united with his brothers. Indra gave many gifts for the Pandavas. Arjuna bowed down at the feet of Indra. Arjuna said Please be on my side when I have to fight Duryodhana.  
Indra said he would do that. Yudhistira was an incarnation of Yama (Dharma) and one who caused trouble to such a person would be punished.  
Arjuna taking leave of all in the heaven, climbed on the chariot.

Matali brought the chariot near the Himalayas. There Arjuna saw Yudhistira. The brothers were united.

The next day morning, Yudhistira got up early and then asked Arjuna to show the divine weapons that he had got from Indra. Arjuna sat on the ground and gladly displayed the weapons for all to see. The power of the weapons was such that the earth started trembling, The creatures, living inside the earth were terrified. They came to Arjuna and asked him to put back the weapons. Narada then came there and asked Arjuna to put the weapons back.

## Arjuna kills the daityas

Thursday, 8 September 2016

Arjuna asked Matali In whose land have you brought me? This does not look to be the lands inhabited by Gods. Tell me the truth.  
Matali said This is the land of the daitya called Nivatkavach. By the powers of his meditation, he had captured a part of the heaven and made it his own. Indra spends sleepless nights thinking about him. Only you can put an end to him.

Arjuna said We should delay the slaying of this enemy of Indra. Drive the chariot towards the daitya.

Matali said The daitya has a huge army and you are alone. How will you fight the army? Let us tell Indra and get an army to accompany you.

Arjuna said I can easily defeat all Gods and Asuras alone. I will get rid of the enemy of the Gods alone.  
Arjuna then started blowing on his conch and starting make a noise with the bow string of Gandiva. Daityas Kalkeya and Nivatkavach hearing this, rushed out to see who was making the noise. They started showering arrows at Arjuna and soon the sky was covered and the day looked like night. Arjuna invoking the fire weapon burned all the arrows. Arjuna invoked clouds which the daityas cut off with the wind weapon. The daityas then created a mountain which Arjuna cut off with a creascent weapon.  
The daityas then hurled ten arrows at Arjuna which hit him on his chest. Arjuna fell on his chariot. Getting up and in pain, Arjuna started showering arrows at the army. The army seeing the weapons started fleeing. Seeing this, Nivatkavach cut off the weapons of Arjuna. Arjuna showered arrows at the daitya and he fainted on his chariot. Seeing this, his cahrioteer started fleeing. Regaining consciousness, he turned back. In the meanwhile, Kalkey and other daityas attacked Arjuna. Arjuna started showering arrows at them. Matali then told Arjuna to use the Pashupat weapon given to him by Shiva.  
Arjuna invoked the weapon and released it.  
The weapon, with fire blazing out of it, killed all the daityas.

A voice from the heaven said this weapon would continue its destruction, if not controlled.  
Arjuna, then reciting the mantras recalled the weapon.

## Arjuna goes around the heaven

Wednesday, 7 September 2016

Back in the heaven, Arjuna was being treated very well by Indra. He learnt all he could. He learnt how to use the powers of various weapons and the power of illusion. he learnt the arts of music and dance. When asked the celestial sages why Arjuna was there, Indra said there was a daitya called Nivatkavach. Only Arjuna had the powers to kill him. Indra had not told Arjuna about this and he had no plans to do so.  
He sent for the charioteer Matali and asked him to take Arjuna around the heaven. While Matali knew that Indra wanted Arjuna to confront the daitya, he kept quiet.  
The next day Indra sent for Arjuna and asked him to go with Matali and meet the Gods.

Arjuna taking the name of Govinda climbed on the chariot. He met the Gods and the celestials. Then he went and saw the wonderful gardens.

Arjuna then went and saw the abode of Yama where he saw the just being rewarded and the wicked being punished.

From there Matali took the chariot towards the land where the daityas lived.

## Jatasura killed

Tuesday, 6 September 2016

Yudhistira said - It seems death has called you, and this is why you have come here. One who acts violently towards those who practice non violence always get punished. You are spoiling your own virtues by committing heinous acts.  
Draupadi started calling out to Krishna for help. Then she started out to Bhima to come and help.  
Bhima was far when he heard the cries. He recognized the voice of Draupadi and rushed to help.

Seeing the rakshasa carrying them, Bhima said Do not worry, I will kill this rakshasa soon.  
Bhima uprooted a tree. Seeing that the rakshasa increased his speed. Bhima flung the tree which hit the asura on his head. In pain, the rakshasa released Yudhistira and the others and caught Bhima. With one hand he caught a tree, uprooted that and hit Bhima on his head. On touching Bhima's head, the tree broke. Bhima with his fist, punched the rakshasa hard, on his chest. In pain, the rakshasa released Bhima. The two began wrestling. Taking an opportunity, Bhima sat on Jatasura's chest and caught his neck. Using his feet, Bhima crushed the head of the rakshasa.

With the rakshasa dead, Bhima returned back to the mountain with all.

## Jatasura captures Yudhistira

Monday, 5 September 2016

One day, Bhima had gone inside the forest to hunt. The priest Dhaumya had gone for a bath and the sage Lomesh had gone inside the forest to get flowers. Yudhistira, Nakula , Sahadeva and Draupadi were alone. At that time, Jatasura came there. Jatasura was a friend of Bakasura and thus considered the Pandavas his enemies. Afraid of Bhima, he did not venture near the Pandavas. Seeing an opportunity, the rakshasa approached Yudhistira and said Hidimb and Baka were my dear friends. Your brother Bhima killed them. I have spending sleepless nights, thinking how to tackle you.Today I see the four of you, with Bhima and Arjuna not there to protect you. Bhima and Arjuna will die when they hear you are dead.

Saying this, the rakshasa captured the four and rose skywards.

## Yudhistira goes in search of Bhima

Sunday, 4 September 2016

Yudhistira was disturbed. Bhima was not returning back. With both Arjuna and Bhima not there, he was afraid. He thought of Ghatotkacha, son of Bhima. Ghatotkacha appeared there and bowed before all.  
Yudhistira said his father had gone in search of flowers and was not returning. He asked Ghatotkacha to carry them where Bhima was. Gahtotkacha asked them all climb up on his back.  
They soon reached the spot where Bhima was. Bhima was pleased to see them all. They all decided to stay in that beautiful spot for sometime.

## Bhima finds the flowers

Saturday, 3 September 2016

Bhima continued his journey northwards. Travelling for days he soon came across the mountain which he was searching for. It was a beautiful mountain with birds and animals roaming peacefully. Beautiful flowers were blooming everywhere. Following the scent of flowers, he soon across a lake. In the lake were golden lotuses over which countless bees were buzzing. There were ducks and swans swimming there in pairs. Bhima also saw the Yakshas who were guarding the lake.

Bhima had a bath in the lake. After worshiping his Gods, he began plucking the lotuses. The yakshas were wondering who this mortal was, who had no fear. They asked Bhima to leave. When Bhima did not, they asked him who was he, who had come to the lake of Kubera.Bhima said he was son of Pandu who had come to gather the flowers for his worship.

The yakshas then attacked Bhima, who started throwing trees at them. The yakshas ran to Kubera and said Bhima was plucking the flowers. Kubera said let him take as many flowers as he wants and let him go in peace.

## Bhima meets Hanumana

Friday, 2 September 2016

Janmajeya asked the sage - Please tell me what happened to the four Pandava brothers in the forest. When did they get united again with Arjuna?

The sage Vaishampayana said, the Pandavas listened to the sage Lomesh for six days.

One day a gentle breeze blew from the north. The breeze was very sweet smelling.

Yudhistira asked the sage Lomesh What was this wonderful smell that was coming? What flower was it that smelt so nice.

The sage said, in the north was a mountain called Gandhamadana. It had a lake there and in the lake bloomed many lotus flowers. These flowers were very dear to Kubera and he had people guarding the lake.

Hearing this Draupadi called Bhima. She said If you have some feeling for me, please get one hundred and eight of these flowers. I want to use them to worship the Gods. There is nothing that you cannot do.

Taking leave of Yudhistira, Bhima went in search of the flowers.

Going northwards, he entered a beautiful forest where he saw a big grove of banana trees. He saw the trees laden with ripe fruits. He plucked a few and started gulping them. In his hunger, he destroyed many trees and also ended up killing animals.

Hanumana resided in that forest. He sensed that his brother, Bhima, son of the wind God was there. He took the form of a sick old monkey and lay down on the path of Bhima.

Bhima, coming that way, saw the frail monkey lying there. He asked the monkey to move. He said he was in a hurry and had an important task to finish.

With great difficulty the sick monkey said, Who are you and where are you going? I do not have the strength to move my body. Please hop over me and continue your journey.

Bhima thought All beings had the Lord in them. It would not be fair to hop over them.

To the monkey, Bhima said It would be a sin for him to jump or hop over a living being.  
The monkey said he was just an animal. How would he know what was fair.

Bhima then tried to move the monkey with his left hand, but he was not able to. Surprised, he tried with both his hands, but he was unable to move the monkey an inch. He kept trying with all his strength. He was sweating profusely, but the frail monkey had not moved a bit.

With folded hands, Bhima said - Who are you? A God or a demi God? Are you a Yaksha, a Gandharva or a Kinnara? I see you have come to me as I have been rash in my deeds. Please forgive me. Let me tell you who I am. In the Lunar dynasty, there was a king called Pandu. I am Bhima, his son. My elder brother is Yudhistira. We are five brothers, living in the guise of hermits, in the forests. I am on my way to get golden lotuses for the worship of Gods.

The monkey then said - I was born in the house of Kesari. I the son of the Wind God and my name is Hanumana. I am one who came to this earth to help Rama.

Hearing this, Bhima bowed down to Hanumana. He said You are my elder brother. Please forgive me. Please show me your real self.

Hanumana then smiled and took his real self. Bhima asked Hanumana to help when the Pandavas would go to war with the Kauravas. Saying this, Bhima took leave of Hanumana.

## The king cuts off his flesh

Thursday, 1 September 2016

The king arranged for weighing scales. He put the dove on one side of the scales and he then cut off flesh from his thigh and put that on the other side of the scales. He kept adding his own flesh, but he was not able to equal the weight of the dove, who was none other than Agni.  
Seeing that it was not working, the king took the name of Hari and got on the scales himself.

The eagle then said I am Indra, the king of Gods, The dove is Agni. We have come here to see how just and noble you are. We are truly pleased with you. Your body will become whole again. You will be able to attain heaven. Come with us, leave this world proceed towards heaven with us.

From the heavens, Gods started showering flowers. A chariot came, and the king with Indra and Agni went towards heaven.

## August

## Dove and the Eagle

Wednesday, 31 August 2016

Lomesh then said to Yudhistira - Listen to the story of the eagle and the dove. This land was once the kingdom of Shibi, in whose land all were in perfect harmony. There was a king there called Ushnir. Indra wanted to test him. After talking to Agni, Indra took the form of an eagle and Agni took the form of a dove.  
The king was performing a sacrifice when a dove came to him, asking for shelter. The dove was being chased by an eagle. The king gave shelter to the dove. The eagle then came and said this was not justice. The dove was his meal.  
The king said the dove had asked for protection, fearing its life, and as a king it was his duty to protect the bird.  
The eagle said it was dying of hunger and needed food immediately else it would die. The king said he would arrange for food for the bird. The eagle said it only hunted and ate doves. If the king was so interested in protecting the dove, then the king should cut off his own flesh and offer that to the eagle.  
If that amount of flesh was equal in weight to that of the dove, then he would go happy.

## Parashurama humbled

Tuesday, 30 August 2016

Continuing his tale, the sage Lomesh said - The place where you are now, is a great pilgrim spot. Anyone who comes here, attains Vaikuntha. This was the place where Parashurama was subdued.

Yudhistira wanted to know what had happened to Parashurama. Lomesh said - Years ago, there was a king called Dashratha who had four sons, who were incarnations of Vishnu. Lakshmi was born as the daughter of king Janaka.  
Janaka had made a promise - any one who could break the bow of Shiva would marry his daughter.  
The news went around and all came to try their luck.

In the ashrama of the sage Vishwamitra, Rama heard this. He was then living with Vishwamitra, helping the sage fight rakshasas.  
Rama went to the palace of Janaka and broke the bow. Winning the hand of Sita, he was on his way back, when he came across Parashurama, who was holding a bow on his left shoulder and an axe (Parashu) in his right hand. He had a quiver on his back full of arrows and he had matted hair oh his head.

His eyes glowing, he said to Rama - You go, breaking a bow and taking Sita, in front of me. All kshatriya are my enemy. I have made the earth free ofkshatriyas three hundred times. If you think you are brave, string my bow and show me.

Rama easily strung the bow. Parashurama then gave him a celestial arrow. Rama asked the sage, in which direction should he release the arrow.

The sage said Shoot the arrow and block my passage to heaven.

Rama shot the arrow and Parashurama's pride vanished.

## Bhagiratha gets Ganga

Monday, 29 August 2016

Bhagiratha went to the Himalayas and started severe austerities. The sages around were amazed to see his dedication. He started his meditation first living on fruits, then on leaves, then on air and finally gave up that too. He was reduced to only skin and bones.

Pleased Ganga came to him to give him a boon, With folded hands Bhagiratha said that his ancestors had been burnt by the sage Kapila and they would attain salvation only when they were touched by the waters of Ganga.

Ganga said she would come but who would be able to bear the force of her waters when she started her journey towards earth.

Bhagiratha then went and started praying to Shiva. Pleased, Shiva appeared before him. Listening to Bhagiratha, Shiva agreed to help him. He went to the Himalayas and asked the mountain range to release Ganga.

Ganga jumped on Shiva's head and from there she began flowing in three different directions. The river flowing towards heaven became known as Mandakini. The river flowing towards earth became Alaknanda and the one flowing to the nether world became known as Bhogvati.

Ganga then said to Bhagiratha - I have come to earth on your invite. Tell me in which direction should I flow. Where are your ancestors?

Bhagiratha started leading the way and Ganga started flowing behind him. She soon got lost in the Himalayan mountains. Ganga then thought of Airavata, the elephant. When the elephant came, Bhagiratha asked the elephant to create a way for Ganga. The elephant said he would be willing to do so if Ganga pleased him.

Bhagiratha, ashamed, told Ganga what Airavata had said. Ganga said to Bhagiratha - Go tell the elephant, if he can withstand my waves, I will please him.

Airavata then created a path with his tusks. Ganga gushed out and soon Airavata was fighting for his life. He appealed to Ganga to save him. Ganga slowed herself, and the elephant fled from there.

They soon reached the ashrama of the sage Jahnu. The sage seeing the river, drank it up. Not seeing the flowing river behind him, Bhagiratha appealed to the sage. The sage, released the river from his knee, by tearing open his flesh. From then on, Ganga became known as Jhanavi.

People were pleased to see Ganga flowing on earth. Flowing behind Bhagiratha, Ganga reached the ashrama of the sage Kapila.

Touched by the waters of Ganga, the sons of Sagara attained salvation and went towards Vaikuntha.

Flowing onwards, Ganga went into the ocean and the ocean was full again.

## Story of Sagara

Sunday, 28 August 2016

Hearing this, Yudhistira asked Who is this Bhagiratha? What did he do?

The sage Lomesh said - There was a king called Sagara. He was a great just king, an enemy of all evil. With his wives Shiavya and Vaidharvi, he went to pray to Shiva to have a son. Pleased Shiva asked the king to ask for a boon.

The king asked for sixty thousand sons.

Shiva said So be it. You will have sixty thousand sons who will die together. One son will be left who will take care of your dynasty. That son would be born to Shaivya.  
Saying this, Shiva vanished.

After sometime Shaivya gave birth to a beautiful boy. Vaidharvi gave birth to a lump of flesh. The king was wondering what to do with this and was about to give orders to discard the lump, when a voice said Why do you discard the seed of your dynasty. Get sixty thousands pots full of clarified butter and divide the lump equally and place the parts in the pots.  
The king did as told and soon sixty thousand sons were born.

After a few years the king started the Ashwamedha (horse) sacrifice and asked the sixty thousand sons to guard the horse.

Indra fearing the power of the king stole the horse and hid it in the nether world where the sage Kapila was meditating.

The princes went back and reported to the king that the horse was missing. The king asked them why they had returned empty handed. He asked them to go in search of the horse. The princes went forth. Searching the whole earth, they then dug a hole into the nether world

Reaching the nether world they saw the sage Kapila meditating. Next to him to him was the horse. Thinking the sage was the horse thief, they insulted the sage.

The sage was disturbed. He opened his eyes. Fire came out and the sixty thousand sons of Sagara were burnt instantly.

Narada told Sagara about the incident. The king asked Anshuman, son of Asmanjas to go and get the horse. Asmanjas was the other son of the king. The king said I have forsaken your father as he was a misbehaved prince. You have all the qualities of a good person. Go and fetch the horse and let the sacrifice complete.

Yudhistira at this point wanted to know aboutAsmanjas. The sage said :

Asmanjas's behavior was bad since childhood. He used to push children and enjoy them being hurt. The king's subjects complained about the behavior of the prince. The king ordered the prince to be exiled.

Anshuman went where the sage Kapila was meditating. He prayed to the sage and asked for forgiveness. The sage was pleased and asked the prince what he wanted. The prince said he wanted the horse and also wanted to know how his uncles would be liberated.

The sage As per destiny, your grandson will the one who will liberate your uncles. He will please Shiva and get Ganga. For now, take the horse and complete the sacrifice.

Anshuman took the horse. The sacrifice was completed.

After some years, Sagara passed away, making Anshuman the king. Anshuman ruled the kingdom well. He had a son named Dilip. Dilip hearing about his ancestors, tried to please Shiva but was unable to. Dilip has a son called Bhagiratha.

When Bhagiratha heard about how his ancestors had burned, he gave the control the kingdom to his ministers and went to the forests to please Shiva.

## Agastya drinks up the Ocean

Saturday, 27 August 2016

The sage said - Listen Son of Pandu. The asuras took refuge in the ocean. They hid in the water in the day, and in the evening, they would come out and attack the sages.  
The tormented sages, who managed to save themselves and the Gods went to Vishnu asking for help. Vishnu asked them to approach Brahma. Brahma took the Gods where Agastya was. They asked the sage for help.  
Agastya went to the ocean and said that for the welfare of all, he would drink up the ocean. He then sucked in and swallowed all the water. The Gods were ready with their weapons. They attacked the asuras hiding in the water. They killed the asuras.

With the ocean now dry, the Gods were worried. They asked Brahma for a solution. Brahma said the ocean would remain dry till Bhagiratha was born on this earth, He would help fill in the ocean.

## Indra kills Vritta

Sunday, 21 August 2016

Yudhistira asked the sage What was done with the body of the sage?  
The sage said - the bones were taken to the celestial architect who decided to form a weapon from the bones of the sage.  
A Vajra was formed and given to Indra. Indra attacked the asura. A fight started between the Gods and the asuras. Climbing on his elephant, Indra attacked the asura with his Vajra. Seeing Indra, the asura increased his size, and started swallowing all the Gods. Indra asked Vishnu for help. Vishnu gave his blessing.  
In that fight, Indra killed Vritta using the Vajra. The army of Vritta seeing him dead, fled and hid in the ocean.

## Dadhichi donates his body

Saturday, 20 August 2016

Yudhistira said - I have heard Agastya had drunk up the ocean. How did that happen?

Lomesh continued - In the earlier days, there was a asura called Vritta. He managed to create an army of ferocious Danavas and asuras and with he attacked all. Indra, leading the Gods went to Brahma and asked how would they be relieved from this menace.

Brahma said I had been thinking of this for a long time. This asura will not be destroyed by any ordinary weapon. Approach the sage Dadhichi. If he is pleased with you, he will donate his body to you. Use his bones then to make a weapon called the Vajra, and using that, Indra is to kill the asura.

The Gods went to Dadhichi, who asked them the reason for their visit. When the Gods expressed their wish, Dadhichi gladly left his body.

## Vindhya humbled

Monday, 15 August 2016

Lomesh said to Yudhistira, Hear me, when I tell you how the sage dispelled the great darkness.  
Off the great mountains, the Sun went around the mountain Sumeru. This hurt the ego of the Vindhya mountain. He asked the sun to go around him also. The Sun said he went where he had been told to go, the creator.  
This angered Vindhya. He said I will see how you go around someone else. Saying this, the mountain began increasing its size till the Sun's rays were not visible on the other side.

The Gods appealed to the mountain but he would not listen. Having no other option, they went to Agastya and asked him to help.

Agastya went to Vindhya. Seeing Agastya, the mountain bowed down at him. Agastya smiled and said, I am going southwards. Till the time I return, remain in this position.

Saying this, the sage went southwards and never returned back, and not able to refuse Agastya, the mountain remained in that position, and the universe was saved.

## Ilvala and Vatapi

Sunday, 14 August 2016

Pandavas reached the forest of Naimasha and from there, having bathed in the river Gomati they went towards Prayag where Yamuna meets Ganga. There, they reached the ashrama of the sage Agastya. Lomesh said to the Pandavas, this is where Agastya killed demons and setup his abode. Listen while I tell you about Agastya.

One day, the sage saw his deceased ancestors hanging in a pit, upside down. Agastya asked them why they were in the pit, and that too upside down.

The ancestors said they were in this position as he, Agastya, was a celibate and did not have any children.

Agastya decided to get married, but to whom? After much thinking, he decided that the princess of Vidharba, Lopamudra, was the person who would be his wife.

In Vidharba, the king was wondering, who would be the fit husband for his daughter. Agastya then came there. The king asked him for the reason of his visit. The sage said he wanted Lopamudra as his wife. The king did not know what to say. He went to his queen and asked for her advice. Lopamudra, hearing this, went to her parents and said Do not grieve for me. Give my hand to Agastya.

The king agreed and with all rituals, he gave away Lopamudra to Agastya. Agastya asked Lopamudra to discard her royal clothes and jewels and take the garb of an sage's wife. Lopamudra immediately did so.
